Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>


### [48] [EDiS: Edge Disjoint Subgraph Sparsification Framework for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09059)
*Sai Karthik Navuluru,Siddhartha Shankar Das,Franck Dernoncourt,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Baris Coskunuzer,Alex Pothen,Lakshman Tamil,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDiS 将图一次性分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再按边预算跨 epoch 重组用于稀疏 GNN 训练，在 19 个节点分类基准上以相同边预算取得最高平均得分与最低平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 GNN 训练可降低计算量，但决定保留哪些边代价高昂：复用单一稀疏图虽便宜却将训练锁定在固定拓扑上，而逐 epoch 变化拓扑又需反复采样或重算。因此需要一种既能支持拓扑变化、又避免重复结构提取开销的稀疏化方案。

Method: 提出 EDiS（Edge-Disjoint Subgraph sparsification）框架，将一次性的结构提取与逐 epoch 的图组合分离：先把图一次性分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再在训练各 epoch 中按边预算约束与保留比例重组这些子图，无需重新提取结构。默认构建使用基于特征的打分与连续最大得分覆盖森林，同一组合机制也兼容其他边选择规则。论文对逐 epoch 采样器（从缓存分解中抽取训练图的组合步骤）进行了组合分析。

Result: 在默认覆盖森林选择器下，存储的分解可确定性地保留高分割边，并给出与选择器无关的高分割边在组合训练图中存活的条件界。在 19 个同配、异配及大规模节点分类基准上，与 17 个基线在相同边预算下比较，EDiS 取得最高平均基准得分（准确率/ROC-AUC）以及最低的平均排名和与最优方法的差距。消融实验显示，在紧张边预算下结构分解与 epoch 变化的收益最明显。

Conclusion: EDiS 通过一次分解、多次重组的方式，在保持稀疏训练低计算成本的同时引入 epoch 级拓扑变化，避免重复采样与结构重算，并在广泛基准与消融中验证了其有效性，尤其适用于边预算受限的场景。

Abstract: Sparse GNN training reduces computation, but deciding which edges to keep can be costly. Reusing one sparse graph is cheap, but locks training to a fixed topology, while varying it across epochs can require repeated sampling or recomputation. We introduce EDiS (Edge-Disjoint Subgraph sparsification framework), which separates one-time structural extraction from per-epoch graph composition. EDiS decomposes the graph once into cacheable edge-disjoint subgraphs, then recombines them into graphs with edge-budget constraints across epochs and retention ratios without re-extracting structure. Our default construction uses feature-based scores and successive maximum score covering forests, while the same composition mechanism also supports alternative edge selection rules. We provide a combinatorial analysis of the per-epoch sampler, the composition step that draws a training graph from the cached decomposition. We show that, under the default covering-forest selector, the stored decomposition deterministically preserves high-score cut edges, and we derive a selector-agnostic conditional bound on high-score cut survival in composed training graphs. Across 19 homophilic, heterophilic, and large-scale node classification benchmarks against 17 baselines under the same edge budget, EDiS achieves the highest mean benchmark score (accuracy/ROC-AUC) and the lowest average rank and gap-to-best among ranked methods. Ablations show the clearest benefits of structural decomposition and epoch variation at tight edge budgets.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Label Topic Assignment via LLM Distillation: A Comparative Analysis of Generative vs. Discriminative Student Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09063)
*Sourabh Kasliwal,Shubhranshu Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了电商UGC多标签主题分类中,不同规模(1B/4B/8B)与架构(生成式 vs 判别式)小语言模型的表现,发现在结构化评论上判别式模型更优,而在复杂多轮对话上最小的1B生成式模型已超越判别式基线,且生成式模型在标签集扩展和长尾分布下更稳健,并已成功落地生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商UGC(产品评论、买家-卖家对话)的多标签主题分类面临非正式语言、标签极度稀疏、分类体系快速演进等可扩展性挑战。用LLM作为标注预言机蒸馏数据已成行业标准,但如何为学生模型选择最优、低延迟的架构仍无定论,因此需要系统评估。

Method: 在SLM参数规模(1B、4B、8B)和架构范式(因果生成式 vs 双向判别式)上做全面评测;将生成式文本到标签分类器与判别式基线(DeBERTa-V3、ModernBERT)进行对比,并测试大规模标签集扩展(最多112个主题)和严重长尾分布下的表现。

Result: 发现数据依赖的关键权衡:在结构化产品评论上判别式模型优于超轻量生成式模型;但在复杂多轮对话数据上,即使最小的1B生成式模型也超过判别式基线。生成式模型在标签集大规模扩展和长尾分布下保持稳健,而判别式基线在规模化时Macro-F1下降35%。

Conclusion: 报告了这些优化模型在产品评论与对话两大类场景中的成功生产部署,实现了严格的延迟合规,并在全球市场规模下产生了切实的业务影响。

Abstract: Multi-label topic assignment for user-generated content (UGC) -- including product reviews and buyer-seller conversations -- poses unique scalability challenges in large-scale e-commerce due to informal language, extreme label sparsity, and rapidly evolving taxonomies. While utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) as labeling oracles to distill ground-truth data has emerged as an industry standard to bypass prohibitive manual annotation costs, determining the optimal, low-latency architecture for the resulting student models remains an open challenge. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation across Small Language Model (SLM) parameter scales (1B, 4B, and 8B) and architectural paradigms (causal generative versus bidirectional discriminative). Comparing generative text-to-label classifiers against discriminative baselines (DeBERTa-V3 and ModernBERT), our analysis reveals a crucial data-dependent trade-off: while discriminative models outperform ultra-lightweight generative models on structured product reviews, even the smallest 1B generative model surpasses discriminative baselines on complex, multi-turn conversational data. Furthermore, generative models maintain robust performance under massive label-set expansion (up to 112 topics) and severe long-tail distributions, whereas discriminative baselines suffer a 35% drop in Macro-F1 at scale. Finally, we detail the successful production deployment of these optimized models across both product review and conversational domains, demonstrating strict latency compliance and tangible business impact at a global marketplace scale.

</details>


### [50] [TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074)
*Yuanzhe Li,Pengxin Wang,Yuxin Ren,Jianing Deng,Jingtong Hu,Song Wang,Jingdi Chen,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP)，首个面向RL训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架，通过将剪枝与高效on-policy恢复结合，在移除60% FFN通道的情况下保持高任务成功率并降低GPU时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体任务需要反复调用模型，推理成本高昂。虽然窄域智能体任务显示激进剪枝可能不损失性能，但针对问答任务的现有剪枝方法应用到智能体模型时性能严重下降。作者将其归因于两个决策问题：剪什么（一次性重要性估计无法追踪剪枝模型的自适应）与如何恢复（离线蒸馏只覆盖教师前缀，全轨迹on-policy蒸馏导致学生错误跨轮累积）。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP)，首个面向RL训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架。TAP将结构化剪枝与高效on-policy恢复耦合：将交互锚定到教师轨迹，同时允许学生生成每个推理-动作响应；由冻结的稠密教师监督学生的响应前缀，解决响应内训练-推理不匹配并防止学生引起的偏差跨训练轮传播。此外，TAP不使用一次性剪枝，而是利用恢复目标在恢复后学生上的梯度重新为通道打分，将迭代通道选择与演化中的策略相连接。

Result: 移除60%的FFN通道后，TAP在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别保留了稠密7B智能体99.2%和88.0%的任务成功率，同时将每个成功任务的GPU时间分别减少约22%和17%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在有限恢复预算下，TAP能够有效实现长时程智能体的结构化压缩。

Abstract: Emerging long-horizon agentic tasks require repeated model calls, worsening the inference cost of already-costly language models. While narrow agentic tasks suggest potential for aggressive model pruning without performance drop, empirical results show existing methods proposed for question answering tasks severely degrade task performance when applied to agentic models. We trace this failure to two decisions: what to prune and how to recover. For pruning, one-shot importance estimates fail to track how the pruned model adapts. For recovery, offline distillation covers only teacher prefixes, while full-trajectory on-policy distillation causes student errors to compound across turns. In this work, we propose Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP), the first structural pruning framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-trained agents. TAP couples structural pruning with efficient on-policy recovery, anchoring interactions to teacher trajectories while allowing the student to generate each reasoning-action response. A frozen dense teacher supervises the student's response prefixes, addressing within-response training-inference mismatch while preventing student-induced deviations from propagating across training turns. Instead of one-shot pruning, TAP re-scores channels using gradients of the recovery objective on the recovered student, connecting iterative channel selection to the evolving policy. With 60% of FFN channels removed, TAP retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B agents' task success rates on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while reducing GPU time per successful task by approximately 22% and 17%. These results demonstrate effective structural compression of long-horizon agents under a limited recovery budget.

</details>


### [51] [Tucker Bottleneck Attention for Multi-Dimensional Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09090)
*Ryan Solgi,Parsa Madinei,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TuBA利用低秩Tucker结构实现高效全局token混合，在视频预测和天气预测任务上以更低计算量取得更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次计算成本限制了其向多维数据长序列的可扩展性。

Method: TuBA将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核，在核上执行多头自注意力和线性投影，再将更新写回原始空间，实现次二次计算；其自回归扩展在核内采用双向交互、核间采用因果注意力。

Result: 在视频预测与全球天气预测上，TuBA相较标准与高效注意力及任务专用模型取得更优的精度-效率权衡；相比标准自注意力，视频预测误差与计算量最多降低24.7%和66.6%，自回归天气预测降低37.1%和85.1%，加速最高达4.27倍；低秩Tucker核与多帧生成分别优于全秩注意力与逐帧生成。

Conclusion: 低秩Tucker瓶颈注意力为多维长序列提供了一种高效且高精度的全局token混合方案。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention limits scalability to long sequences from multidimensional data. We introduce Tucker bottleneck attention (TuBA), which exploits low-rank tensor structure for efficient global token mixing. TuBA projects hidden tensors into compact Tucker cores, performs multi-head self-attention and linear projections on the cores, and writes updates back to the ambient space, enabling subquadratic computation. Its autoregressive extension combines bidirectional interactions within cores with causal attention across cores. On video prediction and global weather forecasting, TuBA achieves favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over standard and efficient attention and task-specific models. Compared to standard self-attention, TuBA reduces error and computation by up to 24.7% and 66.6% for video prediction and 37.1% and 85.1% for autoregressive weather forecasting, with speedups up to 4.27 times. Low-rank Tucker cores and multi-frame generation also outperform full-rank attention and frame-by-frame generation, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [FedRSPO+: A Heterogeneity-aware Algorithm for Decision-focused Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09091)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedRSPO+，一个面向跨机构（cross-silo）场景的异构感知决策聚焦联邦学习框架，基于平滑决策映射的 RSPO+ 代理，缓解多面体问题的决策不连续与异构目标/可行域带来的不稳定，并在理论与实验中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）方法多假设数据集中，联邦场景下标准联邦方法只优化预测精度而非决策质量，且未处理下游目标与可行域的异构性；多面体问题中微小扰动即可导致最优决策不连续，进而破坏客户端更新与聚合的稳定性。

Method: 提出 FedRSPO+，以 RSPO+ 这一正则化"先预测后优化"代理为基础，通过投影平滑决策映射；给出 RSPO+ 对正则化决策误差与后悔值的上界，并在精确正则化与一致 LP 解选择下推广至原始 LP 决策；推导依赖目标与可行域异构性的跨客户端异构界（同质时消失，无需强凸性）；采用退火、模块化训练流程，兼容标准联邦个性化与聚合方法。

Result: 在合成背包、最短路及真实世界能源定价任务上，与仅预测的联邦学习和 DFL 基线在不同异构程度与通信预算下进行对比，结果表明平滑有助于稳定协同决策学习。

Conclusion: 平滑是稳定协同决策学习的有用要素，FedRSPO+ 为联邦 DFL 提供了异构感知的基础框架。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictive models for downstream optimization, but existing methods largely assume centralized data. In cross-silo settings, federated learning offers a natural alternative, yet standard federated methods optimize prediction over decision quality and do not address heterogeneity in downstream objectives or feasible sets. This heterogeneity is especially challenging for DFL because small perturbations in polyhedral problems can cause discontinuous changes in optimal decisions, destabilizing client updates and aggregation. We propose FedRSPO+, a heterogeneity-aware framework for decision-focused federated learning, built on RSPO+, a regularized predict-then-optimize surrogate that smooths the decision map through projection. We show that RSPO+ upper bounds decision error and regret for the regularized decision and, under exact regularization and consistent LP solution selection, for the original LP decision. We further derive cross-client heterogeneity bounds that depend on both objective and feasible-set heterogeneity, vanish at homogeneity, and require no strong convexity. FedRSPO+ uses an annealed, modular training procedure compatible with standard federated personalization and aggregation methods. Experiments on synthetic knapsack, shortest-path, and real-world energy pricing tasks compare against prediction-only federated learning and DFL baselines under varying heterogeneity and communication budgets. Results suggest that smoothing is a useful ingredient for stable collaborative decision learning and provide a heterogeneity-aware foundation for federated DFL.

</details>


### [53] [MaRK: Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels for Dynamic Operator Conditioning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09092)
*Syed Ibrahim Omer,Ginny Y. Wong. Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MaRK框架，将上下文向量映射为冻结SSM递归参数的受限调制，实现参数高效的迭代扩散式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练SSM用于迭代生成时的条件注入通常发生在递归算子之外（如输入注入或激活调制），虽能引入条件信息，但底层时间动态保持不变，缺乏对模型记忆核的动态调整能力。

Method: MaRK将上下文向量直接映射为冻结SSM的递归、读入、读出、跳连与离散化参数的受限调制，从LPV-SSM视角构造上下文索引的马尔可夫参数序列族。在111M参数Hydra SSM骨架上实例化，研究Hypernet、Chebyshev多项式与DCT三种适配器几何，通过低秩辅助映射实现参数高效微调，并给出仿射二次稳定性证明。

Result: 合成LPV恢复实验与马尔可夫算子诊断表明，MaRK在匹配假设下可恢复坐标不变时间算子，并产生稳定的时间步条件记忆轮廓。Chebyshev变体表现最佳，平均验证损失2.55，DCT为2.59，Hypernet为3.77。

Conclusion: MaRK以算子级条件化方式改造冻结SSM的递归动态，参数高效微调成为适配机制的结构性结果，仅需6.3–11M可训练辅助参数即可从双向目标过渡到迭代扩散机制。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) offer an efficient alternative to Transformers for sequence modeling, yet conditioning pre-trained SSMs for iterative generation typically operates outside the recurrent operator, through input injection or activation modulation. While such mechanisms expose the model to conditioning information, they leave the underlying temporal dynamics fixed. We introduce MaRK (Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels), a dynamic operator-conditioning framework that maps context vectors directly into bounded modulations of a frozen SSM's recurrence ($A$), read-in ($B$), read-out ($C$), skip ($D$), and discretization ($Δ$) parameters. Viewed through the lens of LPV-SSM systems, MaRK induces a context-indexed family of Markov parameter sequences, allowing each diffusion timestep to reshape the model's input-output memory kernel. We instantiate MaRK on a frozen 111M-parameter Hydra SSM backbone and study three adapter geometries: Hypernet, Chebyshev polynomial, and Discrete Cosine Transform kernels. Since these adapters modify the Markov parameter sequence through low-rank auxiliary maps on the frozen backbone, parameter-efficient fine-tuning arises as a structural consequence of the adaptation mechanism itself, requiring only 6.3--11M trainable auxiliary parameters to transition from a bidirectional objective to an iterative diffusion regime. The bounded recurrence parameterization further yields an analytic Affine Quadratic Stability certificate for the modulated recurrence. Through synthetic LPV recovery experiments and Markov-operator diagnostics, we show that MaRK recovers coordinate-invariant temporal operators under matched assumptions and produces distinct, stable timestep-conditioned memory profiles. Empirically, the Chebyshev variant yields the strongest performance, achieving an average validation loss of 2.55, followed by the DCT (2.59) and Hypernet (3.77) geometries.

</details>


### [54] [Are We Really Benchmarking Forecasting Models? The Impact of Preprocessing on Time Series Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09096)
*Guilherme Afonso Galindo Padilha,Paulo Salgado Gomes de Mattos Neto,Rafael Menelau Oliveira e Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"预处理感知"的预测基准，在29,000条M4时间序列上评测11种预测模型与16种可逆预处理管线的组合，发现逐序列优化预处理可带来约27%–87%的性能提升，并可使简单架构在基准上具备与复杂SOTA模型相当的竞争力，同时发布配套元数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献已强调预处理对预测精度的重要性，但当前研究普遍忽视该环节：现代基准多仅采用简单缩放，未处理非平稳性所需的差分等关键变换。这种缺失造成结构性预处理偏差，偏向内置数据处理机制的模型，并掩盖了简单架构的真实潜力。

Method: 构建一个预处理感知的基准，将11种预测模型与16种可逆预处理管线组合，在29,000条M4时间序列上进行系统评测，并整理所有结果形成元数据集，以支持后续元学习任务。

Result: 预处理被识别为预测性能的关键驱动因素。针对每条序列优化预处理后，所有被测模型性能提升约27%至87%；缺乏内置预处理的架构获益最大，从而使更简单的架构在现代预测基准中具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 忽视预处理会在基准评估中引入结构性偏差，低估简单模型。将预处理纳入基准设计并逐序列优化，可显著提升性能并重塑模型间比较结论。相关资源与实验结果已存入综合元数据集，供未来元学习研究使用。

Abstract: While established literature underscores the pivotal role of preprocessing in forecasting accuracy, this stage remains largely overlooked in current research. Modern benchmarks typically resort to simple scaling, failing to account for critical transformations required to address nonstationarity, such as differencing. This omission creates a significant structural preprocessing bias that favors models with built-in data treatments while obscuring the true potential of simpler architectures. We study this effect through a preprocessing-aware benchmark that evaluates 11 forecasting models across 16 reversible preprocessing pipelines on 29,000 M4 time series. Our results identify preprocessing as a key driver of forecasting performance. Optimizing preprocessing per series yields gains of approximately 27\% to 87\% across all evaluated models, with architectures lacking internalized preprocessing experiencing the most substantial improvements. This allows simpler architectures to become highly competitive with complex, state-of-the-art models in modern forecasting benchmarks. All resources and experimental results from this benchmark are stored in a comprehensive metadataset to support future metalearning tasks.

</details>


### [55] [Convex-Concave Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09108)
*Shripad V. Deshmukh,Yaswanth Chittepu,Dhawal Gupta,Philip Thomas,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示策略学习目标在log-density-ratio坐标下是一个DC约束的DC规划，提出用序列凸规划求解的多步CCRL方法，并在多个领域验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略优化的核心问题（最大化期望回报）是非凸的，即便在直接策略参数化下也如此。领域内普遍回避该难题，转而优化凸代理近似（如NPG、TRPO、PPO、AWR）并施加信赖域约束。作者希望直接挖掘原始问题的隐藏结构，从而超越代理近似。

Method: 在log-density-ratio坐标 y := log[π/π_n] 下，每迭代目标（可用逐决策重要性采样PDIS计算）被证明是difference-of-convex约束的DC规划。该结构使CPI、NPG、TRPO、AWR成为可解释坐标轴上的特例，并开辟了耦合相邻决策的多步轴k。作者采用DC问题标准求解器——序列凸规划（SCP）求解每迭代问题，并在温和条件下给出收敛保证。

Result: 多步Convex-Concave RL（CCRL）在需要跨时间horizon传播信用的诊断MDP上表现优异，且优势随依赖长度增长；在经典控制任务上与调优后的PPO相当；在真实的随机中horizon医疗领域，收敛速度显著快于调优PPO，达到相同近最优生存率，训练曲线下面积高11.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明策略学习目标并非无结构，而是DC约束的DC规划，架起了DC优化与强化学习两个文献之间的桥梁；CCRL作为多步方法在若干任务上超越或媲美现有方法，验证了利用该结构的价值。

Abstract: Policy learning drives many of the most consequential and heavily-invested applications of reinforcement learning today. Yet the core optimization problem it rests on (maximizing expected return) is notoriously non-convex, even under a direct policy parameterization, and the field has largely responded by avoiding it: optimizing convex surrogate approximations of the return under trust-region constraints (NPG, TRPO, PPO, AWR). We show that this seemingly unstructured problem is not actually structureless. In log-density-ratio coordinates $y := \log[π/π_n]$, the exact per-iteration objective, computable via per-decision importance sampling (PDIS), is a difference-of-convex-constrained difference-of-convex (DC-constrained DC) program. This structure lets us move beyond surrogate approximations: it recovers CPI, NPG, TRPO, and AWR as special cases along interpretable axes, and it opens a multi-step axis $k$ that couples consecutive decisions. We solve the per-iteration program with sequential convex programming (SCP), the standard solver for difference-of-convex problems, and give convergence guarantees under mild conditions, bridging the difference-of-convex optimization and RL literatures. Empirically, multi-step Convex-Concave RL (CCRL) wins on diagnostic MDPs where credit must propagate across a horizon (its advantage growing with the dependency length), is competitive with a tuned PPO on classic control, and on a realistic, stochastic, mid-horizon healthcare domain converges markedly faster than tuned PPO to the same near-optimal survival, with an 11.3% higher area under the training curve.

</details>


### [56] [Breaking Adversarial Transferability in Fine-Tuned Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09109)
*Mojtaba Nafez,Aref Mousavi,Mohammad Ebrahim Mahdavi,Mobina Poulaei,Kiarash Kiani Feriz,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TransferBreaker框架，抑制微调ASR模型的对抗迁移攻击，将对抗WER从92.6降至27.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织常用公开预训练ASR模型微调后部署在黑盒环境，假设有限访问能提供保护。作者证明该假设脆弱：在公开基模型上构造的对抗扰动可有效迁移到微调目标模型，严重降低性能，对安全关键应用构成隐患。

Method: TransferBreaker统一微调框架，包含三个组件：Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning（将对抗训练限制在基模型有效的扰动上）；Latent Jacobian Regularization（通过抑制对抗敏感方向强制潜在空间不变性）；HybridGrad-AFT（插值基模型与目标模型梯度的可迁移扰动，提升对自适应攻击的鲁棒性）。各组件均有理论论证。

Result: 在三种语言和四个大型ASR模型上评估，对抗WER从92.6降至27.8。代码开源于https://github.com/rohban-lab/TransferBreaker。

Conclusion: 黑盒部署假设不可靠，迁移对抗攻击威胁显著；所提框架能有效抑制对抗迁移，提升微调ASR模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Many organizations fine-tune publicly available pretrained Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models and deploy them in black-box settings, assuming limited access provides protection. We show this assumption is fragile: adversarial perturbations crafted on the public base model transfer effectively to fine-tuned target models, severely degrading performance and posing concerns for safety-critical applications. We propose TransferBreaker, a unified fine-tuning framework that suppresses adversarial transfer by integrating Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning, which restricts adversarial training to base-effective perturbations; Latent Jacobian Regularization, which enforces latent-space invariance by suppressing adversarially sensitive directions; and HybridGrad-AFT, which improves robustness against adaptive attacks by interpolating transferable perturbations from base and target gradients. We theoretically justify all components and evaluate TransferBreaker across three languages and four large ASR models, reducing adversarial WER from 92.6 to 27.8. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/rohban-lab/TransferBreaker.

</details>


### [57] [The Deceptive Bandit Problem: Exploratory Coupling and the Fragility of Multi-Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09120)
*Michael Tang,Mahmoud Abdelgalil,Jorge I. Poveda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了随机探索的独立性与隐私性在安全层面的关键作用：对手可利用与受害者探索相关的泄露信号进行欺骗，将学习动态引导至"欺骗性纳什均衡"（DNE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索是赌博机学习、多智能体强化学习和零阶策略搜索的核心，但其独立性与隐私通常仅被视为技术性假设。本文旨在表明这些性质对安全性至关重要，并展示对抗性智能体如何利用关于他人探索的特权信息。

Method: 分析最小二人强单调设定下的"欺骗者—受害者"对，欺骗方仅获得与受害者探索相关的泄露信号。通过将自身探索动作与该信息耦合，欺骗者注入外部性以改变学习动态；提出欺骗性赌博机学习（DBL）动态，并分析其收敛性与速率。

Result: 证明DBL动态收敛到DNE的任意小邻域，同时保持最优收敛速率；且在放松标准赌博机优化文献中二阶光滑性条件的情况下仍达到该最优速率；刻画了欺骗严格改变稳态的条件及其对欺骗者成本的影响，并在资源分配博弈中说明结果。

Conclusion: 探索的独立性与隐私并非单纯的技术假设，而是安全关键属性；欺骗者可借相关泄露信号改变学习系统的稳态，形成DNE。

Abstract: Randomized exploration is central to bandit learning, multi-agent reinforcement learning, and zeroth-order policy search, yet its independence and privacy are usually only treated as technical assumptions. We show that these properties are critical for security purposes and demonstrate how an adversarial agent can exploit privileged information on another agent's exploration. We analyze a deceiver-victim pair in the minimal two-player strongly monotone setting, where a deceptive player obtains leaked signals that are merely correlated with the victim's exploration. We show that, by coupling their own exploratory action with this information, the deceptive player injects an externality that steers the learning dynamics to a new steady state, called the deceptive Nash equilibrium (DNE). We prove that the deceptive bandit learning (DBL) dynamics converge to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of the DNE while retaining optimal convergence rates. Interestingly, our analysis attains these optimal rates while relaxing second-order smoothness conditions from standard bandit optimization literature. We characterize conditions under which deception strictly shifts the steady state and its effect on the deceiver's cost, illustrating the results in a resource-allocation game.

</details>


### [58] [Are Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuning Methods Really Different?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09122)
*Yikuan Li,Pinyan Lu,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较六种参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在语言与扩散模型中的表现，探讨参数化方式与任务性能、遗忘及预训练权重几何变化的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT 存在多种参数化方式，但它们在方法论和功能上的差异仍不清楚，尤其是几何保持是否对适配与保持能力本身重要尚不明确。

Method: 对比六种 PEFT 方法；受正交微调（OFT）的谱保持设计启发，检验谱保持的必要性；对 LoRA 族方法的奇异值谱进行恢复干预；并开展不同谱成分分组的干预实验。

Result: LoRA 族方法也近似保持预训练几何，恢复其轻微漂移的奇异值谱基本不损性能，说明显式几何保持并非必需；LoRA 最稳定地限制遗忘，DoRA 在多数比较中平均任务得分更高，PiSSA 常带来更大保持代价；恢复主导谱成分带来的泛化文本 NLL 或基图漂移平均降幅最大。

Conclusion: 应通过几何约束的功能后果而非单纯保持性来评估 PEFT 方法，不同方法的性能增益可归因于不同谱成分的修改。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers many parameterizations, yet their methodological and functional differences remain unclear. We compare six methods in language and diffusion models to examine how their parameterizations relate to task performance, forgetting, and changes in pretrained weight geometry. Motivated by the spectrum-preserving design of orthogonal fine-tuning (OFT), we first ask whether spectral preservation is itself important for adaptation and retention. We find that the selected LoRA-family methods also approximately preserve pretrained geometry, and that restoring their slightly drifted singular-value spectra largely preserves task performance, questioning the necessity of explicit geometric preservation. Beyond this, we observe that some methods exhibit distinct adaptation--retention trade-offs that vary across settings: LoRA most consistently limits forgetting at competitive performance, DoRA achieves higher mean task scores than LoRA in most comparisons, while PiSSA often incurs greater retention costs. Further intervention experiments suggest that while performance gains from different PEFT methods can be attributed to modifications in different groups of spectral components, we consistently find that restoring dominant rather than intermediate or trailing components produces the largest mean reduction in general-text NLL or base-image drift. Together, these results motivate evaluating geometric constraints through their functional consequences rather than preservation alone. Code is available at https://github.com/Kuaaannn/PEFT_methods.

</details>


### [59] [Spatial Induction Heads: In-Context Learning of Multidimensional Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09124)
*Kimia Kazemian,Menghan Xu,John Thickstun,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"空间归纳头"，解释Transformer如何在二维展开的序列中克服空间邻居被打散的路由难题，实现上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳头理论假设与预测相关的上下文是连续区块，但在多维数据序列化后，空间邻居被分散到序列的远距离位置，破坏了这一假设，因此需要研究Transformer如何解决这一空间路由问题。

Method: 研究多维随机和确定性元胞自动机任务，轨迹由未知局部规则生成并作为展平序列呈现，无显式坐标空间偏置。提出空间归纳头：两层"收集-匹配"电路，第一层重建相关空间邻域，第二层将得到的构型与先前出现匹配；给出收集层的两种显式实现，并构造实现贝叶斯计数的匹配层。

Result: 空间路由所需的位置维度仅取决于局部邻域和空间维度，与网格体积或轨迹长度无关。端到端电路对随机规则可任意逼近贝叶斯后验，对确定性规则可精确预测。训练的两层Transformer在一维和二维设置中泛化到未见规则，确定性展开近乎完美，随机规则下与贝叶斯最优预测器的KL散度小于0.005 nats。

Conclusion: 注意力模式和逐层探针与预测的"收集-匹配"计算一致，为训练后的Transformer存在空间归纳提供了机制性证据。

Abstract: Induction heads provide a mechanistic account of in-context learning in sequential data, but existing theory largely assumes that the context relevant to a prediction forms a contiguous block. In multidimensional data, serialization breaks this assumption by scattering spatial neighbors across distant positions in the token sequence. We study how transformers overcome this routing problem in multidimensional stochastic and deterministic cellular automata, where each trajectory is generated by an unknown local rule and presented as a flattened sequence without an explicit coordinate-based spatial inductive bias. We introduce spatial induction heads, two-layer gather-and-match circuits in which the first layer reconstructs the relevant spatial neighborhood and the second matches the resulting configuration against earlier occurrences. We give two explicit realizations of the gather and show that the positional dimension required for spatial routing depends only on the local neighborhood and spatial dimension, not on grid volume or trajectory horizon. We further construct a matching layer which implements Bayesian counting. The end-to-end circuit can approximate the Bayesian posterior arbitrarily closely for stochastic rules and can predict exactly for deterministic rules. Empirically, trained two-layer transformers generalize to unseen rules in one and two dimensional settings, achieving near-perfect deterministic rollouts and less than 0.005 nats KL from the Bayes-optimal predictor on stochastic rules. Attention patterns and layerwise probes align with the predicted gather-and-match computation, providing mechanistic evidence for spatial induction in trained transformers.

</details>


### [60] [Domain-informed Adaptive Sampling for Generalizable PINNs in Metal Additive Manufacturing via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09126)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向金属增材制造中物理信息神经网络（PINN）的条件感知自适应配点采样策略，通过条件流匹配学习高残差分布并结合领域先验基分布进行混合采样，在相同配点预算下平均降低62.1%的相对L2误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的热建模对理解金属增材制造的工艺-结构-性能链条至关重要。PINN可作为有效的代理热模型，但现有工作多依赖手工设计、静态的配点采样策略，缺乏原理性且难以跨工艺条件扩展，从而限制了泛化能力。

Method: 作者通过经验风险最小化进行理论分析，证明工艺条件感知的自适应采样在泛化上严格优于传统静态采样；据此提出两阶段框架：(1) 条件Flow Matching模型，学习不同工艺条件下近似的高残差分布；(2) 混合采样策略，将该分布与领域先验基分布结合，生成自适应配点以精调PINN预测器。

Result: 在金属增材制造数值基准上，该方法在相同配点预算下持续优于最先进的PINN基线，平均相对L2误差降低62.1%，并能捕捉文献中常被忽略的工艺相关散热区域。

Conclusion: 这是首个面向金属增材制造PINN的自适应采样策略，有助于提升泛化能力与更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Accurate thermal modeling is essential in metal additive manufacturing (AM) for understanding the process-structure-property chain. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer effective surrogate thermal modeling by minimizing physics-based residual losses at collocation points. However, prior works typically rely on manually-crafted, static collocation sampling strategies, which are neither principled nor scalable across process conditions, hindering their generalization capability. In this work, we provide theoretical analysis through empirical risk minimization, showing that process condition-aware adaptive sampling is strictly more favorable than conventional static sampling for generalization. Building on this insight, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy within a two-stage framework: (1) a conditional Flow Matching model that learns approximate high-residual distributions across different process conditions, and (2) a mixed sampling strategy combining this distribution with a domain-informed base distribution to generate adaptive collocation points for refining the PINN predictor. Experiments on metal AM numerical benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PINN baselines, achieving an average 62.1\% reduction in relative $L_2$ error under an identical collocation budget, by capturing process-dependent heat dissipation regions often overlooked in the literature. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first adaptive sampling strategy for PINNs in metal AM, contributing to the enhanced generalization and broader applicability.

</details>


### [61] [Directional Evidence Guided Search-Space Reduction for Exact DAG Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09136)
*Upala Junaida Islam,Abdelmonem Elrefaey,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DECO框架，通过从数据中提取依赖与方向证据构建可允许父集，从而在精确优化前缩小DAG学习搜索空间，实现指数级空间缩减并保持竞争性的结构恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据学习有向无环图（DAG）是组合难题，候选父集配置数量随规模指数增长。现有精确评分方法需大量组合搜索，约束方法在图规模和条件集复杂度增加时可靠性差或计算代价高。因此需要一个能有效缩减搜索空间又不依赖固定参数结构模型的方法。

Method: 提出非参数混合框架DECO（方向证据引导的配置优化）。它先从观测数据中提取依赖性与方向性证据，构建可允许父集，再进行精确优化。通过剔除经验上不支持的父配置来缩减搜索空间，同时保留所有可能边方向的灵活性，无需固定参数结构模型。

Result: 理论分析证明可允许父集配置空间呈指数级缩减，并量化了有界边级遗漏对保留真实父结构概率的影响。在基准贝叶斯网络及合成离散、连续DAG上的实验显示，搜索空间大幅缩减，同时结构恢复性能具有竞争力，在多数评估设置下结构汉明距离表现良好。

Conclusion: 方向证据可作为有效的预处理机制，在不需固定参数结构模型的前提下降低精确DAG学习的计算负担，为可扩展的因果结构学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: Learning a directed acyclic graph (DAG) from observational data is a challenging combinatorial problem due to the exponential growth in the number of candidate parent-set configurations. Existing exact score-based methods often require computationally intensive combinatorial search, whereas constraint-based methods can become unreliable or computationally demanding as graph size and conditioning-set complexity increase. We develop a non-parametric hybrid framework, referred to as DECO (Directional Evidence-guided Configuration Optimization), that extracts dependency and directional evidence from observation data to construct admissible parent sets prior to exact optimization. It reduces the optimization search space by eliminating empirically unsupported parent configurations while preserving flexibility for all plausible edge orientations. Theoretical analysis establishes an exponential reduction in the admissible parent-set configuration space and quantifies how bounded edge-level omission affects the probability of retaining the true parent structure. Experiments on benchmark Bayesian networks and synthetic discrete and continuous DAGs demonstrate substantial search-space reduction while achieving competitive structure-recovery performance, with favorable structural Hamming distance across many evaluated settings. These results show that directional evidence can provide an effective preprocessing mechanism for reducing the computational burden of exact DAG learning without requiring a fixed parametric structural~model.

</details>


### [62] [A Cognitive-Aware QML-CRL Framework for Detecting Affinity and Romance-Investment Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09141)
*Bibhas Adhikari,Ramya Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合量子-经典框架，通过建模操纵性对话中的认知偏差来检测情感与恋爱投资类诈骗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感与恋爱投资诈骗借助操纵性对话利用受害者的认知偏差，传统方法难以捕捉此类对话中的时序重构与叙事共现特征，因此需要新的建模方式。

Method: 采用结构化参数化量子电路：以专用量子比特承载核心认知偏差，用frame qubit使编码对操纵性重构的时间顺序敏感，用narrative qubit通过可训练纠缠层聚合共现信息；电路参数与经典强化学习智能体联合训练，智能体将是否标记对话建模为最优停止问题，逐轮决策。

Result: 在包含困难负样本（合法但紧急、合法但强推销的销售对话）的合成对话上评估了模型性能。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典框架为检测情感与恋爱投资诈骗提供了一种结合认知偏差建模与最优停止决策的新思路。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical framework that detects affinity and romance-investment fraud by modelling the cognitive biases in a manipulative conversation. In our proposed framework, cognitive biases central to this fraud class are carried by dedicated qubits in a structured parameterized quantum circuit, together with a frame qubit makes the encoding sensitive to the temporal order of manipulative reframing, and a narrative qubit that aggregates co-occurrence through a trainable entanglement layer. The circuit parameters are trained jointly with a classical reinforcement-learning agent that decides, turn by turn, whether to flag the conversation, modeled as an optimal stopping problem. We evaluate the model's performance on synthetic conversations that include hard negatives, legitimate but urgent, and legitimate but pushy sales conversations.

</details>


### [63] [Noise Your Prompt: Noising Conditioning Tokens in Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09145)
*Justin Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练连续扩散语言模型时，不再固定干净的条件提示词，而是也对提示词加噪，这一行改动带来组合推理能力和生成多样性提升，但增益不普适于所有自然语言任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型领域普遍采用"训练时保持条件提示词干净"的做法。作者质疑这一惯例，思考是否反而限制了模型泛化与多样性。

Method: 对标准训练目标做极简修改：在训练时同样对条件提示词加噪。该方法仅需改动一行训练代码，默认不增加推理成本，并支持类似 classifier-free guidance 的引导采样。

Result: 在 Sudoku、N-Queens 等组合推理任务上泛化更好，难变体增益最大（Sudoku Hard 求解率 3.73%→24.65%），生成解多样性提升（10x10 N-Queens 覆盖率 50.60%→73.79%）；在 Gigaword 摘要等小规模数据集的自然语言生成上有可测提升，但增益不迁移到所有任务（如开放式对话生成）。

Conclusion: "对提示词加噪"是一处简单、低成本且灵活的改进，能提升组合推理与生成多样性，但收益依赖于任务类型。代码已公开。

Abstract: We revisit a standard accepted practice in the continuous diffusion language model
  literature of fixing conditioning prompt tokens clean during training.
  We make a very simple modification: also noise the conditioning prompt tokens during training.
  We demonstrate that under this modified training objective, we achieve better generalization
  in combinatorial reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and N-Queens, with the largest gains on harder variants
  ($3.73\% \to 24.65\%$ solve rate on Sudoku Hard), and increased diversity of generated solutions ($50.60\% \to 73.79\%$ coverage on
  10x10 N-Queens). We also show measurable improvements to natural language generation quality
  in modest dataset regimes with Gigaword summarization, but notably demonstrate that gains do not
  transfer to all natural language tasks (e.g open ended dialogue generation).
  Our method is a single line change to the training objective, requires no additional inference costs by default,
  and provides the flexibility of classifier-free guidance inspired guided sampling. Our
  \href{https://github.com/LateralIntelligence/noise-your-prompt} {code} is publicly available.

</details>


### [64] [Context-aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Models for Vehicular Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09174)
*Arash Raftari,Babak Ebrahimi Soorchaei,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知注意力高斯混合模型（CAA-GMM），用于多模态、不确定性感知的轨迹预测，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2上性能优异且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂不确定环境下，协作与自动驾驶需要可靠且可解释的轨迹预测，现有方法在多模态建模、交互建模与不确定性鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 将未来运动建模为以场景上下文和智能体动力学为条件的概率混合模型，用可解释的高斯分量捕捉多样行为模式；采用轻量级注意力机制自适应编码智能体间交互与上下文显著性，实现栅格化环境线索与运动历史的高效融合。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2上达到与最先进栅格基线相当或更优的精度，同时保持低计算复杂度；消融实验验证注意力模块对上下文推理与预测精度的关键作用；在不完美通信与感知条件下仍表现出韧性。

Conclusion: CAA-GMM是一种高效可扩展的协作轨迹预测方案，适用于智能交通系统。

Abstract: Reliable and interpretable trajectory prediction is critical for cooperative and autonomous driving in complex and uncertain environments. This paper introduces a Context-Aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Model (CAA-GMM) for multimodal, uncertainty-aware motion forecasting. The proposed approach models future motion as a probabilistic mixture conditioned on both scene context and agent dynamics, capturing diverse behavioral modes with interpretable Gaussian components. A lightweight attention mechanism adaptively encodes inter-agent interactions and contextual salience, enabling efficient fusion of rasterized environment cues and motion history in dense traffic scenes. Comprehensive evaluations on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that CAA-GMM achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared with state-of-the-art raster-based baselines, while maintaining low computational complexity. Ablation analyses confirm the importance of the attention module for robust contextual reasoning and predictive precision. Furthermore, evaluations under imperfect communication and perception conditions highlight the framework's resilience to uncertainty, establishing CAA-GMM as an efficient and scalable solution for cooperative trajectory prediction in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [65] [LayerRoPE: Dynamic Depth-wise Magnitude & Angular Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09179)
*Shikhar Srivastava,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LayerRoPE，将 Transformer 中随深度增长的隐藏状态范数重新解释为一种"深度位置编码"，并用沿深度轴的隐式 RoPE 类比显式建模该编码，从而以更少参数和几乎不变的 FLOPs 显著提升深层模型的训练稳定性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据在 Transformer 中传播，隐藏状态范数随深度呈数量级增长，这一现象被称为"深度诅咒"，几乎被普遍视为需要抑制的病态现象。作者对此提出相反观点，认为该增长实际上反映了一种涌现出的深度位置编码能力。

Method: 作者在来自 9 个家族的 16 个预训练 LLM 上观察残差流上唯一的学习型逐层增益——归一化权重 γ，发现其随深度在幅度上增长、在方向上旋转，共同编码层索引。基于此提出 LayerRoPE，将逐层 γ 向量替换为单个共享向量加深度条件标量，作为沿深度轴的隐式 RoPE 类比，净减少参数且 FLOPs 变化小于 0.02%。

Result: 在扩展到 100B+ tokens 的模型阶梯上，LayerRoPE 持续优于 Pre-Norm、Post-Norm、Peri-Norm 和 Layer-Norm Scaling，以 3.4 倍更少算力达到 Pre-Norm 1.3B 的损失；是唯一在深度扩展到 512 层时仍保持强收敛且近乎单调提升的方法。它还将学习率敏感度改善 3–10 倍，并能朴素迁移至循环潜变量模型和视觉 Transformer 并持续带来提升。

Conclusion: 对 LayerRoPE 学习到的调度进行分析，颠覆了主流前提：它并不缩小残差流，反而拓宽残差流，抑制每个块读取的内容、放大其写入的内容。结果表明，深度稳定性需要的不是抑制残差流，而是对残差流所供给的计算块进行深度条件调制。

Abstract: As data propagates through a Transformer, the norm of its hidden states grows by orders of magnitude with depth, a phenomenon framed as 'curse of depth' and nearly universally treated as a pathology to be suppressed. We take the opposite view. Across 16 pre-trained LLMs from 9 families, spanning dense, mixture-of-experts and hybrid architectures and Pre-, Peri- and Post-Norm designs, we find that this growth reflects an emergent depth-positional encoding, carried by the only learned per-layer gain on the residual stream, the normalization weight $γ$: with depth, $γ$ grows in magnitude and rotates in direction, jointly encoding the layer index. We make this depth-conditioned encoding explicit with LayerRoPE, an implicit analog of RoPE along the depth axis, which replaces all layerwise $γ$ vectors with a single shared vector and depth-conditioned scalars, at a net reduction in parameters and $<0.02\%$ change in FLOPs. Across a model ladder scaled up to $100$B+ tokens, LayerRoPE consistently outperforms Pre-, Post- and Peri-Norm and Layer-Norm Scaling, reaching Pre-Norm's 1.3B loss with $3.4\times$ less compute; LayerRoPE is the only approach that shows strong convergence and improves near monotonically as depth scales to 512 layers. It improves learning-rate sensitivity by $3$-$10\times$, and transfers naively to and consistently improves looped latent models and Vision Transformers. Inspecting its learned schedule inverts the prevailing premise: LayerRoPE does not shrink the residual stream but widens it, damping what each block reads while amplifying what it writes. Depth stability, our results suggest, calls not for suppressing the residual stream, but for depth-conditioned regulation of the computational blocks it feeds.

</details>


### [66] [Q-PACE: Dynamic Precision Allocation for Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09183)
*Alexandra Volkova,Matin Ansaripour,Erik Schultheis,Christoph H. Lampert,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-PACE 提出一种二阶敏感度模型，将量化引起的损失增量建模为逐层曲率系数加权的量化噪声MSE之和，并在训练中周期性重算系数、重新分配精度，从而在混合精度QAT中降低显存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知训练虽能降低大模型部署成本，但激进量化会损害最终性能。常见的缓解方式是混合精度训练，即给部分层分配高精度以保持性能、同时控制成本，但这需要为各层决定精度分配方案。现有分配方法不够理想，因此需要更准确、低开销的精度分配策略。

Method: 提出 Q-PACE：(1) 一个二阶敏感度模型，预测损失增量为各层量化噪声MSE乘以其曲率系数的加权和；(2) 训练过程中通过跨层扰动周期性重算这些曲率系数，并据此重新分配精度。方法还利用敏感度随深度和层类型的可预测性以及其在训练中的稳定性，实现低频、低成本的重校准。

Result: 在参数量最高达4B的LLM上进行预训练和有监督微调实验，Q-PACE 一致优于现有混合精度训练方案，并在显著更低的总显存预算下达到相当损失。此外发现量化敏感度可由深度和层类型高度预测，且训练中保持稳定，支持不频繁、低成本的重校准。

Conclusion: Q-PACE 通过曲率加权的量化噪声敏感度建模实现了有效的动态混合精度分配，在保持性能的同时大幅降低显存开销；敏感度的可预测性与稳定性进一步降低了校准成本，为高效LLM量化训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) leverages lower-precision arithmetic to reduce the cost of LLM deployment, but aggressive quantization degrades final model performance. A common remedy is mixed-precision training, in which high precision is assigned to some of the layers to maintain performance while keeping the cost constrained. This approach then requires precision assignments for model layers during training. We provide a new approach, called Q-PACE, consisting of a second-order sensitivity model that predicts the loss increase as a sum of quantization noise MSE weighted by per-layer curvature coefficients. During training, we periodically re-compute these coefficients using perturbations across layers, and re-assign precision. Pretraining and supervised fine-tuning experiments on LLMs of up to 4B parameters show that Q-PACE consistently improves over existing mixed-precision training recipes, and achieves comparable loss at substantially lower total memory budgets. We further find that quantization sensitivity is highly predictable by depth and layer type, and its stability during training allows for infrequent, cheap recalibration.

</details>


### [67] [The Dichotomy Between Pattern Recognition and Step-by-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09186)
*Amrut Nadgir,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出模式识别与逐步推理是同一谱系的两端，用De Bruijn图刻画推理结构，论证逐步推理样本高效，并通过实验揭示状态频率对准确性与鲁棒性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从理论上统一理解大语言模型中的模式识别与逐步推理两种机制，并用可度量的结构（上下文长度c、状态频率）来解释推理能力、样本效率与鲁棒性之间的关系。

Method: 将下一token依赖的最近c个token建模为De Bruijn图：节点为c长度上下文，边为下一token转移；任务的所有推理轨迹构成该图的有向无环子图。通过理论证明边数远小于轨迹数，并用实验测量transformer所需训练样本数与边数的幂律关系；同时提出通过引入"状态"诱导De Bruijn结构，以状态频率决定c。再用Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct在解方程与故事问答任务上微调，比较不同c下模型的准确性与扰动脆弱性；最后在GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond上限制注意力窗口验证真实数据的De Bruijn结构。

Result: 理论上，子图边数相对推理轨迹数可忽略不计；实验显示训练样本需求与边数呈幂律关系，说明逐步推理样本高效。微调实验表明：频繁状态（小c）带来更高准确率但对测试扰动更脆弱；大c训练的模型仅相当于无推理的模式识别；中等状态密度能平衡准确性与鲁棒性。真实数据方面，Qwen3-14B与Qwen3-32B在GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond上，当注意力限制在不足完整推理轨迹长度15%的滑动窗口时，仍保留超过75%的准确率。

Conclusion: 模式识别与逐步推理可由De Bruijn结构统一描述，逐步推理因边数远少于轨迹数而样本高效；状态频率（即c）决定准确性与鲁棒性的权衡，中等状态密度为最优；真实世界任务数据确实具备De Bruijn结构，支持该理论框架的有效性。

Abstract: We argue that pattern recognition and step-by-step reasoning are two ends of a spectrum. A large language model (LLM) learns to reason step-by-step when data is structured such that the next token depends on a small amount of preceding context. Inference in LLMs resembles pattern recognition when the next token depends on a large amount of preceding context. If the next token depends on only the $c$ most recent tokens, reasoning traces are paths on a De Bruijn graph whose nodes are $c$-length contexts and edges are next-token transitions between contexts. The set of reasoning traces of a task forms a directed acyclic subgraph of the De Bruijn graph. An LLM that has learned all edges of this subgraph can compose them to solve longer, unseen tasks, i.e., it reasons step-by-step. We prove that the number of edges is vanishingly small compared to the number of reasoning traces. Empirically, the number of training samples a transformer needs is a power law in the number of edges, so learning to reason step-by-step is sample efficient. We can induce De Bruijn structure in any task by maintaining a ``state'' that makes future reasoning independent of the past. The frequency of states in the reasoning trace determines $c$. We show, by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to solve equations and answer questions about stories, that frequent states (small $c$) result in higher accuracy but greater fragility to perturbations at test time. LLMs trained with a large $c$ are only as good as models that perform pattern recognition without reasoning. A moderate density of states balances accuracy and robustness. We show that real-world data has De Bruijn structure: Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B retain over 75% of their accuracy on GSM8K, MATH-500 and GPQA-Diamond when attention is restricted to a sliding window less than 15% as long as the full reasoning trace.

</details>


### [68] [Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$): What Counts as Personalization in Medical World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09194)
*Xingrui Gu,Hanxue Gu,Yuxiang Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出P³审计框架与Cancer JEPA模型，用于判断纵向影像预测是否真正利用了患者个体信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向模型能预测患者影像状态的演变，但预测准确率高并不代表预测是由该患者自身的观测轨迹驱动的；群体平均预测可能有用，却无法支持"患者特异世界模型"的主张，因此需要一个能区分"输入被使用"与"具备患者特异预测价值"的审计方法。

Method: 提出Patient, Place, Prior（P³）审计：检验预测是否受益于患者自身纵向影像史（Patient）、是否受益于患者匹配的外部空间支持（Place）、以及在匹配支持与上下文下是否优于群体平均预测（Prior）。并提出Cancer JEPA，一种单步模型，用于预测新辅助治疗期间乳腺动态增强MRI检查的冻结表征；在患者条件化的低复杂度降秩回归基线上，加入病灶约束的神经校正，并用基于遮挡的潜空间目标训练。这种分解结构支持对冻结模型进行事后P³审计。

Result: 在曾用于开发的验证队列中，当神经校正接收的是患者自身病史而非其他患者病史、且接收的是患者匹配的病灶占据图而非替代图时，预测误差更低。然而，将基于患者病史计算的校正与群体平均神经校正进行比较的描述性95%区间包含零。

Conclusion: P³能够将"输入被使用"与"具备超越群体层面模式的、患者特异的预测价值证据"区分开来。

Abstract: Longitudinal models forecast how a patient's imaging state evolves, but accuracy does not show whether the patient's observed trajectory drives the prediction. A population-average forecast may be useful but cannot establish a patient-specific world-model claim. We introduce Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$), an audit asking whether a forecast benefits from the patient's longitudinal imaging history (Patient), benefits from patient-matched externally supplied spatial support (Place), and gains predictive value beyond a population-average prediction under matched support and context (Prior). We also propose Cancer JEPA, a one-step model that forecasts frozen representations of future breast dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI examinations during neoadjuvant therapy. It adds a lesion-constrained neural correction, trained with an occlusion-based latent objective, to a patient-conditioned low-complexity reduced-rank regression baseline. This factorization permits a post-hoc P$^3$ audit of the frozen model. In a validation cohort previously used in development, forecast error is lower when the neural correction receives the patient's history rather than another patient's and patient-matched lesion occupancy maps rather than substituted maps. However, the descriptive 95% interval comparing the correction computed from patient history with the population-average neural correction includes zero. P$^3$ thus separates input use from evidence of patient-specific predictive value beyond a population-level pattern.

</details>


### [69] [Exact Dynamics and Finite-Sample Trajectory Recovery of Linear Recursive Feature Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09196)
*Andrew Cheng,Bobak T. Kiani,Yue M. Lu,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性递归特征机（RFM）在含噪多输出回归中的动力学与统计性质，证明学习到的特征矩阵在每次迭代都接近其无限数据理想值，误差以 $O(\sqrt{d/n})$ 高概率衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归特征机（RFM）通过交替拟合预测器与用平均梯度外积（AGOP）更新特征来学习数据表示。AGOP 与神经网络特征学习之间的联系，促使研究者将线性 RFM 作为分析表示在训练中如何演化的简单设定。然而已有关于线性 RFM 与迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）的联系主要局限于插值（无噪）情形，缺乏对含噪、正则化回归的理论刻画。

Method: 在等距亚高斯输入数据、目标由维度为 $d$ 的低秩教师矩阵生成的多输出含噪回归设定下研究线性 RFM。将线性 RFM 与迭代重加权最小二乘的联系从插值情形推广到带岭正则化的含噪多输出回归，并分析特征矩阵逐次迭代的行为。

Result: 证明学习的特征矩阵在每次迭代都保持与无限数据理想对应量接近；对 $n$ 个样本，特征矩阵的误差以高概率按 $O(\sqrt{d/n})$ 衰减。并在真实世界的文本数据和单细胞基因表达数据上进行实验，展示了该简单线性模型学到的特征。

Conclusion: 线性 RFM 在含噪多输出回归中具有可分析的表示演化动力学，其特征估计随样本量增大而稳定收敛到理想特征，为理解更复杂神经网络中的特征学习提供了简化的理论基准，且在实际数据上具有经验意义。

Abstract: Recursive feature machines (RFMs) learn representations of data by alternating between fitting a predictor to a dataset and updating features of that predictor using the average gradient outer product (AGOP). Connections between AGOPs and feature learning in neural networks motivate linear RFMs as a simple setting for analyzing how representations evolve during training. Here, we study the dynamics and statistics of linear RFM in noisy multi-output regression with isotropic sub-Gaussian input data and targets generated by a low-rank teacher matrix of dimension $d$. We extend the known connection between linear RFM and iteratively reweighted least squares from the interpolating setting to ridge-regularized multi-output regression with noise. We show that the learned feature matrix remains close to its infinite-data ideal counterpart at every iteration. Namely, for $n$ samples, we show the error in the feature matrix decays as $O(\sqrt{d/n})$ with high probability. Experiments on real-world text and single-cell gene-expression data illustrate the features learned by this simple linear model.

</details>


### [70] [An Accuracy--Information Tradeoff for Loss-Difference Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09206)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在光滑凸损失与幂次正则化下，任何具有小超额风险的正常学习器都会被迫具有阶为 n 比特的损失差条件互信息（ld-CMI），且该下界在标准泛化界框架中无法消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论泛化界中，标准观测层次里最小的是损失差条件互信息。已有结果表明准确性会把信息压入模型（模型级信息有下界），但数据处理不等式无法把这类下界传递到损失层面。作者希望填补这一空白，研究准确性是否同样强制损失差条件互信息，以及这种强制是否受正则化、噪声、维度等条件影响。

Method: 作者通过界定损失差的三个矩来建立下界论证。研究对象为线性预测器，配以在零点斜率非零的光滑凸损失（如 logistic 损失），并可选地加入曲率与增长均为幂次 r≥2 的正则项。数据分布为缩放符号立方体上的乘积分布，维度至少与 n 线性相关。在最优样本量 n≍ε^{-2+2/r}（无正则化时 n≍ε^{-2}）下，分析期望超额风险至多为 ε 的任意正常学习器的最坏情况 ld-CMI；并进一步在损失差上加入标准差为 τ 的高斯噪声情形下给出阶 Θ(n/(1+(τ/ε)^2)) 的界。

Result: 在这些分布上，每个期望超额风险至多 ε 的正常学习器，其最坏情况 ld-CMI 为 n 的阶比特；在损失差高斯噪声下变为 Θ(n/(1+(τ/ε)^2)) 比特。无正则化时同样成立，此时 n≍ε^{-2}。因此，经过范围缩放的 ld-CMI 界在这些分布上不能趋于零，尽管每个正常学习器的泛化间隙仅为 O(n^{-1/2})。作者还表明模型级信息不能决定带噪损失差信息，并验证了增长、斜率与维度条件的必要性，其中维度条件的必要性可放宽至对数因子。

Conclusion: 准确性不仅强制模型级信息，也会强制损失差条件互信息，且该下界在范围缩放后仍不消失。这表明仅靠 ld-CMI 无法给出非平凡的、可趋于零的泛化界；模型级信息与损失差信息之间不存在确定的决定关系。相关正则化增长、损失斜率与维度条件在本质上都是必需的。

Abstract: Loss-difference conditional mutual information (ld-CMI) uses the smallest of the standard observations in the supersample hierarchy of generalization bounds: it measures what a learner's loss differences reveal about which candidate of each pair it was trained on. Accuracy is known to force information into the model; data processing does not carry such lower bounds to losses. We show, by bounding three moments of the loss differences, that accuracy also forces ld-CMI. For linear predictors with a smooth convex loss of nonzero slope at zero, such as the logistic loss, plus a regularizer whose curvature and growth are both of power $r\ge2$, on product distributions over a scaled sign cube in dimension at least linear in $n$, every proper learner with expected excess risk at most $\varepsilon$ on these distributions at the optimal sample size $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2+2/r}$ has worst-case ld-CMI of order $n$ bits, and $Θ(n/(1+(τ/\varepsilon)^2))$ bits under Gaussian noise of standard deviation $τ$ on the loss differences. The same holds without a regularizer, at $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2}$. Consequently, range-scaled ld-CMI bounds cannot vanish on these distributions, although every proper learner's generalization gap is $O(n^{-1/2})$. We also show that model-level information does not determine noisy loss-difference information, and that the growth, slope and dimension conditions are needed, the last up to a logarithm.

</details>


### [71] [CurveTQ: Rotation-Free Trellis Quantization of LLM Weights via Curvature-Weighted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09212)
*Guanhua Ding,Zi Wang,Ruichao Li,Jack Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出主流二比特权重量化器未充分利用Hessian信息，提出将Hessian的LDL对角权重放入Viterbi分支度量，构建无需旋转的CurveTQ，在多个模型上取得更优精度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QTIP、Proteus等最优二比特量化器先对权重矩阵做随机正交旋转（解码时须撤销），再用trellis或lattice码在欧氏空间搜索，Hessian仅通过编码块间的误差反馈进入，导致部分Hessian信息被浪费。作者希望更充分地利用Hessian曲率信息，同时省去旋转带来的解码开销。

Method: 分析表明误差反馈将损失转化为各坐标舍入误差的加权和，其权重正是Hessian的LDL分解对角，已有量化器计算了却未使用。作者将该权重引入Viterbi分支度量，使搜索在编码块内跟随曲率；这同时解释了旋转的作用——旋转消除了块内变化，故在原基下加权与旋转是替代方案。基于此构建CurveTQ：无旋转的trellis编解码器，用因子化scale field处理幅度与边缘形状，用闭式分位表，并为每个编码块存储起始状态以使trellis适应误差反馈带来的残差。

Result: 在三个模型上，加权原生搜索与全维随机Hadamard相差约一个下游精度点，旋转后再加权收益甚微。二比特下CurveTQ在三个4-8B Instruct模型上平均下游精度比QTIP和Proteus高1-3点，即使基线获得起始状态后亦然；仅起始状态一项即可提升基线1-3点。在35B混合专家模型上也领先，为该类模型首个trellis编码结果。无旋转撤销使解码器在所有测试批次大小和比特宽度下为三者中最快。

Conclusion: 通过在原基下将Hessian的LDL对角权重纳入Viterbi搜索，可在无需旋转的情况下更充分利用曲率信息，CurveTQ在精度与解码速度上均优于现有最优二比特量化方法，并首次将trellis编码成功用于大规模MoE模型。

Abstract: The best two-bit weight quantizers for large language models, such as QTIP and Proteus, rotate each weight matrix by a random orthogonal transform, which must be undone at every decoding step, then encode it with a trellis or lattice code under a Euclidean search; the layer Hessian enters only through error feedback between coding blocks. We show that this leaves part of the Hessian unused. Error feedback turns the loss into a weighted sum of per-coordinate rounding errors whose weights, the diagonal of the Hessian's LDL factorization, existing quantizers compute but never read. We put these weights into the Viterbi branch metric, so the search follows the curvature within each coding block. This also explains the rotation: it removes this within-block variation, so weighting in the native basis and rotating are substitutes. On three models the weighted native search matches a full-dimension randomized Hadamard to within about one point of downstream accuracy, and weighting after the rotation gains little. Around this search we build CurveTQ, a trellis codec with no rotation, which handles the weights' amplitude and marginal shape with a factored scale field and a closed-form quantile table, and stores a start state per coding block so the trellis can adapt to the residual that error feedback carries into it. At two bits CurveTQ is 1-3 points higher in mean downstream accuracy than QTIP and Proteus on three 4-8B Instruct models, even after both are given our start state, which alone lifts either baseline by 1-3 points. It also leads on a 35B mixture of experts, to our knowledge the first trellis-coded result on such a model. With no rotation to undo, our decoder is the fastest of the three at all tested batch sizes and bit widths.

</details>


### [72] [Consistent Distribution Matching for Data-Free Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09221)
*Yuxiang Fu,Qi Yan,Zike Wu,Yongxing Zhang,Purang Abolmaesumi,Lele Wang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Consistent Distribution Matching，一种无需仿真、无需数据的蒸馏方法，可加速扩散与流模型并保持生成质量，在ImageNet 256×256上1-NFE FID达2.04。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散与流模型推理慢，需大量数值积分；已有蒸馏方法依赖人工整理的数据集、昂贵的教师模型展开或辅助代理网络，导致训练与扩展复杂。

Method: 统一采样生成与分数估计于单一学生网络，框架仅含一个冻结教师和一个可训练学生，优化单一目标；并理论证明该目标最小化意味着学生流映射推前分布向教师边缘分布的Wasserstein收敛。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，训练40轮内实现1-NFE FID为2.04、4-NFE FID为1.37，超越无数据条件下的最先进蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 该方法为扩散与流模型的加速蒸馏提供了免仿真、免数据且理论有保障的简洁方案，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion models suffer from slow inference due to computationally expensive numerical integration. Distillation provides a promising way for a student model to learn from a teacher's dynamics, enabling one-step or few-step generation. However, existing methods often depend on curated distillation datasets, costly teacher rollouts, or auxiliary proxy networks, which complicate model training and scaling. In this work, we propose Consistent Distribution Matching, a simulation-free and data-free distillation method for accelerating diffusion and flow models while preserving strong generative capacity. Our key insight is to unify sample generation and score estimation with one student network. Thus, our framework uses only two models, a frozen teacher and a trainable student, and optimizes one objective. We prove that minimizing our objective indicates Wasserstein convergence of the student flow-map pushforwards to the teacher marginals. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, our method attains an FID of 2.04 with a single function evaluation (1-NFE) and a 4-NFE FID of 1.37 within 40 epochs of training, surpassing the state-of-the-art distillation baselines without data. Our code code and model are available at https://consistentdmd.github.io/.

</details>


### [73] [Conditional Accuracy Profiles: Diagnosing LLM Judges across Deployment Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09229)
*Wenqi Li,Bin Liu,Mindi Ruan,Chuanbo Hu,Minglei Yin,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件准确度画像(CAP)框架，将LLM评判者的配对准确率分解为八种条件，揭示聚合准确率掩盖的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge已成为可扩展评估的标准工具，但评判者性能常被单一准确率数字概括，掩盖了其在何种部署条件下成功或失败，不利于评判者选择。

Method: CAP是一种事后诊断框架，将配对评判准确率分解为内容敏感性、鲁棒性与理由质量三大类共八个条件。框架与基准无关，可通过基准自带标注直接应用、任务子集代理近似应用，或通过受控增强生成扰动对。作者在六个配对评判基准、七个LLM评判者上实例化CAP，并构建judgerEva-Standard受控测试台以支持全部八个条件。

Result: CAP揭示了聚合准确率掩盖的画像差异：在judgerEva的评判者无关Hard-Constructed子集上，对遗漏限定条件最敏感的两个评判者，其总体准确率在七者中排名后三，说明遗漏敏感度无法由聚合准确率预测。跨基准中，位置鲁棒性排名稳定性最强(平均Spearman约0.87)，但在JudgeBench-Pro对抗压力下最脆弱，共享条件中平均准确率下降最大，且主导退化渠道因评判者而异。

Conclusion: 条件级画像比聚合准确率更能为LLM评判者的选择提供可操作依据。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge is now a standard tool for scalable evaluation, but judge performance is still often summarized by a single accuracy number. This aggregate view hides the deployment conditions under which a judge succeeds or fails. We introduce \textbf{Conditional Accuracy Profiling} (CAP), a post-hoc diagnostic framework that decomposes pairwise LLM-judge accuracy into eight conditions organized into content sensitivity, robustness, and rationale quality. CAP is benchmark-agnostic: it can be applied directly when a benchmark provides the required annotations, approximately through task-subset proxies, or through controlled augmentation when perturbation pairs can be generated. We instantiate CAP on seven LLM judges across six pairwise judging benchmarks, including \textsc{judgerEva-Standard}, a controlled testbed we created to support all eight conditions. CAP exposes profile differences hidden by aggregate accuracy: on \textsc{judgerEva}'s judge-independent Hard-Constructed subset, the two judges most sensitive to omitted qualifications rank in the bottom three of seven by overall accuracy, so omission sensitivity is not predicted by aggregate accuracy. Across benchmarks, Position Robustness shows the strongest rank stability (mean Spearman $\barρ{=}0.87$) but is itself fragile under JudgeBench-Pro adversarial stress, showing the largest mean accuracy drop among the shared conditions, though the dominant degradation channel varies by judge. Condition-level profiles provide a more actionable basis than aggregate accuracy for selecting LLM judges.

</details>


### [74] [Symmetry-Informed Causal Partial Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09230)
*Uzair Akbar,Zulfiqar Zaidi,Niki Kilbertus,Krikamol Muandet,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将已知的数据对称性（因果效应在特定数据变换下的不变性）作为一种新的约束来源，用于强化部分识别（PI）的因果效应边界，使其更具信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分识别方法通过将数据生成假设编码为约束优化问题来估计因果效应边界。这类边界即使在因果效应本身不可识别时也足以指导政策决策。然而在实践中，这些边界往往是空洞的（vacuous），因此研究者希望尽可能充分地编码领域知识作为额外约束，使边界更具信息量。作者希望找到新的、尚未被充分利用的背景知识来源来强化边界。

Method: 作者引入“已知数据对称性”——即因果效应在某些数据变换下的不变性——作为PI的新约束来源。具体通过两种方式实现：(1) 将其操作化为对因果函数的形状约束（shape constraint）；(2) 通过变量替换（change of measure）并配合简单的数据预处理来施加约束。

Result: 在两种经典的PI模型下，上述两种方法均被证明能够收紧（sharpen）边界。这一结论在总体（population）情形下得到理论证明，并在有限样本情形下通过实验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将数据对称性确立为稳健因果推断中一种自然但尚未被充分利用的背景知识来源。

Abstract: Partial identification (PI) entails estimating bounds on causal effects by encoding different assumptions on data generation as a constrained optimization problem. Such bounds can suffice to inform policy decisions even if the causal effect itself is not identifiable. Often vacuous in practice, practitioners seek to exhaustively encode domain knowledge as additional constraints to make the PI bounds more informative. We introduce known data symmetries -- invariance of the causal effect under certain data transformations -- as a new source of constraints to inform PI. We operationalize this as a shape constraint on the causal function, and via a change of measure against which PI is posed using simple data pre-processing. Both approaches are shown to sharpen bounds under two canonical PI models. This is shown both theoretically for the population case, and via experiments in the finite-sample case. More broadly, our framework establishes data symmetries as a natural, underutilized source of background knowledge for robust causal inference.

</details>


### [75] [An Informational Curse of Horizon in Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09247)
*John L. Zhou,Yuxuan Dong,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出目标条件策略学习中的一种"信息性时间跨度诅咒"：增大目标重标注跨度会降低策略泛化与性能，原因是动作与事后重标注目标之间的条件互信息随跨度增加而下降，策略敏感度从目标信息转向状态信息；通过将短跨度策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长跨度策略可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标达成策略学习的困难通常归因于"时间跨度诅咒"，表现为时序差分备份中的偏差累积和有噪声的优势估计。本文认为还存在一种额外的信息性时间跨度诅咒，即延长目标重标注跨度会显著损害策略的泛化能力和性能，这一因素此前未被充分认识，对从离线数据学习通用策略具有重要意义。

Method: 作者使用神谕规划器进行一系列受控实验，将训练时采样的目标跨度与测试时要求策略到达的目标跨度解耦。即便只在邻近子目标序列上评估，目标条件行为克隆（BC）策略也表现出严重且依赖训练跨度的性能退化，而强化学习（RL）目标可缓解该问题。作者将现象解释为动作与事后重标注目标之间条件互信息随跨度增加而下降，并通过策略输入雅可比测量发现，在长跨度目标上训练的BC和RL策略的敏感度从目标信息转向状态信息。基于此，作者将短跨度策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长跨度策略中。

Result: 训练跨度越长，BC策略性能退化越严重，RL目标能缓解这一退化。长跨度训练的BC和RL策略均表现出从目标信息向状态信息的敏感度转移（通过输入雅可比衡量）。将短跨度策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长跨度策略可带来显著性能提升，在组合式操作任务中尤其明显。

Conclusion: 目标重标注跨度是学习通用策略时的重要考量因素。研究揭示了信息性时间跨度诅咒的存在及其机制（条件互信息下降、敏感度偏移），并提出雅可比蒸馏作为有效的缓解手段，为从离线数据学习通用目标条件策略提供了新见解。

Abstract: The difficulty of learning goal-reaching policies is often attributed to a "curse of horizon" that manifests as bias accumulation in temporal-difference backups and noisy advantage estimates. In this work, we identify an additional informational curse of horizon in goal-conditioned policy learning, where increasing the goal relabeling horizon can significantly reduce policy generalization and performance. Through a series of controlled experiments with oracle planners, we decouple the goal horizons sampled during training from those that the policy is asked to reach at test time. Even when evaluated only on a sequence of nearby subgoals, goal-conditioned behavioral cloning (BC) policies suffer from severe, training horizon-dependent performance degradation that is mitigated by reinforcement learning (RL) objectives. We explain this phenomenon as a horizon-dependent decrease in the conditional mutual information between actions and hindsight-relabeled goals, and find empirically that both BC and RL policies trained on longer-horizon goals exhibit a shift in sensitivity from goal to state information, as measured by the policy's input Jacobians. Motivated by this observation, we find that distilling the input Jacobians of short-horizon policies into long-horizon policies yields significant performance gains, especially in combinatorial manipulation tasks. Taken together, our results highlight goal relabeling horizon as an important consideration when learning generalist policies from offline data.

</details>


### [76] [Efficient Best-of-N policy evaluation for inference-time alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09250)
*Jonas Schweisthal,Yuxin Wang,Athiya Deviyani,Stefan Feuerriegel,Dennis Frauen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖样本的Best-of-N策略评估与选择框架，无需响应似然，利用顺序统计结构将密度比转化为可由样本估计的得分-排名概率，并提出双稳健估计器BoN-DR及两种预算选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N是常见的推理时对齐方法，但仅能获取样本（无法访问响应似然）时，标准离策略估计所需密度比不可得，导致评估困难。此外，较大采样预算可能放大得分函数误差、引发奖励过度优化。

Method: 提出仅样本框架：利用BoN的顺序统计结构，将所需密度比表达为可从样本估计的得分-排名概率；开发双稳健估计器BoN-DR，跨候选预算复用共享辅助样本池；在奖励估计器设定错误下建立渐近推断并证明效率；导出两种选择规则——最大化估计策略值与最大化相对参考策略改进的下置信界（提供无害保证）。

Result: 在合成实验以及GSM8K上使用多种参考模型与奖励模型的实验中，该框架能准确估计BoN策略价值并选出有效的采样预算。

Conclusion: 该工作为仅样本场景下的BoN评估提供了可行且高效的方法，兼顾统计效率与选择安全性。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) is a common inference-time alignment method that selects the highest-scoring response among N samples from a reference model. Evaluating BoN policies from logged data is challenging under sample-only access because standard off-policy estimators require density ratios that depend on unavailable response likelihoods. In this paper, we propose a sample-only framework for evaluating and selecting BoN policies without access to these likelihoods. We show that the order-statistic structure of BoN allows the required density ratios to be expressed through score-rank probabilities that are estimable from samples alone. We then develop a doubly robust estimator of the BoN policy value (BoN-DR) that efficiently reuses a shared auxiliary sample pool across candidate budgets. We establish valid asymptotic inference even under reward estimator misspecification and prove the efficiency of our BoN-DR estimator. Since larger budgets can amplify errors in the score function and lead to reward overoptimization, we derive two selection rules: (i) maximizing the estimated policy value and (ii) maximizing a lower confidence bound on the improvement over the reference policy, which accounts for estimation uncertainty and provides a no-harm guarantee. Across synthetic experiments and GSM8K with multiple reference and reward models, our framework accurately estimates BoN policy values and selects effective sampling budgets.

</details>


### [77] [The Symbol of the Surrogate: Measuring Numerical Provenance in Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09255)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出傅里叶符号诊断方法，发现神经PDE代理模型学到的其实是训练所用的数值格式（含离散误差），而非真实物理演化；标准基准测试无法区分两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE代理模型基于含物理演化和特定求解器离散误差的数值解训练，而测试也使用同一求解器的轨迹。因此，标准基准只能衡量对数值格式的模仿，无法判断模型是否真正逼近精确演化，这一根本性问题此前缺乏直接的检测手段。

Method: 提出一种经验性傅里叶符号诊断：用单个傅里叶模态探测训练后代理模型的线性化单步算子，并与精确演化参考和训练格式参考进行对比。为排除架构谱偏置干扰，在具有正交耗散与色散特征的格式上训练结构相同的网络，比较其学到的算子。

Result: 在线性对流中，学到的代理模型复现了训练格式的振幅与相位误差；双格式差异达到解析预测的完全模仿上限的99.8%以上。对于非局部傅里叶神经算子同样成立，在非线性Burgers动力学的算子层面亦如此。

Conclusion: 与求解器生成测试数据的一致性本身并不能证明对精确演化的忠实性；傅里叶符号测量为数值来源（数值格式出身）提供了直接诊断工具。

Abstract: Neural PDE surrogates are trained on numerical solver outputs that contain both physical evolution and solver-specific discretization errors. Because surrogates are also evaluated against held-out trajectories from the same solver, standard benchmarks cannot distinguish fidelity to the exact evolution from imitation of the numerical scheme. We introduce an empirical Fourier-symbol diagnostic that probes a trained surrogate's linearized one-step operator with individual Fourier modes and compares it with both exact-evolution and training-scheme references. To address architectural spectral bias, we train identical networks on schemes with orthogonal dissipative and dispersive signatures and compare their learned operators. In linear advection, the learned surrogates reproduce the training schemes' amplitude and phase errors, with the twin-scheme difference reaching more than 99.8\% of the analytically predicted full-imitation ceiling. The same behavior occurs for a non-local Fourier neural operator and at the operator level for nonlinear Burgers dynamics. These results show that agreement with solver-generated test data does not by itself establish fidelity to the exact evolution. Fourier-symbol measurements provide a direct diagnostic of numerical provenance.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluating Trajectory Features for Routing Final-Layer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09272)
*Yupeng Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了隐状态轨迹特征（外推误差、曲率、误差变化）能否在不确定性、位移、位置和状态投影之外更好地预测注意力价值，结果显示所预设的六项比较在族系校正后均无正向增益，且固定投影对照反而优于轨迹路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力路由需要一个能预测当前前缀上注意力价值的信号，作者希望检验基于隐状态轨迹的摘要特征是否比已有的不确定性、一步位移、位置与状态投影等信号提供额外的预测价值。

Method: 在冻结的 SmolLM3-3B-Base 与 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 上，通过最后一层注意力的成对执行获得带符号的下一词损失差；用效用监督的路由器在 100 本留出的 PG-19 书籍上测试，采用相同的因果 20% 调用配额；比较六项预设假设，并对固定投影对照做参数匹配。

Result: 六项预设比较经族系校正后均无正向增益。在 Qwen3.5 中，参数匹配的固定投影对照相对轨迹路由器将 NLL 降低 0.00356 nats/token（95% 区间 0.00218–0.00487）。次要结果依赖所移除的操作、特征位置和评分视野，冻结阈值在更长视野下也明显漂移。实际执行所选查询在长序列上带来较小延迟下降但 NLL 上升，而学习型路由器在缓存续写中仍更慢。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了这些轨迹摘要特征的增量价值有限，并将分配质量与实测推理收益区分开来，说明预测注意力价值并不必然转化为实际推理效率提升。

Abstract: Attention routing requires a signal that predicts the value of attention on the current prefix. We evaluate whether hidden-state extrapolation error, curvature and error change improve this prediction beyond uncertainty, one-step displacement, position and state projections. Paired executions of the final attention layer supply signed next-token loss differences in frozen SmolLM3-3B-Base and Qwen3.5-4B-Base checkpoints. Utility-supervised routers are tested on 100 held-out PG-19 books at an identical causal 20 percent invocation quota. None of six prespecified comparisons shows a positive gain after familywise correction. In Qwen3.5, a parameter-matched fixed-projection control lowers NLL by 0.00356 nats/token relative to the trajectory router (95 percent interval 0.00218 to 0.00487). Secondary results depend on the operation removed, feature location and scoring horizon; frozen thresholds also drift substantially at longer horizons. Actual selected-query execution yields small long-sequence latency reductions with increased NLL, while learned routers remain slower during cached continuation. The study identifies limits on the incremental value of these trajectory summaries and separates allocation quality from measured inference benefit.

</details>


### [79] [Twist Flow for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09281)
*Shiqin Zeng,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 joint twist-flow，一种增广流匹配方法，通过在源态 $(z_x, y)$ 与终态 $(x, z_y)$ 之间学习连续传输，提升贝叶斯逆问题后验采样的多样性与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接条件生成模型在配对逆问题训练下易退化为近似确定映射，导致样本虽满足观测一致性，却低估后验变异性，在多峰后验情形下出现覆盖不足、模态扭曲或虚假跳跃。

Method: 构建增广状态传输：源为 $(z_x, y)$，$z_x$ 为后验采样的高斯参考坐标，$z_y$ 为观测分支对应的高斯似然侧坐标；在高斯观测模型下，$z_y$ 由归一化观测残差驱动，用于将生成样本耦合到观测一致性，而非替代 $x$ 的不确定性；采用 flow-matching 学习该连续传输。

Result: 在低维逆问题中与参考后验样本对比，joint twist-flow 较直接条件流基线更好地保留多峰后验支撑；在图像修复与地震地下速度模型反演中，在维持观测一致性的同时提升后验变异性。

Conclusion: 通过引入观测侧似然坐标进行联合流匹配，可在不牺牲观测一致性的前提下改善后验采样覆盖与多样性。

Abstract: In Bayesian inverse problems, posterior sampling requires generating samples that are consistent with given observations while capturing the range of plausible solutions. Direct conditional generative models introduce latent noise to model this ambiguity, but paired inverse-problem training can still encourage an almost deterministic map from the observation to the target. As a result, generated samples may be observation-consistent while under-representing posterior variability, especially when the posterior is multimodal, leading to undercoverage, mode distortion, or artificial transitions between distinct feasible solutions. We propose joint twist-flow, an augmented flow-matching formulation that learns a continuous transport from the augmented source state $(z_x, y)$ to the augmented terminal state $(x, z_y)$. Here x is the target variable, $y$ is the observation, $z_x$ is the Gaussian reference coordinate for posterior sampling, and $z_y$ is a Gaussian likelihood-side coordinate associated with the observation branch. Under a Gaussian observation model, $z_y$ is motivated by the normalized observation residual associated with observation compatibility. Its role is not to replace uncertainty in $x$, but to couple generated samples of x to observation consistency, helping reduce likelihood-inconsistent variation while preserving variability in weakly constrained directions. We validate the method on low-dimensional inverse problems with reference posterior samples, where joint twist-flow better preserves multimodal posterior support than a direct conditional-flow baseline. We further evaluate the method on image restoration and seismic subsurface velocity-model inversion, showing increased posterior variability while maintaining observation consistency.

</details>


### [80] [Self-attention summary networks for subsurface velocity-model building from common-image gathers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09282)
*Shiqin Zeng,Yunlin Zeng,Abhinav Prakash Gahlot,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D CIG体压缩为紧凑条件嵌入，结合流匹配模型实现概率地下速度反演，提升了后验推断精度并降低了预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规成像流程中，CIG虽通过反射层聚焦和剩余时差蕴含速度模型误差的物理信息，但通常仅用作诊断工具，未被有效用于反演。同时直接以原始CIG作为条件进行速度反演存在维度高、背景速度失配引入变异性等问题。

Method: 提出多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D CIG体映射为紧凑的条件嵌入，保留与偏移相关的运动学结构和空间相干性，同时减少背景速度失配带来的变异性；再以该摘要嵌入为条件，用流匹配模型学习从高斯源分布到合理速度场后验分布的传输映射。

Result: 数值实验表明，与直接以原始CIG为条件相比，所提摘要网络改善了后验速度推断；多尺度注意力设计对背景模型失配具有更强鲁棒性，后验重建更准确，预测不确定性更低。

Conclusion: 将CIG通过多尺度自注意力摘要网络编码为条件嵌入并结合流匹配，可有效提升概率地下速度反演的准确性与鲁棒性，为CIG从诊断工具转向反演条件信息提供了新途径。

Abstract: Common-image gathers (CIGs) contain physically meaningful information about velocity-model errors through reflector focusing and residual moveout, but in conventional imaging workflows they are typically used only as diagnostic tools. In this work, we propose a multiscale self-attention summary network that maps high-dimensional 3D CIG volumes into compact conditioning embeddings for probabilistic subsurface velocity inversion. These learned embeddings preserve offset-dependent kinematic structure and spatial coherence while reducing variability caused by background-velocity mismatch. Conditioned on these summary embeddings, a flow-matching model learns a transport from a Gaussian source distribution to the posterior distribution of plausible velocity fields. Numerical experiments show that, compared with direct conditioning on raw CIGs, the proposed summary network improves posterior velocity inference. In particular, the multiscale attention design provides greater robustness to background-model mismatch, yielding more accurate posterior reconstructions and lower predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [81] [LeCuration: A Tiny World Model as a Data Curation Multi-Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09285)
*Mayank Sengupta,Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以数据集自身物理规律为中心的物理AI数据整理思路，训练小型世界模型LeCuration（基于LeWM潜编码器+预测器并加DiT解码器），在CS:GO数据上做定性概念验证，用于异常检测、内容聚类和动作-状态一致性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI应用多运行于有限或封闭的物理世界（如仓库机器人、电子游戏智能体），其数据受特定物理规律约束。为更好地组织、筛选与整理这类数据，需要一种围绕单个数据集独特设定与物理规律的新方法。

Method: 选择LeWorldModel（LeWM）作为潜空间编码器与预测器，并额外加入扩散Transformer（DiT）解码器，以在自回归游戏 rollout 中生成视觉画面，从而训练出小型世界模型LeCuration，作为服务于独立、更大下游模型的数据整理工具。

Result: 该模型的嵌入可作为异常检测信号和基于内容的聚类启发式；用该模型自回归预测游戏状态，可对动作-状态一致性进行定性检查。论文为基于CS:GO游戏数据的定性概念验证案例研究，尚未报告定量整理指标或下游训练结果。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了以小型世界模型进行数据整理的可行性思路，下一关键步骤是补充定量整理指标与下游训练效果评估。

Abstract: Many applications of physical AI run within finite or closed physical worlds with a limited set of physical laws governing object behavior. Examples include robots working in a warehouse and agents moving around in a video game. In order to better organize, filter, and curate data for physical AI applications, we propose a new approach centered on the unique settings and physical laws of individual datasets. We train LeCuration, a small world model intended to serve as a data curation tool for a separate, larger downstream model. To build this model, we choose LeWorldModel (LeWM)as our latent encoder and predictor, adding a diffusion transformer (DiT) decoder to add visuals to autoregressive gameplay rollout. We find that the embeddings of this model can be used as an anomaly detection signal and as a content-based clustering heuristic, and that auto-regressively predicting the game state with this model allows us to qualitatively check for action-state consistency. This paper presents a qualitative, proof-of-concept case study on CS:GO gameplay data; we do not yet report quantitative curation metrics or downstream training results, which we identify as the key next step.

</details>


### [82] [Node-level Graph Neural Architecture Search Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09297)
*Lintao Yanga,Sirui Lia,Yaqing Wang,Pietro Liò,Xu Shen,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点级图神经网络架构搜索算法N-GNAS，能够为每个节点子集自动选择合适网络架构，并引入对比学习损失，在八个数据集的节点与图分类任务上超越现有GNAS方法与人工设计GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN方法对所有节点施加统一的卷积操作，忽视节点在结构与特征上的差异性，导致模型性能下降，且随层数增加易出现过度平滑问题。

Method: 提出N-GNAS算法，在更新节点特征时为每个节点子集自动选择合适的网络架构；同时引入对比学习损失，使不同类别的样本特征相互分离，同类特征相互靠近。

Result: 在八个数据集的节点分类与图分类任务上，该方法优于当前主流GNAS技术与传统人工设计的GNN，例如在CiteSeer数据集上取得78.26%的准确率。

Conclusion: 节点级架构搜索结合对比学习能有效缓解统一卷积带来的性能损失与过度平滑问题，验证了为不同节点子集定制架构的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and architecture search frameworks have gained extensive application in non-Euclidean data processing, attributable to their superior capacity in managing unstructured data. Nevertheless, traditional approaches typically apply uniform convolution operations to all nodes, regardless of their varying structural and feature characteristics, which can undermine model performance and result in over-smoothing issues as the number of layers increases. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose a \textbf{N}ode-Level \textbf{G}raph \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}rchitecture \textbf{S}earch (N-GNAS) algorithm.
