Abstract: Multiphase flow in porous microstructures is central to CO$_2$ storage, fuel-cell operation, and flip-chip packaging. Predicting these flows remains challenging because wettability and complex pore geometry govern the nonlinear evolution of fluid interfaces. Machine learning holds substantial promise for advancing the field, but progress is constrained by scarce time-resolved 3D datasets and a lack of a unified workflow for training and evaluating models. To fill this critical gap, we introduce PoreML, an open-source framework unifying data generation, model training, and evaluation grounded in pore-scale physics. The framework comprises three core components. (a) A modern GPU-native lattice Boltzmann solver, validated against analytical solutions and published experiments, enables reproducible data generation. (b) A 3.3 TB dataset contains 560 simulation runs and 158,546 stored time steps across four application-driven scenarios. These trajectories span synthetic structures and geometries derived from micro-CT scans of real materials, covering diverse wetting conditions and viscosity ratios. (c) A unified learning framework evaluates one-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts. Its domain-specific evaluation protocols assess predictive accuracy and physical consistency. We evaluate five models of diverse architecture under these protocols. Two complementary challenges assess transfer to larger domains and from synthetic to micro-CT-derived structures. PoreML provides a shared foundation for machine-learning research on multiphase flow in porous media, with the aim of empowering the community to develop reliable predictive models and advance the field.

</details>


### [190] [Thinking in Depth: Retrospective Inference for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10317)
*Hao-Run Cai,Si-Yang Liu,Zi-Jian Cheng,Kun-Yang Yu,Jin-Hao Sheng,Guo Yu,Chonghan Liu,Zhi Zhou,Jun-Peng Jiang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Retro 表格基础模型，通过回顾式推理直接复用中间层表示，在多个基准上进入前三并位于 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFM）依赖堆叠 Transformer 层进行上下文预测，但预测精炼在深度上分布极不均衡，往往集中在靠后的层，导致深度利用不充分。作者希望重新审视中间表示的构建与复用方式。

Method: 提出 Retro，一种基于回顾式推理的表格基础模型，让后层可以显式回访并重组早期中间信息。包含两个互补操作：Attention Residuals 自适应重新加权不同深度的贡献（决定回访哪些信息）；query-conditioned Gated Attention 对注意力输出按表示维度逐元素调制（决定如何为每个查询塑形上下文更新）。

Result: 分析显示 Retro 将预测精炼提前并更广泛地分布于各深度，不同阶段修正不同查询子集，呈现多视角精炼模式。在 TabArena、TALENT、RelArena 上排名进入前三，并位于 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: 直接复用中间表示是更充分利用 TFM 深度的实用途径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are pretrained across diverse tabular tasks and make predictions on a new table at inference time using its labeled examples as context. Most recent TFMs perform such in-context prediction with stacked Transformer layers, repeatedly transforming how examples are represented and compared. By tracing individual queries through several strong TFMs, we find that predictive refinement is highly uneven across depth and is often concentrated in later layers. This uneven refinement motivates us to reconsider how intermediate representations are constructed and reused throughout the network. We introduce Retro, a tabular foundation model based on retrospective inference, where later stages can explicitly revisit and recombine intermediate information produced earlier in the network. Retro organizes this process around two complementary operations: which intermediate information to revisit, and how the resulting contextual update should be shaped for each query. Attention Residuals address the former by adaptively reweighting contributions from different depths, while query-conditioned Gated Attention addresses the latter by modulating the attention output element-wise across representation dimensions. Our analysis shows that Retro shifts predictive refinement earlier and more broadly across depth, with different stages revising different subsets of queries in a pattern suggestive of multi-view refinement. Across TabArena, TALENT, and RelArena, Retro ranks among the top three and lies on the Pareto frontier. These results indicate that directly reusing intermediate representations provides a practical way to better exploit depth in TFMs.

</details>


### [191] [HAN-Mamba: Hierarchical Selective State Space Networks for Multi-Scale Financial Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10323)
*Mihai Bogdan Deaconu,Ioan Daniel Pop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出HAN-Mamba，用选择性状态空间（Mamba）编码器替换HAN-T中的二次注意力编码器，仅保留注意力融合器，在Optiver已实现波动率预测基准上以更少参数取得更低预测误差，并支持更长上下文与流式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期已实现波动率预测需要整合从秒级订单簿动态到周级状态漂移等不同时间分辨率的市场信息，跨尺度整合存在挑战。会议工作中的HAN-T使用Transformer编码器，但二次注意力计算成本高，难以扩展到更长高频上下文。

Method: HAN-Mamba采用分层结构：短、中、长三个尺度的流分别由选择性状态空间（Mamba）编码器处理，通过递归状态利用输入相关门控来刻画波动率的两个结构特性——持久但衰减的记忆和突变的 regime shift；跨尺度整合仍由学习到的注意力融合器完成，但输入只需三个token而非长序列。

Result: 在Optiver已实现波动率预测基准上的时间感知五折交叉验证中，HAN-Mamba平均RMSPE由HAN-T的0.1965降至0.1942，参数减少33%；线性时间编码器使高频上下文从60个bucket扩展到240个bucket，误差进一步降至0.1927，而注意力变体在此处已饱和；同时支持推理时常数时间的流式更新。消融实验表明增益来自编码器替换，确认分层先验可跨序列模型族迁移，且置换不变的注意力融合器仍是跨尺度整合的正确机制。

Conclusion: 用Mamba编码器替换注意力编码器、保留注意力融合器，可在更少参数下提升短期波动率预测精度，并带来更长上下文与流式推理优势；分层先验具有跨模型族的通用性。

Abstract: Short-horizon realized volatility forecasting requires the integration of market information that evolves at incompatible temporal resolutions, from second-level order book dynamics to weekly regime drift. Our conference work introduced HAN-T, a hierarchical architecture in which scale-specific Transformer encoders process short, mid, and long-horizon streams and a learned attention fuser weighs their contributions. This article replaces the quadratic attention encoders with selective state space (Mamba) encoders while retaining attention only in the fuser, where the input is a three-token set rather than a long sequence. The resulting hybrid, HAN-Mamba, summarizes each stream through a recurrent state whose input-dependent gating matches two structural properties of volatility: persistent but decaying memory and abrupt regime shifts. On the Optiver Realized Volatility Prediction benchmark under time-aware five-fold cross-validation, HAN-Mamba improves mean RMSPE over HAN-T (0.1942 vs. 0.1965) with 33% fewer parameters. Its linear-time encoders further allow the high-frequency context to be extended from 60 to 240 buckets, reducing error to 0.1927 where the attention variant saturates, and support constant-time streaming updates at inference. Ablations attribute the gains to the encoder swap, confirm that the hierarchical prior transfers across sequence-model families, and show that the permutation-invariant attention fuser remains the correct mechanism for cross-scale integration.

</details>


### [192] [Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Multichain MDPs: A Hierarchical Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10326)
*Huizhen Yu,Isaiah Heidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个本质无模型的平均奖励多链MDP强化学习算法，基于Bather分解将状态空间分层为通信子系统与瞬态状态，实现三个算法变体并给出几乎必然收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励多链MDP中，最优增益可能依赖初始状态，且不同策略下的递归结构各异，这给强化学习带来挑战；已有方法多依赖模型知识或归约为折扣问题，缺乏通用的无模型算法。

Method: 提出基于异步值迭代的RL算法，仅需MDP转移图信息，利用Bather分解将状态空间分层划分为通信子系统与瞬态状态，把全局决策问题重构为结构化子问题。在此基础上发展两个扩展算法：一个近似求解多链平均最优方程以获得近增益最优策略，另一个通过逼近最优偏置函数并求解诱导的平均奖励多链MDP来实现近偏置最优。

Result: 证明了算法收敛到最优增益并在有限时间内产生增益最优策略；三个算法均给出几乎必然收敛保证；实验表明后两个算法相较基础算法能持续改善瞬态性能，并展示其权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个面向一般多链MDP、不归约为折扣问题的本质无模型平均奖励RL算法，为多链场景提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: We study learning optimal policies in average-reward multichain Markov decision processes (MDPs), where the optimal gain may depend on the initial state and recurrence structures vary across policies, creating challenges for reinforcement learning (RL) methods. We propose an asynchronous value-iteration-based RL algorithm that requires no model knowledge beyond the MDP's transition graph and leverages Bather's decomposition to hierarchically partition the state space into communicating subsystems and transient states. This decomposition induces a recasting of the global decision problem into structured subproblems, which our algorithm exploits. We show that the algorithm converges to the optimal gain and produces gain-optimal policies after finite time. Building on this base algorithm, we develop two further algorithms: one approximately solves the multichain average optimality equations to obtain near gain-optimal policies, and another targets near bias-optimality by approximating the optimal bias function and solving an induced average-reward multichain MDP using the base algorithm. We provide almost-sure convergence guarantees for all three algorithms and empirically compare their tradeoffs, showing that the latter two also consistently improve transient performance relative to the base algorithm. To our knowledge, these are the first essentially model-free average-reward RL algorithms for general multichain MDPs without reductions to discounted problems.

</details>


### [193] [AutoAdapt: Automatic Domain Discovery Enables Low-Cost Extensibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10349)
*Josh McGiff,Salma Mekaoui,Robert Shanahan,Nikola S. Nikolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoAdapt是一个模块化框架，通过自动发现潜在领域并独立训练各领域LoRA适配器，无需全模型重训练即可引入新领域，性能与全领域训练的单一适配器相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型部署环境中的领域异构且不断演化，新增领域或数据通常需要昂贵的重训练，因此需要一种能低成本引入新领域而不损失性能的方法。

Method: 提出AutoAdapt框架：自动发现潜在领域；基于发现结果，并行独立训练每个领域的LoRA适配器；采用无参数路由进行推理。

Result: 在14个领域基准及GPT-4o成对评估中，AutoAdapt达到与在全部领域上训练的单个LoRA适配器相当的性能，且无需全模型重训练；同时发现不同独立发现方法间存在专业化效应收敛的证据。

Conclusion: 让每个适配器仅训练自身领域数据可从结构上避免领域干扰，实现模块化、无需分类体系的领域专业化，既不损失总体性能也无需全模型重训练。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models are deployed into environments where domains are heterogeneous and evolve, yet adding new domains or data typically requires costly retraining. We present AutoAdapt, a modular framework that incorporates new domains and data via targeted single-adapter training without modifying other adapters. The framework automatically discovers latent domains, uses them to train per-domain Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters independently in parallel and performs parameter-free routing. Across 14 domain-specific benchmarks and GPT-4o pairwise judgements, AutoAdapt achieves parity with a LoRA adapter trained on all domains without requiring full-model retraining. We also find evidence of specialisation effect convergence across independent discovery methods. Overall, training each adapter on its own domain prevents domain interference by construction, thus enabling modular, taxonomy-free domain specialisation without aggregate performance loss or full model retraining.

</details>


### [194] [ORDERS: An Empirical Study of Norm-Rank Aggregation for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10361)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对ORDERS个性化联邦学习配置进行了严格、完全指定的实证评估，发现其服务器加权规则（按更新范数降序分配几何权重）所带来的收益在控制本地训练与评估选择后十分有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习通常将共享表示与客户端专属预测器结合，但服务器加权规则的真实贡献容易被本地训练和评估方式的选择所掩盖。作者希望厘清ORDERS中"按更新范数降序分配几何权重"这一服务器规则是否真正带来越优化收益，还是仅由实现细节造成假象。

Method: 研究ORDERS配置：共享主干网络、私有残差适配器与分类器、按更新范数降序分配的几何权重、特征对齐以及私有参数扰动。服务器对来自同一广播模型的更新做加权求和，并不因顺序相加产生额外优化效果。评估包含8种配置、2个数据集、5个随机种子、每个数据集一个固定划分，共80次最终运行，并设置匹配的均匀权重对照与FedPer-R1对比，另做消融实验。

Result: 在二分类每客户端的CIFAR-10上，ORDERS取得80.51±0.79%的原生平均客户端准确率，FedPer-R1为79.02±1.42%，匹配的均匀权重对照为80.27±0.73%。经通用本地微调后，与FedPer-R1的差距缩小至0.32个百分点。在Sent140上，ORDERS为74.71±0.49%，仅比事后客户端训练多数诊断高0.69个百分点。消融仅提供有限且依赖端点的范数排序与对齐证据，扰动无明确收益。参数负载节省分别为5.47%和0.78%。

Conclusion: ORDERS中服务器加权规则的贡献被本地训练与评估选择所掩盖，其增益相对于匹配对照（均匀权重）和简单诊断十分有限。范数排序、对齐的证据薄弱且依赖端点，扰动无明显收益，参数节省也很小，说明该配置的独特性价值尚未得到有力证明。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning combines shared representations with client-specific predictors, but the contribution of a server weighting rule can be obscured by local training and evaluation choices. We study ORDERS, a configuration that combines a shared backbone, a private residual adapter and classifier, geometric weights assigned by descending update norm, feature alignment, and private-parameter perturbations. The server computes a weighted sum of updates obtained from the same broadcast model; it does not obtain an additional optimization effect from sequential addition. A fully specified evaluation comprises 80 final runs: eight configurations, two datasets, and five training seeds on one fixed partition per dataset. On two-class-per-client CIFAR-10, ORDERS achieves $80.51 \pm 0.79\%$ native mean client accuracy, compared with $79.02 \pm 1.42\%$ for FedPer-R1 and $80.27 \pm 0.73\%$ for the matched uniform-weight control. After common local fine-tuning, the difference from FedPer-R1 narrows to 0.32 percentage points. On Sent140, ORDERS reaches $74.71 \pm 0.49\%$, only 0.69 points above a post hoc client training-majority diagnostic. Ablations provide limited, endpoint-dependent evidence for norm ranking and alignment, and no clear benefit from perturbations. Parameter-payload savings are 5.47% and 0.78%, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [Koopman Observers for Diffusion Acceleration: Correcting Feature Forecasts with Shallow Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10366)
*Hanru Bai,Yuanchao Xu,Fengyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种观测校正的Koopman框架，利用新鲜计算浅层特征作为观测来校正深层特征预测，从而加速冻结扩散模型的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征缓存方法仅依赖过去特征进行预测，无法纳入当前去噪状态的变化，因此预测精度受限。作者希望探究廉价的新鲜计算特征能否作为观测，用于校正这些预测。

Method: 使用校准轨迹识别有限维、时变的Koopman近似，联合描述浅层与深层网络特征的增量。加速采样时用该算子预测昂贵的深层特征演化，同时用观测到的浅层特征的新息来校正预测状态；周期性全量评估刷新观测器，生成模型参数保持冻结不变。

Result: 每个数据集三次1万张图像运行中，相比同四步部分步调度下的通道仿射预测，配对Inception特征MSE在CIFAR-10上降低19.9%，在十类ImageNet子集上降低11.9%；匹配消融显示观测校正额外带来4.54%和4.67%的降低。观测器相对DDIM-50实现1.89倍和1.85倍的实测加速。

Conclusion: 该框架可在不重新训练去噪器的前提下提升参考采样器的保真度，并支持对时间预测与观测校正进行受控比较。

Abstract: Feature caching accelerates diffusion sampling by replacing expensive network evaluations with predictions from previously computed activations. However, forecasts based only on past features cannot directly incorporate changes in the current denoising state. We investigate whether inexpensive, freshly computed features can serve as observations for correcting these predictions. We introduce an observation-corrected Koopman framework for accelerating frozen diffusion models. Using calibration trajectories, we identify finite-dimensional, time-dependent Koopman approximations that jointly describe the increments of shallow and deep network features. During accelerated sampling, these operators predict the evolution of expensive deep features, while innovations in the observed shallow features correct the predicted state. Periodic full evaluations refresh the observer, and all generative-model parameters remain unchanged. This formulation enables controlled comparisons of temporal prediction and observation correction. Across three 10,000-image runs per dataset, our method reduces paired Inception-feature MSE by $19.9\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $11.9\%$ on a ten-class ImageNet subset relative to channelwise affine prediction under the same four-partial-step schedule. Matched ablations attribute additional reductions of $4.54\%$ and $4.67\%$ to observation correction. The observer achieves $1.89\times$ and $1.85\times$ measured speedups over DDIM-50, supporting improved reference-sampler fidelity without retraining the denoiser.

</details>


### [196] [Temporally Interpretable Differentiable Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10367)
*Eisuke Hirota,Aarav Sane,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时间可解释性"这一新维度，通过动作分块（action chunking）让可微决策树更适合序贯决策任务，并给出两种策略梯度算法和信息论树重构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性有助于实现安全自主。可微决策树（DDT）能在保持自动可微策略的同时提供离散树状可视化。但现有DDT不适合序贯决策领域，因为树的单时间步行为与人类的多时间步规划存在固有错配。

Method: 引入时间作为可解释性的新维度（时间可解释性），通过动作分块实现时间抽象；提出两种结合动作分块的新策略梯度算法；并开发一种信息论树重构算法，在训练中修改树以保持参数高效。

Result: 在四个仿真环境中，从蒸馏的动作分块策略热启动动作分块DDT是获得时间可解释树的最有效方式：在四个领域中的三个里匹配神经网络策略表现，同时参数最多减少80%。

Conclusion: 通过动作分块赋予DDT时间可解释性，可在保持竞争力性能的同时显著降低参数量，代码已开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: Interpretability offers a solution to safe autonomy by providing transparency into an agent's underlying decision-making model. Within sequential-decision making tasks, differentiable decision trees (DDTs) are one approach to such interpretability, maintaining automatic-differentiable policies while providing humans with a discrete tree-based visualization. Nonetheless, current implementations of DDTs are not well-suited for sequential-decision making domains, as there exists an inherent mismatch between a tree's single-timestep behavior and a human's multi-timestep planning. Our work thus introduces time as a new dimension of interpretability, coined as temporal interpretability, and demonstrates how temporal abstractions via action chunking improve it. We achieve this by first introducing two novel policy gradient algorithms that incorporate action chunking. Additionally, to maintain parameter-efficient trees, we develop an information-theoretic tree restructuring algorithm that modifies the tree during training. Across four simulation environments, we find that warm-starting action chunked DDTs from a distilled action chunked policy is the most effective way to obtain temporally interpretable trees: they match neural network policies in three of the four domains while using up to 80$\%$ fewer parameters. Our code is available at https://github.com/ei5uke/temp-interp.

</details>


### [197] [Continual Learning without Continual Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10379)
*Nikita Narayanan,Ritham Majumdarr,Sonali Parbhoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出以"持续推理"替代"持续训练"：基于PFN的模型经元训练后冻结，仅通过扩展上下文证据集来适应新类/新域，在隐概念空间做贝叶斯推理，参数不变以减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖持续优化，需借助正则化、回放或参数扩展来防止新更新覆盖旧知识，容易产生遗忘且流程复杂。作者希望找到一种不改变参数、同时适用于域增量与类增量（且无需任务身份）的持续学习范式。

Method: 提出Latent Concept PFN：PFN-based模型先元训练再冻结；在捕捉跨域/跨类语义结构的隐概念空间上进行上下文贝叶斯推理。新域或新类到来时，只需将样例加入记忆，适应体现为对隐概念后验信念的更新而非梯度更新。概念标注仅在元训练时使用，作为隐空间的软锚点而非固定瓶颈；通过结合概念标签与原始输入证据，可处理噪声、歧义或不完整标注，并发现预定义概念集之外的区分。

Result: 在类增量与域增量持续学习数据集上的实验表明，该方法取得了有竞争力的持续学习性能，同时学到了可解释的隐概念。

Conclusion: 持续推理是一种可行的持续学习替代方案：冻结模型、仅扩展上下文证据即可适应新类/新域，天然减少遗忘，并统一处理域与类增量场景，兼具性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to adapt to new domains and new classes while retaining prior knowledge. Many existing methods rely on continued optimization, using regularization, replay, or parameter expansion to prevent new updates from overwriting previously learned knowledge. Instead, we propose replacing continual training with continual inference: a PFN-based model that is meta-trained, and then frozen, adapting to new classes only by extending an in-context evidence set. Our model, Latent Concept PFN, performs in-context Bayesian inference over a latent concept space that captures semantic structure shared across domains and classes. As each new domain or class arrives, exemplars are added to the memory; adaptation reflects updated posterior beliefs over latent concepts rather than gradient updates. No parameters are changed, reducing forgetting. The same method handles both domain and class incremental continual learning without task identity. Concept annotations are only used during meta-training, acting as a soft anchor on the latent space rather than a fixed bottleneck. Unlike fixed-vocabulary concept methods, the model also handles noisy, ambiguous, or incomplete annotations by combining concept labels with raw input evidence to discover distinctions beyond the predefined concept set. Experiments on class and domain incremental learning datasets demonstrate competitive continual learning performance while learning interpretable latent concepts.

</details>


### [198] [ResidualQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers with 2-Bit Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10381)
*Heejun Kim,Junyoung Lee,SangLyul Cho,Dongsu Han,Insu Han,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ResidualQuant：利用循环Transformer中各循环KV状态高度相似的特点，以末循环KV为参考、用低位残差表示其余循环，实现INT2级别KV缓存量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过复用共享块提升参数效率，但KV缓存随循环数增长，成为限制批大小与吞吐的内存瓶颈；现有KV量化在激进低精度下精度损失大。

Method: 观察到跨循环KV状态高度相似，故以最终循环KV状态为参考，其余循环用低精度残差表示；并结合最小二乘缩放、残差旋转以及逐循环混合精度，实现到INT2的精确量化与高效重建。

Result: 在多个循环Transformer及数学推理、代码生成基准上，精度-内存权衡优于基于旋转的SOTA KV量化。混合精度下精度接近BF16，理论KV存储降低80.7%，同内存预算下精度最高提升13.0%；RTX 5090上固定批解码吞吐最高提升2.73倍，批大小最高增大2倍，峰值吞吐最高提升4.15倍。

Conclusion: ResidualQuant为循环Transformer提供高效的KV缓存量化方案，在显著降低内存的同时保持精度并大幅提升推理吞吐。

Abstract: Looped Transformers improve parameter efficiency by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks over multiple recurrent loops, increasing computational depth without increasing the parameter count. However, KV cache memory still scales with the number of loops, becoming a key memory bottleneck that limits batch size and inference throughput. KV cache quantization can alleviate this bottleneck, but existing methods often suffer substantial accuracy degradation at aggressive low-precision regimes. We observe that looped Transformers offer a unique opportunity: KV states across loops are highly similar. Based on this observation, we propose ResidualQuant, which uses the final-loop KV states as a reference and represents the remaining loops with low-precision residuals. Our method further combines least-square scaling and rotations applied to the residuals, as well as loop-wise mixed precision, to enable accurate quantization down to INT2 while retaining efficient reconstruction. Across multiple looped Transformer models and mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, ResidualQuant consistently improves the accuracy-memory tradeoff over state-of-the-art rotation-based KV quantization. In particular, our method retains accuracy close to BF16 under mixed-precision settings while reducing theoretical KV storage by 80.7%, achieving up to 13.0% higher accuracy than the rotation-based baseline at the same memory budget. On an RTX 5090, the reduced KV memory traffic improves fixed-batch decode throughput by up to 2.73x, while the smaller memory footprint enables up to 2x larger batches, improving peak throughput by up to 4.15x.

</details>


### [199] [Boosting and the Expressive Power of Simple Weak Learners via the $γ$-VC Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10383)
*Arthur da Cunha,Kasper Green Larsen,Liang-Yu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过γ-VC维度研究Boosting的弱到强学习，证明该参数刻画了样本复杂度，细化了VC维度与γ-VC维度的关系，并给出决策树桩和轴平行矩形的改进上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boosting能将略优于随机的弱假设转化为高精度预测器，但所得分类器的表达能力强烈依赖于基类的结构，作者希望从理论上刻画这一现象。

Method: 采用Alon等人（STOC 2021）提出的γ-VC维度作为分析工具，研究弱到强学习的样本复杂度，并分析经典VC维度与γ-VC维度的一般关系。

Result: 证明γ-VC维度在γ的常数因子范围内刻画了弱到强学习的样本复杂度；细化了VC维度与γ-VC维度的一般关系；对决策树桩和R^d中轴平行矩形这两类基本概念类给出了改进的γ-VC维度上下界。

Conclusion: γ-VC维度是刻画Boosting弱到强学习复杂度的关键参数，且对基本概念类的界可进一步改进。

Abstract: Boosting converts weak hypotheses with a small edge over random guessing into highly accurate predictors, but the expressive power of the resulting classifier can depend strongly on the structure of the base class. We study this phenomenon through the $γ$-VC dimension introduced by Alon et al. (STOC 2021). Our first result shows that this parameter characterizes the sample complexity for weak-to-strong learning up to a constant factor scaling in $γ$. We then sharpen the general relationship between the classic VC dimension and the $γ$-VC dimension. Finally, we also give improved upper and lower bounds on the $γ$-VC dimension for the fundamental concept classes of decision stumps and axis-parallel rectangles in $\mathbb{R}^d$.

</details>


### [200] [OrBIT: Structure-Guided Embedding Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10385)
*Yunied Puig,Amit Kumar Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrBIT提出一种结构引导的嵌入表压缩框架，通过学习轨道动力学中的可复用局部几何来约束共享码字，并结合全局重构残差指导容量分配，实现高倍率压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型的嵌入表是最大的组成部分之一，而现有压缩方法大多固定编码几何（如坐标块、低秩子空间或无限制码本）并在其内优化，缺乏对编码几何本身的探索。本文旨在研究编码几何是否可以被自动发现。

Method: OrBIT从轨道动力学中学习可复用的局部几何，并用其约束一小组共享码字；全局重构残差决定固定编码预算的分配位置，冗余重叠图卡使局部误差在拼接后相互补偿。理论上分析了紧图卡几何对失真的控制、全局残差对顺序分配的指导，以及数据几何引导的细化对编解码器的改进。最终轨道机制被编译移除，留下紧凑解码器。

Result: 在四个LLM嵌入表上，OrBIT相对16位存储实现了GPT-2上37.9倍压缩，以及每个7B表超过23倍的压缩，同时在率失真性能上与已有量化和低秩基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 编码几何本身可被学习发现，学习到的结构能决定存储内容、容量分配位置以及局部信息如何全局组装，从而实现高效嵌入压缩。

Abstract: Embedding tables are among the largest components of modern language models. Most compression methods fix a coding geometry such as coordinate blocks, low-rank subspaces, or unrestricted codebooks, and optimize within it. We instead ask whether the coding geometry can itself be discovered. We introduce \emph{OrBIT}, a structure-guided embedding compression framework that learns reusable local geometry from orbit dynamics and uses it to constrain a small set of shared codewords. The global reconstruction residual then decides where the fixed coding budget is spent, while redundant overlapping charts let local errors compensate one another after gluing. Our theory shows how tight-chart geometry controls distortion, how the global residual directs sequential allocation, and how data-geometry-guided refinement improves the codec. The resulting orbit machinery is compiled away, leaving a compact decoder in which the learned structure governs what is stored, where capacity is allocated, and how local information is assembled globally. Across four LLM embedding tables, OrBIT achieves $37.9\times$ compression on GPT-2 and over $23\times$ on each 7B table relative to 16-bit storage, while delivering competitive rate-distortion performance against established quantization and low-rank baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Kernel Autoresearch for Open-Ended Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10394)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin,Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Kernaut，将核函数设计视为开放式模型发现，通过编码智能体编写核程序、构造契约保证有效性、质量-多样性存档与新颖性筛选，发现的核具有可迁移的归纳偏置且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核函数编码了机器学习模型的归纳偏置，但自动化核设计面临两难：固定语法保证有效性却限制搜索空间；无限制程序虽打破限制却无法保证有效性。压力测试显示 22-58% 通过随机输入数值检查的 LLM 生成核在不同尺度或维度下会失效。

Method: Kernaut 将核设计视为开放式模型发现：编码智能体将核编写为程序，构造契约（construction contracts）确保每个被接受的核都有效；质量-多样性存档保留行为各异的高性能核，新颖性筛选引导智能体探索功能上全新的候选。

Result: 在留出的黑盒优化族上，发现的核优于在同一批 episode 上元学习的深度核；由十个酶动力学速率方程发现的核在五个未见机制上误差低于调优的 ARD 和深度核基线；人类研究者精炼后的核进一步将留出预测误差降低 5.7%、优化遗憾降低 7.8%。

Conclusion: 发现的核编码了可复用的归纳偏置并能泛化到未见任务，同时作为可解释程序可被人类研究者精炼，验证了开放式核发现范式的有效性。

Abstract: Kernels encode the inductive bias of a wide range of machine learning models, yet automated kernel design faces a fundamental dilemma. A fixed grammar of base kernels and operators guarantees validity but limits the search to structures expressible by those building blocks. Conversely, unrestricted programs remove this limitation but no longer guarantee validity. In our stress tests, 22-58% of LLM-generated kernels that pass numerical checks on random inputs fail when evaluated at different scales or dimensions. We propose Kernel Autoresearch (Kernaut), which treats kernel design as open-ended model discovery. Coding agents write kernels as programs, while construction contracts ensure that every accepted kernel is valid. A quality-diversity archive retains high-performing kernels with distinct behaviors, and novelty screening steers agents toward functionally new candidates. Our experiments demonstrate that the discovered kernels encode reusable inductive biases that generalize to unseen tasks. On held-out black-box optimization families, a discovered kernel outperforms a meta-learned deep kernel trained on the same episodes. Furthermore, kernels discovered from ten enzyme-kinetic rate laws achieve lower error than tuned ARD and deep kernel baselines on five unseen mechanisms. The discovered kernels are also interpretable programs that human researchers can refine: a human-refined version of one further reduces the held-out predictive error by 5.7% and optimization regret by 7.8%.

</details>


### [202] [Executing Causal Structure Learning with Linear-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10395)
*Amartya Roy,Sayar Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer能否精确执行因果发现算法，构造了固定权重Transformer以逐次复现连续优化方法的单步更新，并分析其精度与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明Transformer能在输入数据上执行算法，作者追问其是否也能用于因果发现，即能否精确执行标准连续因果发现方法的优化过程，并区分"算法执行准确"与"因果恢复准确"。

Method: 针对一种反复更新候选因果图并强制无环性的标准连续方法，显式构造固定权重Transformer，使其前向传播精确复现该方法的一次更新，重复堆叠即可复现优化轨迹；Transformer在更新间携带当前图与算法的乘子。作者证明保留乘子对精确执行是必要的，并给出在固定阶段内达到目标精度所需更新次数可预先计算及舍入误差有界的条件。

Result: 实验显示构造的模块与参考更新在浮点精度内一致；在合成数据及七个已发表基准网络拓扑上的算术重放继承参考求解器的成功与失败。相比之下，在给定训练预算下测试的普通注意力模型无法可靠执行该更新，也难以迁移到更大图。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对因果发现算法的精确执行，并将算法执行的准确性与因果恢复的准确性区分开来；梯度训练能否在该构造所属架构类中学习出执行器仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Transformers can execute algorithms on data given in their input. We ask whether they can do the same for causal discovery. We study a standard continuous method that repeatedly updates a candidate causal graph while enforcing acyclicity. We explicitly construct a fixed-weight transformer whose forward pass exactly reproduces one update of this method, so repeated blocks reproduce its optimization trajectory. The transformer carries the current graph and the algorithm's multiplier between updates. We show that retaining the multiplier is essential for exact execution, since different multiplier values can lead to different next updates. We also give conditions under which, within a fixed stage, the number of updates needed to reach a target accuracy can be computed in advance and rounding errors stay bounded as depth grows. Experiments show that the constructed block agrees with a reference update to floating-point precision, while arithmetic replay on synthetic data and seven published benchmark network topologies inherits the reference solver's successes and failures. This separates accurate algorithm execution from accurate causal recovery. In contrast, the ordinary attention models tested under our training budgets do not reliably execute the update or transfer to larger graphs. Whether gradient training can learn an executor in the architecture class of the construction remains open.

</details>


### [203] [Cross-Domain Pretraining for Steady-State Neural CFD Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10398)
*Anthony Zhou,Amir Barati Farimani,Shirley Ho,Rudy Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨几何、边界条件和精度训练神经代理模型，发现跨域预训练能显著提升CFD代理模型的零样本和少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD神经代理模型的主要局限是难以泛化到训练集之外的几何和应用场景。当前通常为新应用生成专用数据集，但需要运行昂贵的数值求解器。本文旨在通过跨域预训练缓解这一问题，避免重复付出高昂的CFD数据生成成本。

Method: 在不同几何、边界条件和精度上训练神经代理模型，进行跨域预训练；对比从头训练和从领域专家模型迁移的效果；研究模型规模、预训练数据多样性以及简单池化稳态数据集对性能的影响。

Result: 跨域预训练相比从头训练和领域专家迁移，提升了留出数据集的零样本和少样本性能；微调预训练模型在相同样本量下误差降低2-3倍，达到相同误差所需样本量减少8倍；该收益与架构无关，并随模型规模和数据多样性增加而提升；简单池化稳态数据集即足够且有效。

Conclusion: 鉴于CFD数据生成成本高昂，跨域预训练利用现有数据集是未来代理模型扩展到新问题和新应用的重要策略。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) have the potential to greatly enhance engineering innovation through accelerating simulation. However, the primary limitation for neural surrogates is the lack of generalization to geometries and applications beyond the training set, which is significant given the diversity of engineering scenarios. Currently, this is addressed by generating a new dataset for a specific application; however, this requires running costly numerical solvers. In this work, we take a step toward addressing this by studying neural surrogates trained across different geometries, boundary conditions, and fidelities. We find that cross-domain pretraining improves zero- and few-shot performance on held-out datasets relative to both training from scratch and transferring from domain-specific experts. In particular, finetuning a pretrained, cross-domain model can achieve 2-3x lower errors at the same sample size and use 8x fewer samples to achieve the same error, compared to training from scratch. This benefit is architecture agnostic and improves with model size and pretraining dataset diversity. Furthermore, we study how and why cross-domain pretraining works in CFD surrogates, and find that simply pooling steady-state datasets is both sufficient and effective. Given the high cost of generating CFD data, leveraging existing datasets through cross-domain pretraining will likely be a valuable strategy as future surrogates expand to tackle new problems and use cases.

</details>


### [204] [Training Parallel Speculative Draft Models by Directly Minimizing Expected Decoding Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10411)
*Yunxiao Zhao,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将推测解码训练建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，提出EDR目标函数，无需额外超参数，可直接优化全局解码效率，并在数学推理、代码生成和对话等九个基准上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行与半自回归草稿模型在一次前向中提出整块token，提高起草效率，但其训练存在困难：某位置的草稿分布取决于解码轮从何处开始，而轮次起点又取决于之前轮次接受多少token。现有训练目标依赖块局部代理，忽略跨轮耦合，无法直接优化全局解码效率。

Method: 将推测解码表示为一个马尔可夫奖励过程，提出期望解码轮数（EDR）目标，用状态占用度对局部拒绝代价加权，且恰等于期望解码轮数。进一步推导精确的时序差分梯度，支持从目标模型轨迹中进行无偏随机优化。同一框架还给出精确的离线轮数评估器，可在共享目标轨迹上成对比较草稿器而无需真正运行推测解码。

Result: 用EDR对DSpark和DFly两个先进草稿器进行微调，在涵盖数学推理、代码生成和对话的九个基准上，平均接受长度均持续提升，并优于现有训练目标。

Conclusion: EDR为并行/半自回归草稿器提供了全局解码效率的直接优化目标，无需辅助超参数，并提供离线评估器以支持草稿器比较，实验证明其有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>


### [205] [Which Rollout Taught It That? BehaviorTrace and the Limits of Training-Data Attribution in Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线RL微调（GRPO）中，溯源方法能否真正找到教会模型新行为的训练数据，并提出了BehaviorTrace评测框架，发现在三个随机种子上大部分溯源信号来自混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当强化学习让语言模型习得新行为时，人们希望找到"教会"它的训练轨迹；但溯源方法给出答案时，如何验证其真实性尚缺乏可靠评测。作者希望用一个已知原因的"植入行为"来严格检验现有溯源估计器。

Method: 提出BehaviorTrace开放评测框架，结合全梯度草图（full-gradient sketching）、植入行为设置，并对梯度幅度、流畅度、提升空间（headroom）、随机种子与生成采样等混淆因素设置对照。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上用GRPO对三个种子进行实验，测试GAS（重归一化TracInCP）与TRAK风格估计器，不提出新方法。

Result: 在三个种子上，相当一部分表观溯源信号来自混淆因素。仅按梯度大小（无行为目标）对训练步排序的对照方法达到随机水平的4.2–4.5倍，并在三个种子中的两个上追平或超过最佳有目标估计器。在饱和检查点上，模型流畅度对行为标签的预测能力不逊于所有梯度方法。控制流畅度后，逐轨迹结果随种子和生成采样变化，单次运行无法定论。唯一在三个种子上都稳健的信号是：触发词的梯度与"行为实际发生处"构建的目标对齐。

Conclusion: 作者将这些发现整理为RL中溯源评测的检查清单，强调必须控制梯度幅度、流畅度等混淆因素并跨种子与采样重复验证，单个显著信号不足以下结论。

Abstract: When reinforcement learning teaches a language model a new behavior, can we find the training rollouts that taught it? And when an attribution method says it can, how do we know the answer is real? We study both questions on online RL fine-tuning with GRPO, using a planted behavior with a known cause. We release BehaviorTrace, an open evaluation harness that combines full-gradient sketching, the planted-behavior setup, and controls for gradient magnitude, fluency, headroom, and variation across seeds and generation draws. Across three seeds on Qwen2.5-1.5B, much of the apparent attribution signal comes from confounds. A control that ranks training steps by gradient size alone, with no behavior target, reaches 4.2 to 4.5 times chance and matches or beats the best targeted estimator on two of three seeds. At saturated checkpoints, model fluency predicts the behavior label at least as well as every gradient method we compared it with. Once fluency is controlled, the per-rollout results change from seed to seed and from one generation draw to the next, so a single run cannot settle the question. One signal does hold on all three seeds. The gradient of the trigger tokens aligns with a target built where the behavior actually occurs. We turn these findings into a checklist for evaluating attribution in RL. We test existing estimators, including GAS (renormalized TracInCP) and a TRAK-style estimator, and do not propose a new one.

</details>


### [206] [Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10437)
*Yonghoon Dong,Minsung Yoon,Jaehyuk Kim,Jungwoo Park,Changyeon Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQAM，用标量伴随替代逐步骤向量-雅可比乘积来微调流策略，并在困难基准与真实机器人任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略能表达丰富多样的动作分布，用离策略强化学习进一步超越示范性能受到关注。但流策略动作由多步流生成，针对学习到的价值函数微调并不容易。伴随匹配可从最终动作把价值信息回传到各流步骤，却需每步计算向量-雅可比乘积，代价随流步数和策略规模增长。

Method: 作者观察到预训练流策略的批量平均速度雅可比集中于对角线，据此推导出闭式标量伴随：用流时间缩放最终动作处的价值梯度，从而消除每步向量-雅可比乘积。同时发现标量伴随下控制策略生成动作处的评论家价值尤为重要，于是提出 Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM)，将标量伴随与这些动作上的价值惩罚相结合。

Result: SQAM 的增益集中在四个最难的 OGBench 域，其成功率在每个域上比最强基线高 18 至 35 个百分点。为验证能否扩展到大型预训练策略，作者还在真实双臂机器人上微调视觉-语言-动作策略，SQAM 在三个任务上均优于监督微调。

Conclusion: SQAM 通过标量伴随与价值惩罚，在避免昂贵逐步骤向量-雅可比乘积的同时，有效提升流策略的离策略强化学习微调效果，并在困难基准和真实机器人操作中验证了优势。

Abstract: Flow policies capture rich and diverse action distributions, and fine-tuning them with off-policy RL to improve beyond the demonstrations has drawn growing interest. However, fine-tuning a flow policy against a learned value function is not trivial, because the policy generates its action over many flow steps. Adjoint matching offers a principled way to update the flow model itself by propagating value information from the final action back to each flow step, but it requires a vector--Jacobian product through the policy at every step, a cost that grows with the number of flow steps and the policy size. We observe that the batch-averaged velocity Jacobian of pretrained flow policies concentrates on its diagonal. Motivated by this finding, we derive a closed-form scalar adjoint that scales the value gradient at the final action by the flow time, eliminating the per-step vector--Jacobian products. We further find that controlling the critic's value at policy-generated actions is particularly important under the scalar adjoint. Based on these findings, we propose Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM), which combines the scalar adjoint with a value penalty at those actions. SQAM's gains concentrate on the four hardest OGBench domains, where its success rate exceeds that of the strongest baseline in each domain by 18 to 35 percentage points. To test whether SQAM extends to large pretrained policies, we also fine-tune a vision-language-action policy on a real bimanual robot. SQAM improves over supervised fine-tuning on all three tasks.

</details>


### [207] [Seq-Flow: Efficient Probabilistic Forecasting with Self-Rollout Error Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10440)
*Yinan Huang,Shitij Govil,Bo Dai,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Seq-Flow 是一种条件流模型，其 ODE 将样本从上一时刻的预测分布传输到更新后的分布，从而实现少步、高质量的科学预测更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学预测任务需要随新观测不断更新未来轨迹分布。传统扩散/流模型每次从高斯噪声生成，采样步骤多、成本高；热启动方法虽复用早期预测降低成本，但模型未针对“预测更新”本身训练，在少步采样下质量欠佳。

Method: 提出 Seq-Flow：用条件流模型的 ODE 把上一轮预测分布传输到新分布，因相邻预测差异较小，可少步得到准确更新。为缓解递归复用导致的误差累积，采用 self-rollout 训练，用模型的滑动平均副本生成预测来初始化后续训练更新，并将生成输出作为下一流的起点（区别于 self-forcing 仅作条件上下文）。

Result: 在粒子加速器束流溢出预测中，少 NFE 采样预算下 CRPS 降低 65%；在流体动力学预测任务上与强基线相比仍具竞争力。虽仅用至多 4 次更新的自 rollout 训练，模型在 400 多次连续更新下仍保持准确。

Conclusion: Seq-Flow 通过将预测更新建模为分布间的条件流传输，实现少步采样的高效、准确且可长期递归的预测更新，代码已开源。

Abstract: Many scientific forecasting tasks require updating a distribution over future trajectories as new observations arrive. Conventional diffusion and flow models generate each forecast from Gaussian noise, often at the cost of many sampling steps. Warm-start methods reuse earlier predictions to reduce this cost, but their models are not trained to perform the forecast update itself, which can compromise quality under few-step sampling. In this work, we introduce Seq-Flow, a conditional flow model whose ODE transports samples from the previous forecast distribution to the updated one. Because successive forecasts often differ only modestly, this transport starts from an informative distribution and can produce accurate updates with few flow evaluations. Recursive reuse also creates a challenge: errors in one forecast become errors in the initial states of subsequent flows. We address this with self-rollout training, in which a moving average copy of the model generates forecasts that initialize later training updates. Unlike self-forcing methods, which reuse generated outputs as conditioning context, Seq-Flow reuses them as the source of the next flow. Experiments On particle-accelerator beam spill forecasting show Seq-Flow reduces CRPS by 65% under a few-NFE sampling budget, while remaining competitive with strong baselines on fluid-dynamics forecasting tasks. Although trained on self-rollouts of at most four updates, Seq-Flow remains accurate over more than 400 consecutive updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/Seq-Flow.

</details>


### [208] [A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447)
*Randy Ardywibowo,Arnav Dalal,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JOLT方法，通过联合训练特权教师与非特权学生，解决自蒸馏中特权信息导致的监督错配问题，提升长时程任务的学习效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖结果奖励，在困难长时程任务中监督稀疏、成功轨迹稀少且代价高；而自蒸馏虽无需独立教师模型，但特权条件化本身不保证蒸馏更新能提升学生，因为特权信息可能使教师走捷径，产生与学生当前行为不匹配的监督。

Method: 作者分析特权教师选择对学生更新的影响，推导教师局部蒸馏更新为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件；据此提出结合结果奖励与对学生token级KL正则的教师训练代理目标，并设计JOLT，让同一策略同时担任KL正则化的特权教师和接受稠密on-policy蒸馏的无特权学生。

Result: 在数学推理、编程、工具使用和终端使用等任务上，JOLT提升了训练效率与性能，且加入学生奖励后获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: 教师不仅需在任务上表现良好，还应提供契合学生当前能力的指导；通过KL正则化联合学习与教学，可有效缓解特权自蒸馏的监督错配问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the student's current behavior. Consequently, even a higher-performing teacher can provide guidance that degrades student performance. To address this, we analyze how the choice of privileged teacher affects the student's update. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [209] [NeuralBES: A Differentiable, Control-Aware Emulator for Scalable Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10459)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Takuya Kurihana,Wing Yee Au,Hon Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralBES是一个可微分的建筑能耗仿真器，用共享神经编码器将建筑元数据映射为物理有界的RC热模型参数，并通过对数空间并行扫描与预测-校正循环实现物理一致且准确的全年度能耗预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求侧灵活性（预测、转移、削减住宅能耗负荷）依赖于可信的热模型在数百万异质建筑上泛化。现有工具存在硬性权衡：高保真物理仿真器（如EnergyPlus）准确但串行、需逐建筑标定；纯数据驱动序列模型可扩展但丢弃了物理结构，导致预测难以被信任。

Method: 提出NeuralBES，一种可微分仿真器：用共享神经编码器将静态建筑元数据（面积、建造年代、HVAC类型等）映射为物理有界的电容、电导和设备系数，作为标量线性递推的系数，通过对数空间并行扫描求解；预测-校正循环处理恒温器-温度非线性，同时保留全时段梯度流。在ResStock数据集三个气候区上训练。

Result: 单一训练编码器可处理异构建筑原型、年代和气候区；黑盒基线产生统计上合理但物理不一致的轨迹。年度全年滚动预测中，NeuralBES是唯一同时满足物理有效、且误差在最强原始误差基线4个MAPE点以内的数据条件模型，参数量比Transformer和循环基线少约一个数量级；在参数量持平的物理有效基线中，其MAPE比灰盒RC方法降低一半以上。

Conclusion: NeuralBES通过将物理结构嵌入可扩展的神经参数化框架，解决了物理准确性与数据驱动可扩展性之间的权衡，为跨异质建筑的可靠能耗建模提供了高效且物理一致的方案。

Abstract: Demand-side flexibility i.e. forecasting, shifting, and curtailing residential energy loads, depends on thermal models trusted across millions of heterogeneous buildings. Existing tools force a hard tradeoff: high-fidelity physics simulators such as EnergyPlus are accurate but sequential and require per-building calibration, while purely data-driven sequence models scale but abandon the physical structure that makes their predictions trustworthy.
  We introduce NeuralBES (Building Energy Simulation), a differentiable emulator that resolves this tradeoff by parameterizing a resistance--capacitance (RC) based thermal model with a shared neural encoder: static building metadata such as floor area, vintage, and HVAC type is mapped to physically bounded capacitances, conductances, and equipment coefficients, which become the coefficients of a scalar linear recurrence solved via a log-space parallel scan, and a predictor--corrector loop closes the thermostat--temperature nonlinearity while preserving full-horizon gradient flow. Trained on the ResStock dataset across three climate zones, NeuralBES handles heterogeneous building archetypes, vintages, and climate zones within a single trained encoder, while black-box baselines produce statistically plausible but physically inconsistent trajectories. On the annual full-year rollout, NeuralBES is the only data-conditioned model that is simultaneously physics-valid and accurate to within 4 MAPE points of the strongest raw-error baseline, while operating at roughly an order of magnitude fewer parameters than the transformer and recurrent baselines; among physics-valid baselines at parameter parity it more than halves the MAPE of the grey-box RC alternative.

</details>


### [210] [Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation through Teacher-Relative Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460)
*Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Xiaomin Li,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Δ-MOPD方法，将每个教师的"教师减基座"logit偏移重新锚定在学生冻结初始化上作为蒸馏目标，替代传统的教师端点策略监督，在多教师在线策略蒸馏中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）通常迁移教师的端点策略，而端点策略混合了后训练带来的改变与教师继承自其基座的偏好。作者旨在揭示并解决"端点迁移"所受到的阻碍机制，探索目标构建这一独立设计维度。

Method: 提出Δ-MOPD，迁移每个教师的"教师减基座"logit偏移，并重新锚定在学生的冻结初始化上；在共同域组合与路由域蒸馏两种设置下，与端点监督进行对比，同时固定教师选择不变。

Result: 揭示了阻碍端点迁移的机制：继承的基座拉力可能超过后训练偏移，去除后可降低教师项范数比与目标-学生KL。三个组合教师时，Δ-MOPD比端点组合高出4.11 Math分和1.95五基准分；两个教师时与端点精度持平；分阶段路由下两种阶段顺序均取得更高平均性能，顺序差距从10.50降至6.42分；交错路由下两者表现相当。

Conclusion: 目标构建是MOPD中一个独立的设计轴，与教师选择互补。偏移目标在教师信号于同一状态组合时尤为有用，分阶段结果提供了收益可延伸至跨训练阶段累积信号的证据。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) is used in two settings. In common-domain composition, several teachers score each student rollout from one prompt domain and their signals form a single target; in routed-domain distillation, prompts from different domains are assigned to the corresponding specialist. Both settings usually transfer each teacher's endpoint policy, which mixes what post-training changed with preferences inherited from the teacher's base. We introduce $Δ$-MOPD, which transfers each teacher's teacher-minus-base logit shift re-anchored at the student's frozen initialization, and compare it with endpoint supervision in both settings while holding teacher selection fixed. We first expose the mechanism that impedes endpoint transfer: inherited base pull can exceed the post-training shift. Removing it reduces the teacher-term norm ratio and target--student KL.
  Across our experiments, the results suggest that shift targets are particularly useful when teacher signals are combined at a state. With three composed teachers, $Δ$-MOPD exceeds endpoint composition by $4.11$ Math and $1.95$ five-benchmark points; with two, it matches endpoint accuracy. Under phased routing, it achieves higher mean performance in both phase orders and reduces the observed order gap from $10.50$ to $6.42$ points. Under interleaved routing, where each update involves one teacher, the two targets perform comparably. The phased results provide supporting evidence that the benefit may extend to signals accumulated across training phases. Target construction is thus an independent design axis in MOPD, complementary to teacher selection.

</details>


### [211] [Oracle-Efficient and Parameter-Free Agnostic Smoothed Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10499)
*Sasha Voitovych,Adam Block,Alexander Rakhlin,Abhishek Shetty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在不可知（agnostic）设定下、无需已知基测度μ的oracle高效算法，基于Gaussian Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader，实现参数无关且次线性遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有smoothed online learning的oracle高效算法需满足两个额外假设之一：可采样基测度μ，或标签能被固定假设完美预测。这两者都限制了适用性，与经典统计学习中ERM在agnostic设定下无需知道数据分布即可高效学习形成对比。因此作者希望去除这些假设。

Method: 提出基于Gaussian Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader的算法，参数无关：无需知道μ、平滑参数σ或时间horizon T；每轮仅需一次ERM oracle调用。分析中引入若干新技术以建立遗憾界。

Result: 算法对VC维为d的二分类问题达到遗憾界Õ(d√(T/σ))，在最优意义下仅差√d因子，且无需μ的先验知识即可在agnostic设定下取得次线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 表明采样μ与完美标签两个假设均非必要，首次给出agnostic设定下无需μ知识的oracle高效算法，缩小了smoothed online learning与经典学习之间的差距。

Abstract: Online learning is an attractive framework in many domains because it permits well-defined learning even when data are dependent or chosen adversarially. This generality, however, comes at a steep price, introducing significant statistical and computational barriers. Recently, smoothed online learning has emerged as a promising framework that interpolates between the fully adversarial and fully stochastic settings by assuming that the conditional law of each covariate has density at most $1/σ$ with respect to some fixed base measure $μ$, and it is known to match the statistical and computational guarantees of classical learning while still allowing for much of the flexibility of online learning. However, existing oracle-efficient algorithms require either (i) sampling access to the base measure $μ$ or (ii) labels that are perfectly predicted by a fixed hypothesis. Both assumptions limit the applicability of these algorithms, in contrast to statistical learning, where empirical risk minimization (ERM) learns efficiently in the agnostic setting without any knowledge of the data distribution. We show that neither assumption is necessary, giving the first oracle-efficient algorithm that achieves sublinear regret in the agnostic setting without knowledge of $μ$. Our algorithm, based on Gaussian Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader, is parameter-free: it requires no knowledge of $μ$, the smoothing parameter $σ$, or the horizon $T$, and it achieves regret $\widetilde O(d\sqrt{T/σ})$ for binary classes of VC dimension $d$ with a single call to an ERM oracle per round, which is optimal up to a $\sqrt{d}$ factor. En route to establishing the regret bound, we introduce several new techniques that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [212] [Why Forget-Only Unlearning Needs Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10519)
*Luka Radić,Vikrant Singhal,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"仅遗忘式"机器遗忘（只给训练好的模型和要遗忘的样本，无保留数据），探讨其是否总是可行，并给出匹配再训练精度的下界及所需记忆信息量的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘希望删除指定样本后的输出接近从头再训练。本文关注最受限的设定——仅遗忘式遗忘（forget-only unlearning），即只获得已训练模型和待遗忘样本，没有保留数据或其他训练信息，因而需要回答：仅遗忘式遗忘是否总能实现。

Method: 从两方面展开理论分析：（1）指出不同数据集可产生相同训练模型，但在删除同样样本后所需输出迥异，据此推导遗忘输出与再训练之间的匹配精度下界，并针对若干标准学习算法具体化；（2）推导算法为应对任意删除请求必须记忆多少训练数据的信息量下界。

Result: 对若干标准学习算法给出了遗忘与再训练匹配精度的下界；对简单阈值学习器，所需记忆信息量可能高达整个数据集，尽管普通训练只需保留一个边界点。

Conclusion: 普通学习中丢弃的信息在后续删除时可能是必需的，因此为仅遗忘式遗忘设计的模型可能需要比标准训练保留更多信息。

Abstract: Machine unlearning asks for a deletion algorithm whose output is close to retraining from scratch without the selected forget examples. In this work, we study forget-only unlearning, where the deletion algorithm receives only the trained model and the examples to forget, with no retained data or extra training information. We ask whether forget-only unlearning is always possible. We first show that this depends on the learning method: different datasets can produce the same trained model but require very different outputs after the same examples are removed. Using this observation, we derive lower bounds on how accurately unlearning can match retraining and instantiate them for several standard learning algorithms. We then ask what must be true when forget-only unlearning succeeds. To this end, we derive lower bounds on what an algorithm must memorize about the training data to handle arbitrary deletion requests. For simple threshold learners, the required information can be as large as the entire dataset, even though ordinary training keeps only one boundary point. Overall, our results show that information discarded during ordinary learning may be needed later for deletion, so models designed for forget-only unlearning may need to retain more information than standard training does.

</details>


### [213] [Distilling Graph Geometry: Knowledge Gap from GNNs to MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10520)
*Zhewei Chen,Hao Zhu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Ahad N. Zehmakan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G^2MLP，用Ollivier-Ricci曲率指导图到MLP蒸馏的监督分配，解决稀疏/稠密图上的谱欠拟合与过拟合，推理时仅需标准MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-to-MLP蒸馏只迁移节点级预测或用置信度重加权，未明确学生应在何处保留教师由图结构诱导的几何信息。该缺失导致学生表示空间出现两类谱失败模式：稀疏图上谱欠拟合（丢失边界区域的高能方向），稠密图上谱过拟合（保留教师经聚合已坍缩的虚假方向）。

Method: 以能量加权的师生对齐目标为动机，提出G^2MLP训练时蒸馏框架，由Ollivier-Ricci曲率引导。曲率用于识别两类谱误差的集中位置，并在预测级对齐与表示级对齐之间分配监督。部署模型仍为标准MLP，推理无需图结构。

Result: 在节点分类基准上稳定优于无图蒸馏基线，在两种图密度情形下均缩小师生秩差距，并可无架构改动迁移至Graph Transformer教师与链路预测任务。

Conclusion: 显式建模教师图诱导几何的保留位置是图到MLP蒸馏的关键；曲率引导的监督分配能同时缓解谱欠拟合与谱过拟合，且不增加推理时的图依赖。

Abstract: GNN-to-MLP distillation aims to retain the predictive accuracy of a message-passing teacher while deploying a graph-free MLP at inference. Existing methods mainly transfer node-wise predictions or use confidence-based reweighting, but they do not specify where the student should preserve the teacher's graph-induced geometry. We show that this omission leads to two spectral failure modes in the student's representation space. On sparse graphs, the student suffers from spectral underfit, missing high-energy teacher directions concentrated near boundary regions. On dense graphs, it suffers from spectral overfit, retaining spurious directions that the teacher has collapsed through aggregation. Motivated by an energy-weighted teacher-student alignment objective, we propose Graph Geometry-aware MLP (G^2MLP), a training-time distillation framework guided by Ollivier-Ricci curvature. Curvature identifies where the two spectral errors concentrate and is used to allocate supervision between prediction-level and representation-level alignment. The deployed model remains a standard MLP and requires no graph access at inference. Across node-classification benchmarks, G^2MLP consistently improves over graph-free distillation baselines, reduces the teacher-student rank gap in both regimes, and transfers without architectural changes to Graph Transformer teachers and link prediction.

</details>


### [214] [Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536)
*Saif Punjwani,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis) 框架，将探索与优化解耦以提升 RLVR 的推理策略发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 理论上能采样出训练数据之外的新推理策略，但直接用强新颖性激励增强 RLVR 效果有限，甚至会损害模型质量，且由于可验证奖励仅监督模型知识与行为的很小一部分，这种损害难以恢复。

Method: ExpDis 将探索与优化分离：先用带新颖性奖励的探索者策略生成轨迹，按正确性和质量过滤，再蒸馏到独立的学生策略中；学生策略训练时不再使用新颖性奖励。该过程交替进行多轮探索与优化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和两个模型家族上，相同 wall-clock 预算下 ExpDis 优于 DAPO，并观察到更好的 pass@k 扩展性，说明生成模型的正确解更多样。

Conclusion: 解耦探索与优化可以激进地扩展探索而不损害学生策略，从而在推理任务上取得更优性能与多样性。

Abstract: Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them into a separate student policy. The student policy is then trained without a novelty bonus. We repeat the above procedure for several rounds, alternating between exploration and optimization. This decoupling allows us to aggressively scale exploration without degrading the student policy. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families, ExpDis outperforms DAPO at the same wall-clock budget. Moreover, we observe improved pass@$k$ scaling, indicating that ExpDis produces models that generate more diverse correct solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Part of the Strassen algorithm can speed up matrix multiplication in a parallel pebbling game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08929)
*Aaron Dayton,Thomas E. Baker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 Partial Strassen 算法，仅使用部分 Strassen 步骤进行矩阵乘法，内存占用小，性能优于传统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Strassen 算法虽能减少计算时间，但内存消耗大，限制了其实际性能。作者希望找到一种兼顾时间与内存的折中方案。

Method: 提出 Partial Strassen 算法，只使用一部分 Strassen 步骤进行矩阵乘法，并证明单线程与多线程下的内存占用都很小。在深度 1、2、3 上与 BLAS 及传统 Strassen 进行对比，并提供支持任意深度和矩形矩阵乘法的开源实现。

Result: 三级 Partial Strassen 算法耗时约为 BLAS 的 80%，内存需求为其 2.25 倍，理论上对足够大的矩阵可提升 75%。与传统 Strassen 相比，随矩阵增大性能更优，原因是内存消耗降低。

Conclusion: Partial Strassen 算法在时间与内存之间取得良好平衡，在大矩阵场景下优于传统 Strassen，并提供了通用开源实现。

Abstract: We present a novel variant of the Strassen algorithm called the Partial Strassen algorithm, which uses a fraction of the Strassen steps to perform matrix multiplication in less time than traditional implementations. The memory footprint required to implement this algorithm is provably small whether used with a single thread or in a multi-threaded context. Data comparing the Partial Strassen algorithm at depths one, two, and three with BLAS matrix multiplication show that the three-level Partial Strassen algorithm is able to perform matrix multiplication in $80\%$ the time of BLAS with $2.25$ times the memory requirement, with a theoretical improvement of $75\%$ for sufficiently large matrices. The Partial Strassen algorithm is also compared against an implementation of the traditional Strassen algorithm, and is shown to be more performant with increasing matrix size due to decreased memory consumption. An open source implementation of the Partial Strassen algorithm for arbitrary depth and rectangular matrix multiplication is also included.

</details>


### [216] [What Bloats Your Floats? Right-Sizing Numerical Precision for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09089)
*Pratyai Mazumder,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Yakup Koray Budanaz,Shriram Chandran,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文质疑偏微分方程仿真中高精度算术的必要性，提出基于信噪比的精度缩减评估准则，并给出面向混合精度子图的自动化数据流工作流，在天气与气候应用中实现最高1.8倍加速且不损失物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件趋势日益偏向面向AI负载的低精度格式，优先追求吞吐与能效而非全精度能力；而在PDE仿真中，空间与时间离散误差形成物理噪声下限，掩盖了FP64低位比特，使其变得无关紧要，因此高精度算术的必要性值得重新审视。

Method: 提出一种基于信噪比（SNR）的准则，相对于空间与时间加密来评估安全精度缩减对科学计算的影响；并提出一种自动化、以数据流为中心的工作流，用于在复杂科学应用的混合精度子图中进行有针对性的精度降低。

Result: 在天气和气候应用上进行大规模评估表明，在GPU上策略性地进行精度缩减可实现最高1.8倍的加速，同时不损害物理保真度。

Conclusion: 在PDE控制的仿真中，安全地降低精度是可行且有效的，策略性精度缩减可显著提升GPU性能而不影响物理保真度。

Abstract: While FP64 (binary64) remains the standard representation of real numbers in scientific computing, hardware trends increasingly favor low-precision formats optimized for AI workloads. This shift prioritizes throughput and energy efficiency over full-precision capabilities. We challenge the necessity of high-precision arithmetic in PDE-governed simulations, where spatial and temporal discretization errors introduce a physical noise floor that masks the lower-order bits of the FP64 format, rendering them irrelevant. We propose a criterion based on the Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) with respect to spatial and temporal refinements to evaluate the impact of safe precision reduction on scientific computations. Finally, we present an automated dataflow-centric workflow for targeted lowering within mixed-precision subgraphs in complex scientific applications. Large-scale evaluations of weather and climate applications show that strategic precision reduction on GPUs achieves up to 1.8x speedup without compromising physical fidelity.

</details>


### [217] [vLLM-Omni Technical Report: A Unified Serving Runtime for Omni-Modality Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09307)
*vLLM-Omni Team*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 vLLM-Omni，一个面向全模态生成（文本、音频、图像、视频、动作）的统一服务运行时，将各类负载建模为单一编排器下的多阶段流水线，支持跨阶段传输、流式输出与长时会话交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能系统交互已从文本类聊天机器人和编码智能体扩展到语音原生助手、视觉生成与编辑、世界模型环境和机器人动作循环，要求模型能输出文本、音频、图像、视频与动作。这些模型执行模式各异：多阶段自回归全模态与 TTS 流水线、迭代式扩散或流匹配生成器、以及跨步骤携带状态的长时世界模型或机器人循环。这使得服务不再只是单一文本解码循环，而是具有跨阶段传输、流式和会话形态交互的异构多阶段工作流。现有推理栈通常只针对某一架构族优化：LLM 服务器侧重自回归调度与 KV 管理，扩散栈侧重去噪与并行生成，二者都未提供面向分别处理语音、像素或动作生成器的共享控制平面，导致生产部署常退化为跨互不相通运行时的临时组合。

Method: vLLM-Omni 将每个工作负载组织为单一编排器下的多阶段流水线：编排器负责接收请求、在各阶段间推进请求，并对流式输出进行解复用。专用引擎与阶段副本提供算力；连接器在数据平面上传输大负载；面向会话的控制支持长时双工、世界模型与机器人工作负载。报告还覆盖了架构设计，包括阶段级 KV 路径、副本池、多硬件平台与效率栈，并提供 OpenAI 兼容 API 与 OpenPI API，服务于全模态、TTS、图像/视频、世界模型、机器人及双工工作负载。

Result: 在 H100 上的多模态 nightly CI 中进行评估（TTS 与 MiniCPM-o 在 H200 上），重点聚焦 Qwen3-Omni。

Conclusion: vLLM-Omni 作为统一的全模态生成服务运行时，通过单一编排器下的多阶段流水线与共享控制平面，解决了现有推理栈各自为政、依赖临时组合的问题，并以 OpenAI 兼容及 OpenPI API 支撑多样化的全模态、TTS、图像/视频、世界模型、机器人与双工工作负载，在 H100 与 H200 多硬件平台上完成评估验证。

Abstract: Interaction with intelligent systems is expanding beyond text-centric chatbots and coding agents. Speech-native assistants, visual generation and editing, world-model environments, and robot action loops require models that emit text, audio, images, video, and actions. These models differ in execution pattern: multi-stage autoregressive omni and TTS pipelines, iterative diffusion or flow-matching generators, and longer-lived world-model or robot loops that carry state across steps. As a result, serving is no longer a single text decode loop, but a heterogeneous multi-stage workflow with cross-stage transfer, streaming, and session-shaped interaction.
  Existing inference stacks are typically optimized for one architecture family. LLM servers deepen autoregressive scheduling and KV management, while diffusion stacks deepen denoising and parallel generation. Neither provides a shared control plane for pipelines that emit speech, pixels, or actions through separate generators, so production deployments often fall back to ad-hoc composition across disjoint runtimes.
  We present vLLM-Omni, a unified serving runtime for omni-modality generation. vLLM-Omni organizes each workload as a multi-stage pipeline under a single orchestrator that admits requests, advances them across stages, and demultiplexes streaming outputs. Specialized engines and stage replicas provide compute; a connector carries heavy payloads on the data plane; and session-oriented control supports long-lived duplex, world-model, and robot workloads.
  This report covers the architecture (stage-level KV paths, replica pools, multi-hardware platforms, and efficiency stack) and OpenAI-compatible and OpenPI APIs for omni, TTS, image/video, world-model, robot, and duplex workloads. We evaluate on the multimodal nightly CI on H100 (TTS and MiniCPM-o on H200), focused on Qwen3-Omni.

</details>


### [218] [Democratizing MoE inference on commodity GPUs with CoMoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09424)
*Ruwen Fan,Yuezhi Zu,Junru Li,Qingda Hu,Xinjun,Yang,Jiwu Shu,Youyou Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CoMoE：面向消费级GPU的通信高效MoE推理系统，通过以主机为中心的主动路由，减少通信量并消除全局同步停顿，显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE部署依赖专家并行，产生大量GPU间通信，现有系统需NVLink等高带宽P2P互联，部署成本高昂。消费级GPU算力相当但价格更低，有望实现民主化本地部署，然而其PCIe带宽受限且无P2P、需经主机中转，导致严重通信瓶颈。因此需要解决算力与通信不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出CoMoE：利用独特通信拓扑，将主机提升为主动路由中心。①token分发采用host-backed token multicast，将共享token仅写入主机一次，消除出站冗余传输；②token合并提出细粒度、token级聚合机制，使用主机暂存缓冲区，替代刚性全局同步，缓解长尾拖慢效应。

Result: 在RTX 5090 GPU上评估，CoMoE将推理吞吐最高提升1.46倍，性能接近支持NVLink的A800 GPU，而硬件成本仅为后者的23.4%。

Conclusion: CoMoE通过以主机为中心的路由设计，有效缓解消费级GPU在MoE推理中的通信瓶颈，以更低成本实现接近高端数据中心GPU的推理性能，推动MoE的本地化与普及部署。

Abstract: Deploying Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models relies heavily on Expert Parallelism, which generates intense inter-GPU communication. Consequently, state-of-the-art inference systems require high-bandwidth, P2P interconnects (e.g., NVLink) in datacenter GPUs to handle massive token routing, making deployment prohibitively expensive. Consumer GPUs offer comparable compute power at significantly lower cost, promising to democratize MoE inference for individuals and enable privacy-preserving local deployments. However, their bandwidth-limited (only weak PCIe bus bandwidth) and host-mediated interconnects (no P2P support) introduce severe communication bottlenecks.
  We present CoMoE, a communication-efficient MoE inference system that resolves this mismatch through novel host-centric routing. Our key insight is that the unique communication topology provides the opportunity to elevate the host to an active routing hub, which can fundamentally reduce communication volume and eliminate global synchronization-induced stalls. Specifically, for token dispatch, we introduce host-backed token multicast to write shared tokens to the host exactly once, eliminating outbound transmission redundancy. For token combine, we propose a fine-grained, token-level aggregation mechanism using host staging buffers, which replaces rigid global synchronization and mitigates straggler effects. Evaluation on RTX 5090 GPUs shows that CoMoE improves inference throughput by up to 1.46x, approaching the performance of NVLink-capable A800 GPUs at only 23.4% of the hardware cost.

</details>


### [219] [A Distributed Accelerated Preconditioned ADMM for Solving Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport on Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09512)
*Jiayi Zhu,Haihang Lan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出DAP-ADMM，一种全分布式算法，用于求解二次正则化的分布式最优传输模型，实现全局收敛与非遍历O(1/k)收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输模型不适用于具有图结构连接和不平衡质量的分配问题。分布式OT虽能应对这些需求，但通常由ADMM求解，其收敛慢限制了通信效率。

Method: 将加速预条件ADMM（AP-ADMM）适配到二次正则化分布式OT模型，提出DAP-ADMM全分布式算法，各节点仅与图邻居通信。针对局部ADMM子问题中的区间约束投影，推导出无需精确求解器的计算流程；并提出分布式自适应惩罚机制，利用局部可计算的KKT残差分量更新各节点惩罚参数，无需全局聚合。

Result: 在固定局部惩罚参数下，证明了KKT残差范数和原始目标间隙的全局收敛性与非遍历O(1/k)界。数值实验验证了无求解器流程的高效性以及DAP-ADMM相对标准分布式ADMM的实际加速效果。

Conclusion: DAP-ADMM在保持全分布式通信结构的同时，实现了理论收敛保证与实用加速，适用于图结构、不平衡质量的分布式最优传输问题。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has found broad applications in machine learning, economics, and management. Classical OT models, however, are not directly suited to allocation problems with graph-structured connectivity and unbalanced mass. Distributed OT formulations on bipartite graphs address these requirements but are typically solved by ADMM, whose slow convergence limits communication efficiency. We adapt the accelerated preconditioned ADMM (AP-ADMM) to the quadratically regularized distributed OT model and develop DAP-ADMM, a fully distributed algorithm in which each node communicates only with its graph neighbors. For fixed local penalty parameters, we establish global convergence and non-ergodic $O(1/k)$ bounds for both the KKT-residual norm and the primal objective gap. We derive an exact solver-free routine for the interval-constrained projections arising in the local ADMM subproblems. We propose a distributed self-adaptive penalty mechanism that updates node-specific penalties using locally computable KKT-residual components without global aggregation. Numerical experiments confirm the efficiency of the solver-free routine and the practical acceleration of DAP-ADMM over standard distributed ADMM.

</details>


### [220] [Fast and Memory Efficient Offload Training Framework with Hybrid XPU Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09657)
*Zhiyi Yao,Zuning Liang,Yuedong Xu,Jin Zhao,Jessie Hui Wang,Tong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MemFerry利用GPU的Direct Host Access (DHA)能力，实现CPU内存中数据的直接计算，构建GPU与DHA混合训练框架，并通过执行调度器、影子模型、梯度卸载动态规划算法及ScaleUp-MemFerry扩展，显著提升训练速度与可训练模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型规模不断增大，训练时GPU内存容易不足。主流方案ZeRO-Offload将优化器状态移至CPU内存并用CPU更新参数，但存在GPU利用率低、通信与计算重叠不佳、卸载策略不灵活等缺陷。作者希望借助GPU的DHA能力直接在CPU内存上计算，设计更高效的混合训练系统。

Method: 利用GPU的Direct Host Access (DHA)实现CPU内存数据计算，设计并实现MemFerry，包含执行调度器与影子模型。调度器策略性选择DHA计算层并同时传输其余参数到GPU以缩短前向传播时间，再将DHA参数加载到GPU以减少反向传播时间；影子模型为分散存储在GPU和CPU内存中的参数分区提供统一内存抽象。提出动态规划算法将梯度通过DHA卸载到CPU内存以进一步降低GPU内存占用。进一步扩展为ScaleUp-MemFerry，利用加速器互连带宽，通过自适应多路径传输辅助主机到加速器的数据搬移。

Result: 在单GPU上，MemFerry训练速度最高比ZeRO-Offload快1.68倍，可训练模型规模大1.52倍；扩展到8 GPU数据并行时训练速度至少提升28.1%；进一步扩展到含8个NPU的华为CloudMatrix384 scale-Up节点，ScaleUp-MemFerry相比DeepSpeed将端到端迭代时间最多减少20.7%。

Conclusion: MemFerry通过GPU的DHA能力实现GPU与CPU内存的混合计算与灵活卸载，有效提升训练速度、扩大可训练模型规模，并在单GPU、多GPU数据并行及新兴scale-up节点上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: With the ever-growing size of deep learning models, GPU memory is prone to being insufficient during training. A prominent approach is ZeRO-Offload, which moves the optimizer states to CPU memory and performs parameter update using CPU. However, the deficiencies of ZeRO-Offload include low GPU utilization, imperfect overlapping of communication and computation, and inflexible offloading. In this paper, we leverage Direct Host Access (DHA) on the GPU that can compute data in CPU memory, forming a novel hybrid on-GPU and DHA. We design and implement MemFerry consisting of an execution scheduler and a shadow model. The scheduler strategically chooses layers of parameters for DHA computation and transmits the remaining parameters to GPU memory simultaneously to shorten forward propagation time, and further loads DHA parameters to GPU memory to reduce backward propagation time. The shadow model presents a unified memory abstraction for the parameter partitions stored separately in GPU and CPU memories. To further reduce GPU memory usage, we present MemFerry along with its dynamic programming algorithm that offloads gradients to CPU memory via DHA. We further extend MemFerry to emerging scale-up domains with ScaleUp-MemFerry, which exploits otherwise underutilized accelerator interconnect bandwidth to assist host-to-accelerator data movement through adaptive multi-path transfer. Our experiments show that \system trains up to $1.68\times$ faster and MemFerry can train $1.52\times$ larger model compared to ZeRO-Offload on a single GPU, and increase training speed by at least $28.1\%$ when scaling to data parallelism on 8 GPUs. We further extend the design to a Huawei CloudMatrix384 scale-Up node with up to 8 NPUs, and our ScaleUp-MemFerry reduces the end-to-end iteration time by up to $20.7\%$ over DeepSpeed.

</details>


### [221] [Communication-Aware Qubit Placement and Automatic Node-Count Allocation for Distributed State-Vector Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09659)
*Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Sergio Iserte,Petter Sandås,Ricard S. Raigada-García,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种通信感知的量子比特布局优化框架（集成于QuCSLO库），通过优化qubit分配到节点来最小化节点间通信，并动态选择最优计算资源数；在合成与QASMBench电路上实现通信割显著下降，运行速度较identity布局提升2倍以上，尤其在高纠缠电路中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子电路模拟通过将qubit分布到多个计算节点来支持大规模量子算法，但低效的qubit布局会导致过多的节点间通信，严重限制性能与可扩展性。因此需要一种能系统性减少节点间数据交换、并动态适配计算资源规模的布局优化方法。

Method: 提出通信感知的qubit布局优化框架，系统性地最小化节点间数据交换，同时动态选择最优计算资源数量。该框架集成到QuCSLO库中，支持动态节点扩展、OpenQASM解析，并可与QuEST模拟器无缝集成。

Result: 在合成基准和真实QASMBench电路上的大量评估表明：优化布局显著降低了通信割，相比identity布局实现超过2倍的运行时加速，在高纠缠电路中尤为明显。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了战略性qubit布局对分布式模拟效率的关键影响，并提供了一种实用、资源可扩展的量子工作负载解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed quantum circuit simulation enables the execution of large-scale quantum algorithms by partitioning qubits across multiple computational nodes. However, inefficient qubit placement frequently leads to excessive inter-node communication, severely limiting performance and scalability. This work introduces a communication-aware qubit layout optimization framework that systematically minimizes data exchange among nodes while dynamically selecting the optimal number of computational resources. Integrated into the QuCSLO library, our approach supports dynamic node scaling, OpenQASM parsing, and seamless integration with the QuEST simulator. Extensive evaluations on both synthetic benchmarks and real-world QASMBench circuits demonstrate that optimized layouts significantly reduce communication cuts and yield runtime speedups of over 2x compared to identity layouts, particularly in highly entangled circuits. Our results underscore the critical impact of strategic qubit placement on distributed simulation efficiency and provide a practical, resource-scalable solution for quantum workloads.

</details>


### [222] [Rendezvous under Variable Disorientation:The Algorithmic Power of Fixed Unit Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09713)
*Toshimitsu Masuzawa,Yuichi Sudo,Koichi Wada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在弱几何与同步假设下研究带灯机器人的确定性 Rendezvous，聚焦可变朝向（VD）与 VD+固定单位距离（FUD），给出多组不可行性与颜色复杂度边界，并提出周期性距离类技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱几何与同步假设下，机器人局部坐标系可任意旋转、反射、缩放，使得确定性 Rendezvous 的可行性与颜色需求不明确，需要刻画度量稳定性、单侧可见性与调度原子性如何共同影响可解性和颜色复杂度。

Method: 针对 VD，在 FSTA/SSYNCH 与 FCOM/ASYNCH 下证明颜色无关的不可行性，并在 RSYNCH 与 R-RSYNCH 下建立双色边界；针对 VD+FUD，引入周期性距离类，将物理距离的乘性变化转化为可预测的循环相位偏移。

Result: VD+FUD 下得到 FSTA/FCOM 在 RSYNCH 与 R-RSYNCH 中的双色自稳定非 L-Rendezvous 算法（允许非刚性运动）；刚性运动下得到 FSTA/SSYNCH 的最优三色算法、FSTA/M-atomic ASYNCH 与 FCOM/CM-atomic ASYNCH 的四色算法，以及全 ASYNCH 下六色 FCOM 算法，将上界从十二色减半。

Conclusion: 固定局部度量可作为持久循环记忆；度量稳定性、单侧可见性与调度原子性共同决定 Rendezvous 的可解性与颜色复杂度。

Abstract: We study deterministic Rendezvous of two robots with lights under weak geometric and synchronization assumptions. Our focus is on Variable Disorientation (VD), where each robot may arbitrarily rotate, reflect, and rescale its local coordinate system at every \Look, and on VD+Fixed-Unit-Distance (FUD), where each robot's local unit distance remains fixed. We consider the one-sided visibility models FSTA and FCOM, and schedulers ranging from the energy-restricted RSYNCH and R-RSYNCH to fully asynchronous executions.
  Under VD, we prove color-independent impossibility results for FSTA under SSYNCH and for FCOM under ASYNCH, and establish sharp two-color boundaries under RSYNCH and R-RSYNCH. Under VD+FUD, we introduce periodic distance classes, which turn multiplicative changes of the physical distance into predictable cyclic phase shifts. This technique yields a two-color self-stabilizing non-$L$-Rendezvous algorithm for both FSTA and FCOM under RSYNCH and R-RSYNCH even with Non-Rigid movement.
  Under Rigid movement, periodic distance classes further yield an optimal three-color non-quasi-self-stabilizing algorithm for FSTA under SSYNCH, a four-color FSTA algorithm under $M$-atomic ASYNCH, and a four-color FCOM algorithm under CM-atomic ASYNCH. By guarding the latter phase mechanism with two additional colors and replacing its initial swap by passive initialization, we obtain a six-color FCOM algorithm under full ASYNCH, improving the previous twelve-color upper bound by a factor of two.
  These results show that a fixed local metric can serve as persistent cyclic memory, and clarify how metric stability, one-sided visibility, and scheduler atomicity jointly determine Rendezvous solvability and color complexity.

</details>


### [223] [HPC-MQBench: Qualification-First Benchmarking on Slurm with a Single-Broker Kafka Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09786)
*Sepehr Mahmoodian,Julian Kunkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 HPC-MQBench，一个由 Slurm 编排的基准测试框架，用于在高性能计算集群上对消息系统进行可恢复的实验控制、可信证据校验与配置资格筛选，并以 Kafka 为例完成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算集群上的消息实验需要协调服务、客户端与测量，并与调度器分配边界绑定。现有方法难以保证实验可恢复、证据可审计，且在选择配置时缺乏资格校验，直接在速率排名前就需要严格筛选。

Method: 构建 HPC-MQBench：由 Slurm 编排，将可恢复实验控制与记录校验、投递核算、资格判定相连，之后再作速率排名。Kafka 评估将生产者/控制器、单 broker、消费者与监控部署在四个节点；采用内存后端日志；设置两阶段验证，每阶段将十个工作负载重复五个区块；最终阶段选取的工作负载在全部五次观测中合格。

Result: 120 种工作负载配置产生 99 个合格观测，14 个不符合生产者投递策略，7 个证据无效。最终阶段所选工作负载五次观测全部合格，中位数为 2,927 MiB/s 均衡端点速率、1.24% 待投递、99 百分位延迟 2.56 秒。早期阶段该工作负载仅在两次观测中合格，且在分配边界后合格率提升，但未确立因果性的分配效应。选择结果依赖于阶段与投递策略；资源测量未隔离出唯一瓶颈。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是在固定 broker 数量下实现分布式实验控制与可审计配置选择的框架。多 broker 支持与扩展性实验为未来工作。

Abstract: Messaging experiments on high-performance computing clusters must coordinate services, clients, and measurement within scheduler allocations. We present HPC-MQBench, a Slurm-orchestrated benchmark that links resumable experiment control to record checks, delivery accounting, and qualification before rate ranking. Its Kafka evaluation placed producers/controller, one broker, consumers, and monitoring on four nodes. With memory-backed logs, 120 workload configurations yielded 99 qualified observations, 14 outside the producer-delivery policy, and seven with invalid evidence. Two validation stages each repeated ten workloads in five blocks. In the final stage, the selected workload qualified in all five observations, with medians of 2,927 mebibytes per second balanced endpoint rate, 1.24 percent pending deliveries, and 2.56 seconds for the 99th-percentile latency. It qualified in only two observations in the earlier stage, where qualification improved after an allocation boundary without establishing a causal allocation effect. Selection is conditional on the stage and delivery policy. Resource measurements did not isolate a unique bottleneck. The contribution is a framework for distributed experiment control and auditable configuration selection at a fixed broker count; multi-broker support and scaling experiments remain future work.

</details>


### [224] [Reconciling Bottom-Up Metrics with Top-Down Reporting for Cloud Carbon Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10148)
*Philipp Wiesner,Loïc Lannelongue,Alexander Acker,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"可调和软件碳强度(rSCI)"愿景：通过残差将自下而上的单工作负载能耗估算锚定到云提供商的顶层报告，从而弥合两类碳核算方法之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云应用碳足迹测量存在两套相互脱节的方法：自下而上的指标（如SCI）可为碳感知优化提供信号，但租户无法获知提供方侧的大量开销信息；自上而下的提供商报告更全面，却粒度粗、方法不透明。二者在范围与报告频率上不一致，当前被当作解耦的两个体系（前者用于优化，后者用于企业披露）。作者认为二者应当可调和——若单工作负载指标的改进永不出现在提供商经审计的报告中，该指标就难以在规模化问责中被采纳。

Method: 综述云计算碳核算的最新进展，提出rSCI（reconciled SCI）概念：以残差方式将自下而上的能耗估算重新锚定到云提供商的顶层报告；并通过对该残差按其物理驱动因素进行分解与分配，使rSCI在保留运营激励的同时，将闲置容量与隐含碳归因到真正驱动它们的工作负载上。同时评估当前顶层报告是否具备支持可信调和所需的粒度与方法一致性。
