Result: 分析表明，当今的自上而下报告在粒度和方法一致性上仍过于粗糙，无法支持可信的碳核算调和；作者据此给出了在提供商报告实践与相关标准方面使其可行的具体步骤。

Conclusion: 云碳核算的自下而上指标与自上而下报告应当且可以实现调和。rSCI通过残差分解与分配，兼顾运营优化激励与全面的碳归因（含闲置容量与隐含碳）；但要落地，需提供商报告与行业标准在粒度与方法一致性上进一步改进。

Abstract: Organizations seeking to reduce the carbon footprint of their cloud applications must rely on two largely disconnected ways of measuring it, each with important limitations. Bottom-up metrics like Software Carbon Intensity (SCI) provide signals for carbon-aware optimization, but tenants lack the information needed to account for many provider-side overheads. Top-down reports by cloud providers provide a more comprehensive view of a tenant's carbon footprint but are coarse and methodologically opaque. Because the two approaches differ in scope and reporting frequency, the current state of the art treats them as decoupled: bottom-up metrics for optimization, top-down reports for corporate disclosure.
  We argue that the two signals should be reconcilable. A per-workload metric whose improvements never surface in the provider's audited report will not be adopted for accountability at scale. We survey the state of the art in cloud carbon accounting and propose a vision for reconciled SCI (rSCI): a per-workload metric that re-anchors a bottom-up energy estimate to the cloud provider's top-down report through a residual. By decomposing and allocating this residual according to its physical drivers, rSCI can preserve operational incentives while also attributing idle capacity and embodied carbon to the workloads that drive them. We show that today's top-down reporting is still too coarse and methodologically inconsistent to support credible reconciliation and lay out concrete steps in provider reporting and standards to make it practical.

</details>


### [225] [Efficient Heuristics and Machine Learning Approach for Fault Characterization in Distributed Self-Stabilizing Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10386)
*Amit Garu,Duong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式系统中"一致性违反故障(cvf)"下自稳定程序的行为，通过冲突边状态空间探索（针对(Δ+1)图着色）与机器学习方法（针对极大独立集）两种互补途径来刻画故障发生的时机与位置，以支持运行时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统依赖分布式协议，但增加资源会提升复杂度并引入故障。其中使用分布式共享内存的系统会出现一致性违反故障(cvf)，即进程读到其他进程写入的陈旧数据。这类故障在优先保证可用性的系统中必然存在。已有研究表明，只要cvf在可容忍范围内，自稳定程序仍能保持正确，因此刻画cvf下自稳定程序的行为有助于设计既正确又高效的系统。

Method: 由于穷举状态空间探索面临状态爆炸问题，本文采用两种互补方法：(1) 针对单调稳定的(Δ+1)图着色程序，提出基于冲突边的状态空间探索算法；(2) 针对极大独立集(MIS)程序，采用机器学习方法构建经过严格验证的ML模型。

Result: 提出了基于冲突边的状态空间探索算法；构建了经过严格验证的机器学习模型，并在分布外数据集上通过针对已知图不变量的结构一致性检查进行了评估。

Conclusion: 通过冲突边探索与ML方法相结合，可有效理解cvf下自稳定程序何时何地发生故障，从而支持提升计算效率的运行时措施，帮助设计者构建正确且高效的分布式系统。

Abstract: Modern large-scale systems rely on distributed protocols to maximize efficiency while preserving the correctness guarantees of single-process execution. However, designing such protocols is non-trivial: adding resources to a system inherently increases its complexity, which in turn introduces faults that must be addressed during design. One such fault class, arising in systems that use distributed shared memory (e.g., replicated databases), is consistency violating fault (cvf), a fault in which a process accessing shared memory reads stale data previously written by another process. Cvfs are inherent to systems that prioritize availability over strict consistency. Prior work has shown that self-stabilizing programs remain correct under high availability, provided such faults stay within a tolerable bound. Characterizing the behavior of self-stabilizing programs in the presence of cvfs can therefore help system designers build systems that are both correct and efficient. In this paper, we study self-stabilizing programs under cvfs to understand when and where such faults occur, enabling runtime measures that improve computational efficiency. Since exhaustive state-space exploration does not scale, it suffers from state-space explosion; our study investigates two complementary approaches: a conflict-based state-space exploration technique for monotonically stabilizing $(Δ + 1)$-Graph Coloring programs, and a machine-learning-based approach for a Maximal Independent Set program. We propose a conflicting-edges-based state-space exploration algorithm alongside a rigorously validated ML model, evaluated on an out-of-distribution dataset through structural consistency checks against known graph invariants.

</details>


### [226] [LOCAA: An Agentic System for Automated Lossy Compressor Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10487)
*Khondoker Mirazul Mumenin,Dong Dai,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Jinzhen Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LOCAA是一个基于大语言模型的科学有损压缩自动调优智能体，通过工具集成执行、压缩器感知引导和持久记忆实现压缩在环搜索，显著减少调优试验次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学模拟产生海量数据，有损压缩对降低存储与数据传输成本至关重要。但用户以数值误差界限（EB）配置压缩器，却用量化指标和端到端性能评估结果。由于EB与这些结果的关系随数据集和压缩器变化，找到合适配置通常需要穷举搜索，耗时且计算开销大。

Method: 提出LOCAA——基于LLM的科学有损压缩自动调优智能体，采用工具集成执行实现压缩在环搜索，结合压缩器感知引导和持久记忆。支持用户自定义目标与约束，无需专门设计搜索策略。在三个科学应用、五种压缩器、三种用例（固定比率压缩、多质量约束下调优、质量与时间约束下调优）上评估。

Result: 固定比率搜索中，跨两个应用12个字段，LOCAA平均比二分搜索少1.98倍试验、比FRaZ少5.03倍。在联合PSNR与SSIM约束下最大化压缩比，跨CESM的6个字段，LOCAA将平均试验次数从二分搜索的61.5降至17，减少72.4%。持久记忆使同一字段多个时间步的平均试验次数再降27.9%。

Conclusion: 结果证明工具增强的LLM智能体能在多样科学数据和用户自定义目标下提供灵活高效的压缩器调优。

Abstract: Large-scale scientific simulations generate substantial data volumes, making lossy compression essential for reducing storage and data movement costs. However, users configure compressors through numerical error bounds (EBs) while often evaluating results using quality metrics and end-to-end performance. Because the relationship between an EB and these outcomes varies across datasets and compressors, identifying a suitable configuration typically requires exhaustive search, which can be time-consuming and computationally demanding. We present LOCAA, an Large language model-based scientific lossy compression auto-tuning agent that performs compression-in-the-loop search using tool-integrated execution, compressor-aware guidance, and persistent memory. LOCAA supports user-defined objectives and constraints without requiring a specialized search strategy. We evaluate LOCAA across three scientific applications, five compressors, and three use cases: fixed-ratio compression, compression tuning under multiple quality constraints, and compression tuning under quality and time constraints. For fixed-ratio search across twelve fields from two applications, LOCAA requires 1.98x fewer evaluation trials than binary search and 5.03x fewer than FRaZ on average. For compression ratio maximization under joint Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) and Structural Similarity Index Measure constraints across six fields from the Community Earth System Model application, LOCAA reduces the average evaluation trials from 61.5 using binary search to 17, corresponding to a 72.4% reduction. Persistent memory further reduces the average number of trials by 27.9% across multiple timesteps of the same field. These results demonstrate the potential of tool-augmented LLM agents to provide flexible and efficient compressor tuning across diverse scientific data and user-defined objectives.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [RiftANN: Efficient Graph Traversal for Vector Search with RDMA-Based Memory Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08990)
*Qi Lin,Zhenyu Zhang,Jun Kong,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RiftANN 是面向被动 RDMA 分离内存的图近似最近邻搜索系统，通过紧凑向量导航、精确向量选择性验证、有界单边 RDMA 流水线、并行评估与影响门控、反馈控制等机制，在十亿级向量检索中显著降低延迟并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿级向量集合的图索引常超出单服务器 DRAM 容量。被动 RDMA 分离内存可扩展容量且无需内存节点计算，但传统最佳优先搜索在此环境下表现差：细粒度、非必要的远程读取增加通信开销；候选评估与后续扩展之间的依赖使遍历串行化，导致 CPU 空闲。

Method: RiftANN 在计算节点用紧凑向量导航，并基于观测到的近似误差设定阈值，选择性验证精确向量；采用有界单边 RDMA 流水线，将剩余远程访问批量处理并与本地计算重叠；并行评估返回的邻居并增量整合已完成结果；通过影响门控估计未完成评估是否可能改变候选集，在影响较小时继续遍历，同时避免不完整搜索状态导致不安全推进；使用轻量反馈控制器随搜索配置和查询负载变化协调 RDMA 并发与后台评估能力。

Result: 在 100M 规模的 SIFT、DEEP、SPACEV 及 SIFT1B 上评估。在相同召回率下，RiftANN 相比 DistVS 实现 1.6x 至 4.3x 的延迟加速和 1.1x 至 2.2x 的吞吐提升；相比基于 SSD 的 DiskANN 和 PipeANN 实现 1.6x 至 5.6x 的延迟加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，被动分离内存无需在内存节点进行查询时计算，即可支持低延迟的图向量检索。

Abstract: Graph indexes for billion-scale vector collections can exceed a single server's DRAM capacity. Passive RDMA-based disaggregated memory provides scalable capacity without memory-node computation, but conventional best-first search performs poorly in this setting. Fine-grained and unnecessary remote reads increase communication cost, while dependencies between candidate evaluation and subsequent expansion serialize traversal and leave CPUs idle.
  We present RiftANN, a graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) system for passive RDMA-based disaggregated memory. RiftANN navigates using compact vectors at compute nodes and selectively verifies exact vectors using a threshold calibrated from observed approximation errors. A bounded one-sided RDMA pipeline batches and overlaps the remaining remote accesses with local processing. RiftANN evaluates returned neighbors in parallel and incrementally incorporates completed results. An impact gate estimates whether unfinished evaluations may change the candidate set, allowing traversal to continue when their expected influence is small while preventing unsafe advancement from incomplete search state. A lightweight feedback controller coordinates RDMA concurrency with background evaluation capacity as search configurations and query loads change.
  We evaluate RiftANN on SIFT, DEEP, and SPACEV at 100M scale and SIFT1B. At matched recall, RiftANN achieves 1.6x to 4.3x latency speedups and 1.1x to 2.2x higher throughput than DistVS, and 1.6x to 5.6x latency speedups over SSD-based DiskANN and PipeANN. These results show that passive disaggregated memory can support low-latency graph-based vector retrieval without query-time computation at memory nodes.

</details>


### [228] [Building Navigable Graphs Without Search in Three Composable Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09041)
*Édgar Chávez*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种无需邻居搜索即可构建可导航图的三阶段构造方法，指出中间阶段决定质量；其终点阶段可与任意分区器组合，在多个大规模语料上以更少距离评估达到相当召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可导航图通常需要搜索邻居，开销大。本文希望无需邻居搜索即可构建高质量可导航图，并厘清哪一阶段决定最终质量。

Method: 三阶段可分离构造：分区得到每点若干成员资格的候选池；终点阶段将候选转为出边，采用有界堆、按每语料松弛量的遮挡剪枝，并追加反向边、仅在列表溢出处重新剪枝；骨架阶段使用任意边集（免剪枝）保持从入口可达，本文用随机样本上的半空间近邻边。

Result: 在PiPNN候选池上，六个十万到一亿点语料中，终点阶段在等召回下距离评估减少3%至14%；每点60至120成员时，在k=10和k=100上与全稠密构建相当或更优，构建时间为0.5至0.9倍且确定；GIST上各池均在精确kNN上限4%内，块覆盖遗漏由kNN图局部聚类预测，零聚类尾部决定所需成员数并随n增长。

Conclusion: 可导航图构建可分解，中间候选池阶段决定质量；终点与骨架设计使构造高效、确定且可组合，并给出语料所需成员数的可预测依据。代码、补丁与日志均已公开。

Abstract: Navigable graphs can be built without searching for neighbors: partition the data, evaluate every pair inside each part, and select each point's edges from the candidates. We give such a construction in three separable stages and show that the middle one decides the quality. The pool is any partition with a few memberships per point. The ending turns a point's candidates into out-edges; ours keeps a bounded heap, prunes by occlusion with a per-corpus slack, and appends reverse edges, re-pruning only where a list overflows. The spine is any edge set, exempt from the prune, that keeps the graph reachable from its entry; ours, half-space-proximal edges over a random sample, routes monotonically to every sampled point and replaces a spanning tree at 1/10 to 1/500 of its cost. The ending composes with any partitioner: on PiPNN's own candidate pool it beats PiPNN's ending on each of six corpora from $10^6$ to $10^8$ points, by 3 to 14% in distance evaluations at equal recall, and with 60 to 120 memberships per point the composed build matches or beats a full dense construction at k=10 and k=100 on all six, in 0.5 to 0.9 of its build time, deterministically. The analysis explains why. Once a pool is localised its quality is set by the data: every pool built on GIST lands within 4% of the exact-kNN ceiling, and the pairs a block cover misses are predicted, point by point, by the local clustering of the kNN graph, whose zero-clustering tail sets the memberships a corpus needs and grows with n. All code, patches and logs are public.

</details>


### [229] [CATune: Structural Constraint-Aware Bayesian Optimization for DBMS Configuration Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09276)
*Fangping Lan,Qi Zhang,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CATune将数据库旋钮间的确定性顺序约束显式建模为搜索空间结构，实现约束一致子空间内的贝叶斯优化，显著提升调优效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代DBMS配置旋钮众多，搜索空间高维异构，自动调优成本高。现有基于机器学习的调优方法将配置域视为箱式约束，仅通过工作负载反馈隐式捕捉旋钮间关系，忽略了DBMS文档中明确的确定性依赖（尤其是顺序约束），导致大量无效采样、效率低。

Method: 提出CATune约束感知贝叶斯优化框架：将旋钮间的确定性顺序约束建模为搜索域的结构组件，在约束一致的子空间内优化而非通过采样违规学习可行边界；设计拓扑感知采样策略，在探索时遵守依赖结构；并构建精度优先的自动约束抽取流水线，结合LLM解析与可靠性保障机制以缓解幻觉依赖。

Result: 在PostgreSQL和MySQL上使用TPC-C与TPC-H负载的实验表明：跨代理模型与BO框架，CATune显著提升采样效率与最终调优质量；默认范围下达到基线最优的速度最高快12.5倍，吞吐量提升最高63.37%；在知识引导的缩减范围及替代优化实现下改进依然保持。

Conclusion: 显式建模系统定义的确定性顺序约束可增强优化鲁棒性与系统稳定性，为数据库自动配置调优提供了更高效可靠的范式。

Abstract: Modern DBMSs expose hundreds of configuration knobs, resulting in a high-dimensional and heterogeneous search space that makes automated tuning costly. Existing ML-based tuning systems typically treat the configuration domain as box-constrained and rely on workload feedback to implicitly capture inter-knob relationships. However, DBMS documentation specifies deterministic knob dependency constraints, particularly ordering constraints, that characterize structurally valid regions of the configuration space. We present CATune, a constraint-aware Bayesian optimization (BO) framework that models deterministic inter-knob ordering constraints as structural components of the search domain. Instead of learning feasibility boundaries through sampled violations, CATune performs optimization within a constraint-consistent subspace. We develop a topology-aware sampling strategy that respects dependency structure during exploration and avoids the inefficiencies of post-hoc constraint handling. To enable automated constraint discovery, we further design a precision-first extraction pipeline that combines LLM-based parsing with reliability safeguards to mitigate hallucinated dependencies. Experiments on PostgreSQL and MySQL using TPC-C and TPC-H workloads show that CATune substantially improves both sample efficiency and final tuning quality across surrogate models and BO frameworks. Under default ranges, CATune reaches the baseline optimum up to 12.5x faster and improves throughput by up to 63.37%. The improvements persist under knowledge-guided reduced ranges and alternative optimization implementations. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling system-defined deterministic ordering constraints enhances optimization robustness and system stability.

</details>


### [230] [No Trace, No Claim: Two Contracts for Database Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09286)
*Xiaofei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出面向LLM智能体的数据系统需要两类可执行契约：计划契约（定义智能体可执行和引用的内容）与证据契约（记录结果支持主张时的信念状态、完整性与溯源）。作者在双时态图系统TGMS中实例化这两类契约，并通过静态计划验证器与主张验证器实现。实验表明该系统能捕获仅靠输入模式与值接地无法发现的失败（不存在的结果字段、局部计数被当作完整计数），在CollegeMsg上达到0.408的答案准确率，且主张门控可将220个答案中的21个无支撑主张降至0个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体能生成数据库操作并解释结果，但生成计划、执行条件与呈现给用户的主张之间存在鸿沟，导致诸如不存在的字段、局部计数被误报为完整结果等问题，且仅靠输入模式校验和值接地无法发现这些失败。因此需要可强制执行的契约来约束智能体的执行与主张生成。

Method: 作者提出计划契约与证据契约，并在TGMS双时态图系统中实现：LLM在固定的时间算子接口上进行规划，静态验证器在执行前检查计划，主张验证器将类型化主张与内容寻址的溯源记录进行核对，并传播完整性信息。

Result: 结果字段检查使不存在字段变为可修复的拒绝；完整性传播在15个受控案例中全部检出，关闭后全部漏检。在CollegeMsg上TGMS达到0.408的类型化答案准确率，优于基线0.064–0.284；在Bitcoin-OTC上，直接SQL与TGMS相当，说明固定算子接口并无普遍优势。主张门控前220个答案中21个含无支撑主张，门控后199个输出答案中无此类主张，代价是少输出21个答案。

Conclusion: 计划契约使无效计划可被拒绝且执行在记录条件下可复现，证据契约使受门控的主张与引用证据保持一致，但两者均不保证对用户意图的正确解释。

Abstract: LLM agents can generate database operations and explain their results, but current interfaces often leave a gap between generated plans, execution conditions, and claims presented to users. We argue that agent-facing data systems need two enforceable contracts. A plan contract defines what an agent may execute and reference; an evidence contract records the belief state, completeness, and provenance under which a result supports a claim.
  We instantiate these contracts in TGMS, a bi-temporal graph system in which an LLM plans over a fixed temporal operator interface. A static verifier checks plans before execution, and a claim verifier checks typed claims against content-addressed traces. Live model runs exposed two failures missed by input-only schemas and value-only grounding: nonexistent result fields and page-local counts reported as complete-result counts. Result-field checking makes the first a repairable rejection. Completeness propagation detects the second in all 15 controlled cases and misses all 15 when disabled.
  On the frozen CollegeMsg workload, TGMS reaches 0.408 typed-answer accuracy versus 0.064--0.284 for the evaluated baselines. On Bitcoin-OTC, direct SQL over the same bi-temporal store matches TGMS, showing no universal accuracy advantage for the fixed operator interface. On correction probes, TGMS and bi-temporal SQL answer historical-belief questions, while latest-state baselines cannot. Before claim gating, 21 of 220 answers contain an unsupported gated claim; after gating, none of 199 emitted answers does, at the cost of 21 fewer answers. The plan contract makes invalid plans rejectable and execution reproducible under recorded conditions, while the evidence contract makes gated claims faithful to cited evidence. Neither guarantees correct interpretation of user intent.

</details>


### [231] [Indexing Designs and Adaptive Data Distribution Optimization for In-Memory Databases on Hybrid DDR--CXL Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09414)
*Huijie Cao,Shun Yang,Yinan Zhang,Huiqi Hu,Xuan Zhou,Weining Qian*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文针对DDR-CXL混合内存数据库，提出五种索引设计方案，分析其权衡关系，并开发自适应数据分布优化方法。实验表明单索引直接寻址设计在多数场景下性能最佳，自适应优化可提升DDR内存效率，吞吐量最高达1.59倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL内存虽能扩展单节点内存容量，但延迟高于本地DDR、带宽更低。混合DDR-CXL数据库需要索引与数据分布的联合设计。不同索引设计在访问与迁移路径上存在差异，会约束索引与元组的放置，导致运行时性能、DDR内存效率与系统集成复杂度之间的权衡难以确定。

Method: 提出五种基于两种思路（将两层视为统一数据空间或分别管理）的索引设计，分析其权衡与成因；针对显式管理分布的单一索引设计，开发自适应数据分布优化：分别管理索引对象与元组，通过分层热度追踪与索引对象驻留策略降低元数据开销，并扩展收益感知准入与随机淘汰策略，根据各类型的预期收益与内存占用调整放置位置。

Result: 在混合DDR-CXL数据库原型上使用YCSB评估：单索引直接寻址设计在多数工作负载与系统配置下性能最佳；双索引设计在DDR容量受限或访问高度集中时表现更好，且集成复杂度较低。自适应分布优化提升了DDR内存效率与系统性能，使单索引设计吞吐量最高达到未分离管理方案对应设计的1.59倍。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统化的索引设计分类与自适应分布优化，为DDR-CXL混合内存数据库在性能、内存效率与集成复杂度之间提供了有效的权衡指导，单索引直接寻址结合自适应分布优化是多数场景下的优选方案。

Abstract: CXL memory expands single-node memory capacity for in-memory databases but has higher latency and lower bandwidth than local DDR. Hybrid DDR--CXL databases require joint indexing and data distribution design. Indexing designs differ in access and migration paths and constrain the placement of indexes and tuples, creating trade-offs in runtime performance, DDR memory efficiency, and system integration complexity. Their performance impact is thus difficult to determine. Indexes and tuples may differ in access characteristics: placing them in the same tier may reduce DDR efficiency, while separate management adds tracking and decision overhead.
  We propose five indexing designs based on two approaches: treating the two tiers as a unified data space or managing them separately. We analyze their trade-offs and underlying causes and develop adaptive data distribution optimization for single-index designs in which the database explicitly manages distribution. The method manages index objects and tuples separately, reduces metadata overhead through hierarchical hotness tracking and an index object residency policy, and extends benefit-aware admission and randomized eviction to determine and adjust their placement according to each type's expected benefits and memory footprints.
  We implemented the designs in a hybrid DDR--CXL database prototype and evaluated them using YCSB. The single-index design with direct addressing achieves the best performance under most workloads and system configurations. The dual-index design performs better mainly when DDR capacity is limited or accesses are highly concentrated, and has relatively low integration complexity. Adaptive distribution optimization improves DDR memory efficiency and system performance, enabling single-index designs to achieve up to $1.59\times$ the throughput of their counterparts with non-separated management.

</details>


### [232] [Correspondences as Decisions: JevNexus for Decision-Centric Schema Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09487)
*Runze Li,Hanchen Wang,Ying Zhang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: JevNexus通过类型化成对决策结合模式/实例证据，仅在证据分歧且融合间隔小时触发列表精炼，在保持精度的同时将延迟降低约7.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式匹配 increasingly 使用生成式语言模型对检索到的候选列进行重排序，但底层任务实为有界对应决策，无条件的列表精炼可能带来性能退化与高延迟。

Method: JevNexus将类型化成对决策与模式/实例证据结合，并设置门控机制：仅当证据存在分歧且融合间隔小时才调用列表精炼。

Result: 在六个基准族共561个案例上，JevNexus取得数据集宏平均MRR 0.930、Hits@1 0.909，对比Magneto的0.926和0.903；平均延迟从123.452秒降至15.929秒（约7.75倍）。配对分析显示MRR与Hits@1均无统计显著差异。门控仅对5.665%的源列触发列表精炼，避免了无条件精炼导致的退化。

Conclusion: JevNexus表明，通过有选择地触发列表精炼，可在不损失匹配精度的前提下大幅降低延迟，优于无条件精炼策略。

Abstract: Schema matching increasingly uses generative language models to rerank retrieved column candidates, although the underlying task is a bounded correspondence decision. We present JevNexus, which combines typed pairwise decisions with schema/instance evidence and invokes listwise refinement only when the evidence disagrees and the fused margin is small. The evaluation covers 561 cases from six benchmark families. JevNexus obtains dataset-macro MRR and Hits@1 of 0.930 and 0.909, compared with 0.926 and 0.903 for Magneto, while reducing mean latency from 123.452 to 15.929 seconds (7.750). Paired analysis finds no statistically significant difference in either MRR or Hits@1. The gate invokes listwise refinement for only 5.665% of source columns and avoids the degradation caused by unconditional refinement. Code and experimental artifacts are available at https://github.com/RazeenLI/JevNexus.

</details>


### [233] [LDPGraph: Locally Differentially Private Graph Synthesis by Exploiting Neighborhood Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09642)
*Jiawei Dong,Zhikun Zhang,Quan Yuan,Zhe Liu,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出LDPGraph，在本地差分隐私下利用邻域结构（三角计数）合成图数据，兼顾隐私与结构保真度，实验证明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据的广泛使用带来隐私风险，尤其在不可信管理者场景下数据去中心化、用户仅持有邻居连接。采用本地差分隐私（LDP）可缓解风险，但现有方法要么扰动本地邻接表导致注入过多噪声，要么因简单图编码造成严重结构信息损失。

Method: LDPGraph在LDP下挖掘邻域结构：从扰动邻接表聚合含噪全局视图，并结合投影局部连接估计节点级三角计数；针对分别扰动度与三角计数导致的结构不一致，联合优化为可行的度-三角目标；采用triangle-first策略重建全局图，先保留局部聚类结构再满足剩余度要求。

Result: 在四个真实数据集和多个常用图指标上的大量实验验证了LDPGraph的优越性。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: LDPGraph通过利用邻域结构与联合精化，在LDP约束下有效提升合成图的结构保真度，优于现有方法。

Abstract: The widespread application of graph data inevitably brings significant privacy risks, as its unprotected use can lead to the leakage of sensitive information. These risks are particularly acute in the setting with an untrusted curator, where the data remains decentralized and each user only holds the connections to their neighbors. To mitigate such privacy risks, we adopt local differential privacy (LDP) to collect users' private information and generate a synthetic graph. However, existing methods suffer from either excessive noise injection by perturbing the local adjacency lists or significant structural information loss due to the simplistic graph encoding process. To address these issues, we propose LDPGraph, an effective graph synthesis algorithm that takes one step further by exploiting neighborhood structures under LDP. To obtain neighborhood statistics beyond degrees, LDPGraph aggregates a noisy global view from perturbed adjacency lists and combines it with projected local connections to estimate node-level triangle counts. To correct the structural inconsistency caused by separately perturbing degree and triangle count, LDPGraph jointly refines them into feasible degree-triangle targets. To reconstruct a global graph from these estimated targets, LDPGraph adopts a triangle-first strategy that first preserves local clustering structures and then fulfills remaining degree requirements. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets and multiple commonly used graph metrics validate the superiority of LDPGraph. Source code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-TrustAID/LDPGraph.

</details>


### [234] [QCATS: Query Context-Aware Transformer Slicing for Efficient Predictive Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09894)
*Yueying Li,Zhongle Xie,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QCATS是一个查询上下文感知的Transformer切片框架，利用查询谓词和元数据统计在数据库内进行高效的稀疏推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库内推理仅向推理运行时暴露元组级模型输入，使关系谓词和元数据统计对神经执行规划不可见，限制了推理效率。

Method: QCATS在查询粒度上执行，不逐个token或元组路由，而是用查询谓词和元数据统计预先选择上下文对齐的FFN切片。框架包括离线专家构建和轻量级查询级路由，并引入异步CPU-GPU流水线和路由感知批处理等系统优化。

Result: 在四个预测查询工作负载上使用BERT-base和Qwen-0.6B进行实验，QCATS实现最高4.42倍的延迟降低，同时保持与稠密基线相当的预测精度。

Conclusion: QCATS通过查询上下文感知的Transformer切片，在数据库系统内实现高效稀疏推理，显著降低延迟并保持精度。

Abstract: In-database predictive query processing increasingly applies Transformer-based models within relational pipelines. However, existing in-database inference typically exposes only tuple-level model inputs to the inference runtime, leaving relational predicates and metadata statistics invisible to neural execution planning. In this paper, we propose QCATS, a query context-aware transformer slicing framework that enables efficient sparse inference inside database systems. QCATS executes at query granularity: instead of routing individual tokens or tuples during inference, it uses query predicates and metadata statistics to pre-select context-aligned FFN slices before model execution. The framework comprises offline expert construction and lightweight query-level routing that dynamically selects experts during execution. QCATS further introduces system optimizations, including asynchronous CPU-GPU pipelines and routing-aware batching. Experiments on four predictive-query workloads with BERT-base and Qwen-0.6B show that QCATS achieves up to 4.42x latency reduction while preserving prediction accuracy comparable to dense baselines.

</details>


### [235] [ERP Event Logs: A Canonical Form for KPI Time-Series Extraction and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09903)
*Sherri Hadian,Adrian Rebmann,Atacan Korkmaz,Gregor Berg,Ulf Brackmann*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 将ERP多表结构转为规范化事件日志(case, activity, timestamp)，可实现跨客户的KPI统一提取与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP系统数据分散在数百张规范化关系表中，几乎每个KPI都需要单独定制连接查询，导致指标提取碎片化、异构性强，难以跨客户与行业进行比较。

Method: 从相同数据表导出规范化事件日志，一次性映射后将KPI族归约为可复用操作；基于数百家SAP S/4HANA客户的标准化事件日志与共享活动本体，定义三类过程KPI(量、时长、率)，在订单到现金与采购到付款中统一提取；对率做归一化并按最小覆盖率筛选，用规范形式导出的描述符刻画行业与租户异质性，随后分析KPI时间序列。

Result: 同一流程在不同客户与行业间执行高度异构，但共享表示使得销售侧与采购侧信号(交期、订单到达)可直接比较；KPI族可预测性呈稳定排序(量→时长→率)，跨两流程与异构客户群均成立；预训练时序基础模型Chronos在率与时长序列上与ARIMA、ETS等经典基线相当，而经典模型在量的预测上仍占优。

Conclusion: 规范化事件日志为ERP数据提供统一表示，降低KPI提取成本并支持跨客户比较；不同KPI族的可预测性存在稳定层级，模型选择应依KPI族而定。

Abstract: Enterprise resource planning (ERP) systems record operations as transactions spread across hundreds of normalized relational tables. Extracting an operational key performance indicator (KPI) from this schema requires a separate, bespoke join for almost every metric. We show that an event log, a canonical (case, activity, timestamp) representation derived from the same tables, collapses this heterogeneity into one flat structure, from which KPI families reduce to reusable operations after a one-time mapping. Using standardized event logs built from hundreds of SAP S/4HANA customers under a shared activity ontology, we define three families of process-derived KPIs (volumes, durations, and rates) and extract them uniformly across customer systems for two common end-to-end organizational processes, order-to-cash and procure-to-pay. We normalize rates and screen series for minimum coverage to support comparison across customers of very different sizes. We then characterize heterogeneity across industries and tenants using descriptors derived directly from the canonical form, before analyzing the resulting KPI time series. The same process executes heterogeneously across customers and industries, yet this shared representation enables direct comparison of sales- and procurement-side signals, including lead times and order arrivals. Using this shared representation, we characterize which KPI families are forecastable and find a recurring ordering from volumes through durations to rates across both processes and a highly heterogeneous customer base; a pretrained time-series foundation model (Chronos) is competitive with classical baselines such as ARIMA and ETS on rate and duration series, while classical models retain an edge on volumes.

</details>


### [236] [The Case for Synthesized Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09913)
*Ivan Iachnyk,Mihail Stoian,Luisa Neubauer,Ryan Marcus,Andreas Kipf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一个智能体，为每个数据集即时合成定制压缩算法，构建时即可完整验证，实验表明可将压缩率/解压速度帕累托前沿外扩，压缩率比通用方法提升近2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方案多由人工设计，面向多种数据类型的通用算法覆盖面广但在特定数据集上未必最优，形成一个通用算法的"动物园"却难以针对单个数据集达到最佳效果。受近期智能体编码进展启发，作者希望按数据集定制压缩算法。与以往用智能体合成数据库组件不同，针对数据的压缩算法在构建时可被完全验证，从而基本消除正确性顾虑。

Method: 将压缩算法合成问题定义为按数据集定制：由合成智能体为该数据集生成专用压缩算法；由于算法在构建期可被完整验证，能以较低的正确性代价换取针对性优化；借助 AnyBlox、F3 等现代文件格式，把合成出的代码与数据一并存储。

Result: 实验显示合成智能体能扩展压缩率/解压速度的帕累托前沿，在压缩率上比通用方法提升近2倍。

Conclusion: 按数据集合成定制压缩算法是可行且有效的，其构建时可完整验证的特性使其相比其他智能体合成的数据库组件更易落地，配合支持携带代码的现代文件格式，可显著优于通用压缩方法。

Abstract: Compression is a fundamental tool in data management. Today's compression schemes are generally designed by hand to apply to many different types of data (e.g., run length encoding or arithmetic encoding), leading to a zoo of general-purpose algorithms which work well for many datasets, but may not be optimal for any specific dataset. Inspired by recent advancements in agentic coding, we propose synthesizing custom-tailored data compression algorithms on a per-dataset basis. Unlike prior work using agents to synthesize database components, a data-specific compression algorithm can be fully verified at construction time, alleviating most correctness concerns. Modern file formats, like AnyBlox and F3, allow including the synthesized code alongside the data itself. Experimentally, we show that our synthesis agent can expand the compression-factor/decompression-speed Pareto front, outperforming general-purpose methods by nearly $2\times$ in compression factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Mitigating Uncertainty Interactions in GenAI-based Adaptive Systems: Vision, Challenges and Preliminary Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08881)
*Karthik Vaidhyanathan,Norha Villegas,Raffaela Mirandola,Nicolás Cardozo,Ivana Dusparic,Marin Litoiu,Ralf Reussner,Gabriel Tamura*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，提出一个初步概念框架，用于刻画和缓解GenAI软件密集型系统中的不确定性交互，并强调需在整个软件生命周期中考虑缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI组件（含LLM与智能体子系统）为系统各层引入新颖且叠加的不确定性来源。其概率性输出、幻觉、上下文窗口限制和记忆陈旧等特性，要求重新审视既有的不确定性管理与缓解策略，尤其是不确定性发生与表现之间的交互。

Method: 提出一个初步概念框架，为GenAI软件密集型系统中不确定性交互的刻画与缓解建立初步指南；引入"缓解函数"概念，将不确定性交互类别、涉及的不确定性类型、系统设计特征、关键性与缓解性架构战术，映射到受影响的质量属性及相应权衡。以LLM服务、AI驱动软件运维、智能体云管理三个用例作为概念验证。

Result: 框架被应用于三个示例性用例进行概念验证；论证缓解必须贯穿需求分析、设计时决策与运行时自适应全过程，因此无法与执行期的监控和分析解耦。

Conclusion: 缓解GenAI系统的不确定性交互需覆盖完整软件生命周期，且不能脱离执行期监控与分析；所提缓解函数为将不确定性与设计决策、质量属性及权衡建立联系提供了初步结构化方法。

Abstract: Modern software-intensive systems increasingly incorporate GenAI components, including LLM and agentic subsystems, which introduce novel and compounding sources of uncertainty across system layers. The self-adaptive systems community has made significant strides in understanding and managing uncertainty. The intrinsic characteristics of GenAI, including probabilistic outputs, hallucinations, context window limitations, and memory staleness, demand a re-examination of existing frameworks and mitigation strategies for dealing with uncertainty, and especially, the interactions among uncertainty occurrences and manifestations. This position paper posits an initial conceptual framework that establishes preliminary guidelines for characterizing and mitigating uncertainty interactions in GenAI-based software-intensive systems. We argue that mitigation must be considered across the full software lifecycle, encompassing requirements analysis, design-time decisions, and run-time adaptations, which implies the unfeasibility of decoupling it from monitoring and analysis at execution. We introduce the notion of a mitigation function that maps uncertainty interaction categories, involved uncertainty types, system design characteristics, criticality, and mitigating architectural tactics to impacted quality attributes and corresponding tradeoffs. As a proof of concept, we apply the framework to three illustrative use cases: LLM serving, AI-driven software operations, and agentic cloud management.

</details>


### [238] [Psychological Safety in Software Engineering Teams: A Systematic Mapping Study of Team Processes and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08896)
*Adnan Manzoor,Muhammad Azeem Akbar,Jussi Kasurinen,Muhammad Hamza,Aakash Ahmad,Abdul Razzaq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统映射研究分析了2006年至2026年6月的112篇文献，揭示软件开发团队中的心理安全感是一个多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，与团队互动和绩效密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理安全感是软件开发团队中支持协作、知识共享、人际风险承担和学习的重要人文与行为因素，但软件工程领域的相关证据分散于团队过程、绩效结果、情境条件与障碍之中，缺乏系统整合。

Method: 遵循软件工程系统映射研究指南，检索并分析2006年至2026年6月间发表的112篇原始研究，采用描述性统计与主题分析，围绕六个研究问题展开。

Result: 心理安全感表现为多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，特征包括人际风险承担、开放沟通、协作、学习、信任与支持性互动；主要与生产力、软件质量、创新、团队满意度、协作、沟通和决策相关，并受领导力、组织文化、工作环境、信任及社会文化与个体条件影响；常见障碍包括沟通问题、组织限制、领导力问题、多样性与包容性挑战及工作负荷压力；相关实践多样但有效性与长期可持续性证据有限。

Conclusion: 心理安全感是多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，与团队互动和绩效相关。研究强调需要在软件开发环境中开展纵向、情境敏感的心理安全感干预研究。

Abstract: Context: Psychological safety is an important human and behavioral factor in software development teams, supporting collaboration, knowledge sharing, interpersonal risk-taking, and learning. However, evidence in software engineering remains fragmented across team processes, performance outcomes, contextual conditions, barriers. Objective: This study maps literature on psychological safety in software engineering, examining its conceptualization, associations with team performance and processes, contextual influences, barriers, and strengthening strategies. Method: We conducted a systematic mapping study following established software engineering guidelines. We analyzed 112 primary studies published between 2006 and June 2026 using descriptive statistics and thematic analysis across six research questions. Results: Psychological safety emerges as a multidimensional, context-dependent, team-level construct characterized by interpersonal risk-taking, open communication, collaboration, learning, trust, and supportive interaction. It is predominantly associated with productivity, software quality, innovation, team satisfaction, collaboration, communication, and decision-making. These relationships are shaped by leadership, organizational culture, work environment, trust, and sociocultural and individual conditions. Recurring barriers include communication problems, organizational constraints, leadership issues, diversity and inclusion challenges, and workload pressures. Although diverse practices are reported, evidence of their effectiveness and long-term sustainability remains limited. Conclusion: Psychological safety is a multidimensional, context-dependent team-level construct associated with team interaction and performance. This study highlights the need for longitudinal, context-sensitive research on psychological safety interventions in software development environments.

</details>


### [239] [Evaluating Change Point Detection Methods for Software Performance Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09023)
*Diego Elias Costa,Michele Tucci,Luca Traini,Daniele Di Pompeo,Thomas Bach,Francois Farquet,David Daly,Simon Eismann,Petr Tůma,Vittorio Cortellessa,André van Hoorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对12种变化点检测（CPD）方法在真实软件性能测量数据上的有效性进行了全面评估，并提供了人工标注的一致性分析及适用性见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件性能问题会侵蚀用户信任、违反服务等级协议并影响业务效率，因此需要在开发周期中尽早检测性能回归。性能回归分析常依赖性能测量的时间序列来检测显著变化，而CPD方法虽在金融、医疗等领域广泛用于自动识别此类变化，但其在软件性能测量上的有效性尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 首先从三个大型软件系统收集性能测量数据，刻画性能时间序列数据的独特属性；随后进行大规模标注工作，评估人工标注者对潜在性能变化识别的一致性；最后评估12种不同CPD方法检测潜在性能变化的准确性。

Result: 获得了三个大型软件系统的性能测量数据及其时间序列特征，得到人工标注者识别潜在性能变化的一致性评估结果，并给出12种CPD方法的准确率评估及其在软件性能回归分析中的适用性和有效性见解。

Conclusion: 该研究为CPD方法在软件性能回归分析中的适用性与有效性提供了依据和见解，帮助在实际场景中选择合适的CPD方法。

Abstract: Performance issues in software systems are a critical quality issue that can erode user trust, violate service-level agreements, and ultimately affect business efficiency. Consequently, software performance engineering has shifted its focus to developing robust techniques to detect performance regressions as early as possible in the development cycle. Performance regression analysis often relies on time series of performance measurements to detect significant changes in performance behavior. Change Point Detection (CPD) methods have been widely used to automate the identification of such changes in various domains, including finance, healthcare, and performance monitoring. However, the effectiveness of these methods for software performance measurements has not been thoroughly evaluated. In this paper, we present a comprehensive study to evaluate the effectiveness of various CPD methods on real-world software performance measurement datasets. We start by collecting performance measurement data from three large software systems and characterizing the unique properties of performance time series data. Then, we undertake a large-scale effort to annotate and evaluate the consistency of human annotators' identification of potential performance changes. Thereafter, we evaluate the accuracy of twelve distinct CPD methods in detecting potential performance changes, providing insights into their applicability and effectiveness in software performance regression analysis.

</details>


### [240] [Large-scale Repository Engineering via Agent-Native Reusable Code Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09079)
*Haibo Jin,Peng Kuang,Xucheng Yu,Jerry Wang,Dehao Wu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"代码原语"（Code Primitives）这一面向智能体的可复用可执行组件，并构建 CodeFace 库与 LEGO 方法，实现仓库级代码构建；配套 LEGO-REPO 基准显示 LEGO 使 13 个模型平均提升 0.1474。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型已具备开发环境并走向仓库级代码生成，但构建完整仓库依然困难，因为相互作用的模块、接口、配置、测试与依赖必须协同工作，现有方法难以处理跨组件约束与整体一致性。

Method: 提出 Code Primitives：带接口契约、依赖闭包、验证测试和来源信息的智能体原生可复用可执行组件，每个原语用常驻 LLM 评估相关性并适配目标仓库的实现、接口与依赖；在 CodeFace 中组织 1,424 个经验证的原语。提出 LEGO 方法：激活任务相关原语，将其适配实现与任务专用代码集成并解决跨组件约束，再依据已执行测试迭代修订。构建 LEGO-REPO 基准（522 个可执行重建任务，覆盖 7 个软件领域、22 条能力轨道、5 个难度等级，以原生测试套件在空包下界与原始源码上界间评分）。

Result: 13 个评测骨干中最强者交付得分 0.318，41.0% 任务得零分；LEGO 使全部 13 个模型平均提升 0.1474，GPT-5.6-terra 从 0.3180 升至 0.5134（+61.4%）。对照实验中，适配后的原语优于作为上下文检索的代码或原样引入的代码；该效果在独立仓库智能体、三个外部基准及重新挖掘的不相交 CodeFace 上依然成立；使用 GPT-OSS-20B 做适配与诊断，以低 24.0% 成本保留 95.1% 同构得分。

Conclusion: 通过带契约与验证的智能体原生可复用原语及 LEGO 集成与修订流程，可显著提升仓库级代码构建的成功率，且收益对模型、基准与库来源具有稳健性，并具备成本效率优势。

Abstract: Large language models equipped with development environments have moved code generation toward repository-scale construction, yet building complete repositories remains difficult because interacting modules, interfaces, configurations, tests, and dependencies must work together. We introduce Code Primitives, agent-native reusable executable components with interface contracts, dependency closures, validation tests, and provenance. Each primitive uses a resident LLM to assess relevance and adapt its implementation, interfaces, and dependencies to the target repository, and we organize 1,424 validated primitives in CodeFace, a searchable library for repository construction. We introduce LEGO (Large-scale repository Engineering via aGent-native reusable cOde primitives), which activates task-relevant primitives, integrates their adapted implementations with task-specific code while resolving cross-component constraints, and revises the result against executed tests. To measure construction end to end, we build LEGO-REPO, a benchmark of 522 executable reconstruction tasks spanning seven software domains, 22 capability tracks, and five difficulty levels, scored against native test suites between an empty-package floor and original-source ceiling. The strongest of 13 evaluated backbones reaches a delivery score of 0.318 and scores zero on 41.0% of tasks; LEGO improves all 13 by 0.1474 on average and raises GPT-5.6-terra from 0.3180 to 0.5134 (+61.4%). In controlled comparisons, adapted primitives outperform retrieved code supplied as context or vendored unchanged. The effect persists against independent repository agents, across three external benchmarks, and with a disjointly re-mined CodeFace; GPT-OSS-20B for adaptation and diagnosis retains 95.1% of the homogeneous score at 24.0% lower cost.

</details>


### [241] [Who Broke Me? Execution-Guided Repair of Behavioral Dependency Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09267)
*Juntao Chen,Tingting Bi,Yanlin Wang,Patanamon Thongtanunam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出BBCFixer方法，通过定位根API并过滤库diff，帮助LLM代理高效修复依赖升级引发的行为性破坏变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖升级即使不改变库接口，也可能破坏下游项目（行为性破坏变更）。这类问题难以修复：失败测试未必指向根API，编译器不报错，库文档也常常缺失。现有多数LLM修复方法缺乏上游证据，且失败修复往往无法识别根API。

Method: BBCFixer在旧、新库版本下分别运行失败测试，对比调用返回值差异并进行排序以识别候选根API，再用该候选过滤库diff以选取相关上游证据，供LLM代理修复。同时构建BBCBench基准，包含Python与JavaScript共100个行为性依赖破坏案例。

Result: 在BBCBench上对比Pure Agent（无升级证据）与BDUpdater（挖掘库文档）：BBCFixer将平均通过率最多提升17%，且每次成功修复所需代理步数最多减少27%。

Conclusion: 通过筛选行为性破坏背后的上游证据并定位根API，BBCFixer使LLM代理能够更有效、更高效地修复行为性破坏变更。

Abstract: Dependency upgrades can break downstream projects without changing the library interface. Such behavioral breaking changes are difficult for developers to fix, because the failing test does not always point to the root API, the upstream API that causes the break. Existing LLM-based repair methods obtain evidence for the repair from compiler feedback or library documentation. However, a behavioral break produces no compiler feedback and is often undocumented. Agents that receive no upgrade evidence also usually do not retrieve upstream evidence themselves, and most of their failed repairs do not identify the root API.
  The library diff provides useful upstream evidence, but the root API must first be identified to select the relevant part of the diff. We present BBCFixer, a repair method that runs the failing test under the old and new library versions, ranks the calls whose return value differs to identify a candidate root API, and filters the library diff with that candidate. To evaluate repairs of behavioral breaks, we further introduce BBCBench, a benchmark of 100 behavioral dependency breaks in Python and JavaScript.
  We compare BBCFixer on BBCBench with two baselines: (1) Pure Agent, an agent that receives no upgrade evidence, and (2) BDUpdater, a method that mines library documentation. BBCFixer raises the average pass rate by up to 17% relative to Pure Agent and needs up to 27% fewer agent steps per successful repair. BBCFixer thus provides a way to select the upstream evidence behind a behavioral break, so that an LLM agent can repair behavioral breaking changes more effectively and efficiently.

</details>


### [242] [Beyond FAIR: A Fitness Function Framework for Sustainable Research Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09580)
*Irdin Pekaric,Philipp Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在既有可持续研究软件适应度函数框架基础上，超越FAIR原则，新增环境函数（资源效率与执行足迹）与安全函数（依赖健康、漏洞暴露、安全配置），将研究软件可持续性扩展为包含FAIR、环境责任与长期安全的多维属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件可持续性常以FAIR原则评估，但FAIR并不涵盖所有相关关切：软件还需环境负责与长期安全。过度资源消耗增加环境成本，不安全软件带来维护负担、技术债务与复用障碍。因此需要将评估从FAIR合规扩展到更完整的可持续性视角。

Method: 在先前提出的可持续研究软件适应度函数框架上作扩展，引入两组新的适应度函数：一是面向资源效率与执行足迹的环境函数；二是面向依赖健康、漏洞暴露与安全配置的安全函数。以此将连续软件评估从FAIR合规扩展为更全面的可持续性度量。

Result: 构建出扩展后的适应度函数框架，涵盖FAIR、环境与安全三方面的函数集合，可支持面向多维可持续性的连续软件评估。

Conclusion: 研究软件可持续性应被视为多维属性，不仅包含FAIR，还包含环境责任与长期安全；所提框架为更完整的持续评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Research software sustainability is often assessed through the FAIR principles: findability, accessibility, interoperability and reusability. While important, FAIR does not cover all relevant sustainability concerns. Research software should also be environmentally responsible and secure over time. Excessive resource consumption increases environmental cost, while insecure software creates maintenance overhead, technical debt and barriers to reuse. To this end, in this paper, we extend a previously proposed fitness function framework for sustainable research software beyond FAIR. We introduce two additional sets of fitness functions: environmental functions targeting resource efficiency and execution footprint, and security functions targeting dependency health, vulnerability exposure and secure configuration. Together, these functions broaden continuous software assessment from FAIR compliance to a more complete view of sustainability. The resulting framework treats research software sustainability as a multidimensional property that includes FAIR, environmental responsibility and long-term security.

</details>


### [243] [GRAML: Graph-Grounded Reasoning and Multi-Task Learning for LLM-Based Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09605)
*Ruohan Li,Miaoqing Tian,Haipeng Qu,Ruobing Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GRAML框架结合图证据、漏洞描述生成与多任务训练，提升LLM漏洞检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件漏洞检测中常因控制流与数据流信息利用不足而性能受限。

Method: 对C/C++程序静态分析，提取关键源码行与类型化行关系作为结构证据；用该证据引导Tree-of-Thought漏洞推理（ToT-VR）生成漏洞描述；将描述与检测、定位、评估样本组合，构建四任务统一训练数据集。

Result: 在分布内测试集和六个分布外数据集上，平均F1为66.67%–68.70%，最高超越SOTA基线30.92%；消融显示ToT-VR与图引导描述优于标准CoT和原始CPG序列化。

Conclusion: 为构建更可靠、更安全的LLM软件工程系统提供实践指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to software vulnerability detection. However, their performance is often limited by insufficient use of control-flow and data-flow information. In this paper, we propose GRAML, a framework that combines graph evidence, vulnerability description generation, and multi-task training. GRAML first performs static analysis on C/C++ programs to extract critical source lines and typed line relations as structural evidence. It then uses this evidence to guide GPT-5 through the Tree-of-Thought-guided Vulnerability Reasoning (ToT-VR) process and generate vulnerability descriptions. These descriptions are further combined with Detection, Localization, and Assessment samples to build a unified four-task training dataset. We evaluate GRAML on an in-distribution (ID) test set and six out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets. The results show that GRAML achieves average F1 scores ranging from 66.67% to 68.70%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30.92%. Ablation experiments further show that ToT-VR and graph-guided vulnerability descriptions improve detection performance compared with standard Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and raw Code Property Graph (CPG) serializations. These findings provide practical guidance for building more reliable and secure software engineering systems with large language models.

</details>


### [244] [A Chat Assistant for Software Exploration in a 3D Software Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09901)
*Malte Hansen,Karim Issa,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个嵌入3D软件可视化工具ExplorViz的聊天助手，基于大语言模型（LLM）实现交互动画探索，用户可通过自然语言查询当前可视化的软件系统并触发改变可视化的操作。助手通过CopilotKit集成，结合概率性LLM与确定性工具动作（类似MCP）。实验邀请11名参与者评估，结果显示助手生成的摘要和解释被评价为基本正确，高亮实体和创建颜色主题等功能可用性高，但开放式的软件重构效果参差不齐，表明需要更强的LLM护栏和更好的用户反馈整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件探索和程序理解任务中，用户需要频繁进行交互操作，交互开销较大。作者希望借助大语言模型降低这种交互开销，通过自然语言对话来辅助用户理解软件系统并控制可视化，从而提升探索性程序理解任务的效率。

Method: 作者在3D软件可视化工具ExplorViz中集成了一个聊天助手，基于当前大语言模型（LLM）构建。助手集成使用CopilotKit库，将概率性LLM与确定性、基于工具的动作相结合（类似Model Context Protocol, MCP的实现方式）。助手能够回答关于当前可视化软件系统的问题，触发改变可视化的动作，并支持通过添加、删除或修改软件系统部分来重构可视化。随后进行了包含11名参与者的实证实验，评估感知理解支持和聊天助手触发的工具调用。

Result: 实验结果显示，参与者认为助手生成的摘要和解释基本正确，并对高亮可视化中的实体、创建新颜色主题等操作报告了较高的可用性。相比之下，开放式的聊天辅助软件重构在可视化中表现出参差不齐的结果。整体上，助手被认为可用且有前景，能够降低探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销。

Conclusion: 该聊天助手被普遍认为可用且具有潜力，可减少探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销。但对于开放式软件重构，结果参差不齐，表明需要为所用LLM引入更强的护栏机制，并更好地整合用户反馈。作者提供了展示助手使用的视频以及评估所用软件系统的复现包。

Abstract: We present a chat assistant for interactive software exploration, embedded in the 3D software visualization tool ExplorViz. The assistant builds upon current Large Language Models (LLMs) and enables users to ask questions about the currently visualized software system and trigger actions that change the visualization through natural language. We integrate the chat assistant in our ExplorViz frontend using the CopilotKit libraries such that probabilistic LLMs are combined with deterministic and tool-based actions similar to implementations using the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The chat assistant is also enabled to restructure the software system by adding, removing, or modifying parts of the software system in the visualization. An empirical experiment with eleven participants evaluated both perceived comprehension support and the tool calls that were triggered by the chat assistant. Participants rated the assistant's generated summaries and explanations as largely correct. They also reported high usability for actions like highlighting entities in the visualization and the creation of new color themes. In contrast, open-ended chat-assisted software restructuring in the visualization showed mixed results. This suggests a need for stronger guardrails for the employed LLM and better incorporation of user feedback. Overall, the assistant was perceived as usable and promising for reducing interaction overhead during exploratory program comprehension tasks. We provide a video presenting the chat assistant's use and a reproduction package of the software system that was used in our evaluation.

</details>


### [245] [Analysis and Visualization of the Linux Kernel's Software Evolution Using the City Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09910)
*Malte Hansen,Lukas Damerau,Moritz Bugla,David Moreno-Lumbreras,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用软件可视化工具ExplorViz，以3D城市隐喻对Linux内核的代码结构及其演化进行可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核是现存规模最大、维护时间最长的开源项目之一，代码超过4000万行，理解其内部结构并评估软件演化面临巨大挑战。

Method: 通过自定义分析服务解析Linux Git仓库中的提交；采用基于Web的前端，以3D城市隐喻展示软件结构；为每个文件收集代码行数等度量指标，并向上累积到目录和提交层级；将计算的度量映射到建筑物尺寸或通过热力图显示，同时提供可视化、搜索与过滤选项以支持交互式探索。

Result: 展示了Linux内核仓库中全部文件的可视化结果，并对内核中Rust文件的代码演化进行了可视化分析。

Conclusion: 该可视化方法能够有效支持对超大规模软件系统Linux内核的结构理解与演化分析，具备交互式探索能力。

Abstract: The Linux kernel is one of the largest and longest-maintained open source projects in existence. With more than 40 million lines of code, understanding the kernel's internal structure and assessing its software evolution is a great challenge. In this paper, we present an approach to visualize the Linux kernel using our software visualization tool ExplorViz. We analyze commits from the Linux Git repository using a custom analysis service. The web-based frontend utilizes the 3D city metaphor for visualization of the software structure. Metrics such as the number of lines are collected for each file and are accumulated for directories and commits. Calculated metrics can be mapped to the building's dimensions or be displayed via a heat map. A wide range of visualization, search, and filter options enable the interactive exploration of the visualized data. We present both a visualization of all files in the kernel repository and a visual analysis of the code evolution of Rust files in the Linux kernel. Demo video: https://youtu.be/3ODnERqD8g0

</details>


### [246] [AgentTracer: Tracing Indirect Prompt Injection Attack through Fine-Grained Intention-Execution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09935)
*Zitong Yao,Jiangrong Wu,Yixi Lin,Anrui Huang,Luoyun Zhang,Yuhong Nan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentTracer，一个意图感知的追踪框架，将间接提示注入视为任务意图漂移，通过重建工具调用间的隐式决策依赖来定位注入源与注入点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体通过外部资源交互完成复杂任务时易受间接提示注入（IPI）影响。现实环境难以完全防御 IPI，事后追踪对定位注入源、重建攻击链至关重要。但现有追踪方法主要捕捉显式的控制流与数据流依赖，忽略了恶意指令驱动的工具调用之间的隐式关系，这些调用可能缺乏显式依赖并与正常操作交织，导致攻击链难以完整重建。

Method: AgentTracer 将 IPI 建模为任务意图漂移，恢复工具调用间的隐式决策依赖，构建"意图驱动执行图"，以任务意图连接分散的工具调用。它结合用户请求与操作知识库构建用户意图授权空间，识别意图漂移的工具调用；从审计中的异常工具调用出发，执行基于目标的剪枝与后向追踪，重建攻击链并定位注入源和注入点。

Result: 为在正常任务噪声下评估，作者将 AgentDyn 和 InjecAgent 中成功 IPI 攻击的执行日志，与包含 1,800 个用户请求、9,000 个无 IPI 背景工具调用的正常日志结合。端到端实验中，AgentTracer 达到 94.17% 的注入点准确率和 93.56% 的路径精度；对比实验显示其注入点准确率较现有方法提升 18%–54%。

Conclusion: AgentTracer 通过意图感知的隐式依赖重建，有效解决了现有方法难以完整重建 IPI 攻击链的问题，在噪声环境下仍保持高追踪精度，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with external resources to complete complex user tasks, exposing them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), where malicious instructions redirect agents toward attacker-intended tasks. Since IPI is difficult to defend against in real-world environments, post-incident tracing is essential for locating the injection source and reconstructing the attack chain. However, existing tracing methods primarily capture explicit control-flow and data-flow dependencies, overlooking the implicit relationships among tool calls driven by the malicious instruction. These tool calls may lack explicit dependencies and be interleaved with legitimate operations, making complete attack-chain reconstruction difficult. In this paper, we present AgentTracer, an intent-aware tracing framework that treats IPI as task intent drift. AgentTracer recovers implicit decision dependencies among tool calls to construct an Intent-Driven Execution Graph that connects dispersed tool calls by task intent. It combines the user request with an operation knowledge base to construct a user intent authorization space and identify intent-drift tool calls. Starting from an anomalous tool call under audit, AgentTracer performs target-based pruning and backward tracing to reconstruct the attack chain and locate the injection source and injection point. To evaluate AgentTracer in the presence of noise from normal tasks, we combine execution logs constructed from successful IPI attacks in AgentDyn and InjecAgent with normal execution logs containing 1,800 user requests and 9,000 background tool calls without IPI. In end-to-end experiments, AgentTracer achieves 94.17 percent injection-point accuracy and 93.56 percent path precision. Comparative experiments show that AgentTracer improves injection-point accuracy over existing methods by 18 to 54 percent.

</details>


### [247] [Designing Collaborative AI-Driven Workflows for Scientific Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09995)
*Akash Dhruv,Max Knobbe,Pedro Machado,Anshu Dubey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文主张将代理式AI系统用于协作团队结构而非全自动化，提出由领域专家写规格、代理在确定性编排下生成代码、每阶段数值对比并需人工审核的科研计算工作流，并以Fortran转C++的高能物理应用进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学计算中，代理生成的代码不仅需要正确，还要能被不同专业背景的团队成员理解，验证难度大，因此不宜完全自动化。

Method: 提出协作式工作流：领域专家负责规格说明与规划，代理在确定性编排模式下编写目标代码；每个阶段与参考代码进行数值对比，并需人工审核批准后才进入下一阶段。在大型高能物理Fortran到C++翻译任务上，比较不同编排器、设计模式和模型。

Result: 在14个实验中，采用带强制限制的简单“作者—审阅者”循环，完成文件数量与多代理工作流相当，但每文件成本约为后者的三分之一。

Conclusion: 科研计算中代理式AI应嵌入人机协作与人工审核流程；简单的作者—审阅者循环在成本效率上优于复杂的多代理工作流。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence systems can carry out a broad range of tasks in software engineering and scientific research, from writing and translating code to running workflows for data analysis and visualization. In scientific computing, the difficulty is verifying that agent-generated code is both correct and understandable to teams whose members bring different areas of expertise. We therefore argue that these systems are best used within collaborative team structures rather than as full automation. In the workflows we propose, domain experts write the specification and plan, and agents operate under a deterministic orchestration pattern to write the target code. Each stage ends with a numerical comparison against the reference code and requires human review and approval before the next begins. We evaluate these workflows on the translation of a large high-energy physics application from Fortran to C++, running the same task under different orchestrators, design patterns, and models. Across fourteen experiments, a simple author--reviewer loop with enforced limits completed a comparable number of files to a multi-agent workflow at about one-third of the cost per file.

</details>


### [248] [AdaT$^2$: Adaptive Test Transformations for Black-Box Boundary Testing of Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10141)
*Liting Lin,Boxi Yu,Qinghua Xu,Yuzhong Zhang,Lionel Briand,Emir Muñoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdaT$^2$通过从探索性对话中抽取策略条件语句，并用语句引导边界测试生成，结合自适应配对选择，在四个领域生成更多有效边界测试并提升策略缺陷检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的对话智能体必须遵守策略，而策略中的每个条件都在用户请求上划出边界，智能体需在边界两侧表现出不同行为，因此需要系统化地生成边界测试以验证其合规性。

Method: AdaT$^2$让LLM扮演用户进行探索性对话，从智能体回复中抽取描述单个条件及预期行为的语句；除单语句引导的普通测试外，还生成由语句与测试变换指令（如“省略一个必需输入”）配对的变换测试。由于语句与指令组成的配对数量远超单次运行可尝试的范围且许多不适用，采用自适应配对选择：语句按新颖度选取，指令用Bayes-UCB赌博机算法选择，并根据早期配对是否产生测试及智能体是否通过LLM评判来学习。

Result: 在$\tau^3$-bench的四个领域上，AdaT$^2$有62.7%到83.3%的测试为有效边界测试且预期行为被显式定义，各领域均高于AgentEval的47.7%到68.1%；变换测试额外补充了13到46个普通测试遗漏的显式定义边界。作为回归测试，AdaT$^2$的测试套件在航空领域检出全部8个植入策略缺陷，在零售领域检出8个中的4个，而测试数不到其三分之一的AgentEval分别检出5个和2个。

Conclusion: AdaT$^2$能更有效地生成有效边界测试并提升策略缺陷检出能力，优于AgentEval基线，同时其基准区分了显式定义边界与由领域知识推断的边界。

Abstract: Conversational agents based on large language models (LLMs) must comply with policies. Each condition in a policy draws a boundary between user requests, and the agent must behave differently on its two sides. We present AdaT$^2$, which extracts statements from the agent's replies in exploratory conversations with an LLM acting as the user, and uses the statements to guide boundary test generation. Each statement describes one condition and the behavior expected when the condition holds. Besides plain tests guided by single statements, AdaT$^2$ writes transformed tests guided by pairs of a statement and a test transformation instruction, such as "omit one required input". The instruction of a pair can move a test to the other side of the statement's boundary or to another boundary. The statements and instructions form far more pairs than a run can try, and many pairs are not applicable. Adaptive pair selection therefore chooses the statement of each pair by novelty and the instruction with the bandit algorithm Bayes-UCB, which learns from whether earlier pairs yielded a test and whether the agent passed it according to an LLM judge. Our benchmark counts the two sides of each boundary separately and distinguishes boundaries explicitly defined by the agent's prompt, tool code, or knowledge base from boundaries that the agent's LLM infers from domain knowledge. On four domains of $τ^3$-bench, 62.7% to 83.3% of AdaT$^2$'s tests are valid boundary tests whose expected behavior is explicitly defined, higher in every domain than AgentEval's (47.7% to 68.1%). Transformed tests add 13 to 46 explicitly defined boundaries that plain tests miss. As regression tests, AdaT$^2$'s test suites detect all eight seeded policy faults in the airline domain and four of eight in the retail domain, and AgentEval's test suites, with fewer than a third as many tests, detect five and two, respectively.

</details>


### [249] [Why Software Engineering Is Indispensable in the Age of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10226)
*Alfonso Fuggetta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文论证了具备能力的AI编码代理的兴起使软件工程（SE）与软件工程师变得不可或缺而非多余：AI辅助开发若缺乏SE基础，会产生看似合理但无法验证、最终不可信的软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图回应"AI是否会淘汰软件工程学科及软件工程师职业"这一问题，指出AI编码代理虽能力增强，但存在结构性缺陷，因此需要重新审视SE与软件工程师的核心价值。

Method: 论文通过分析大语言模型的三个结构性属性——概率性生成、不可知论（agnosticism）与语义无状态性（semantic statelessness），论证其形成"结构性真空"，且该真空无法通过增加训练消除。

Result: 填补该真空需要四类知识杠杆：方法论知识、领域知识、设计选择与流程选择，且这四者都必须被具体化为持久性制品（persistent artifacts）。

Conclusion: 每类知识杠杆都需要软件工程师作为方法论者、中介者与守护者（custodian），因此SE与软件工程师不仅不会过时，反而更加关键。

Abstract: Can AI make Software Engineering (SE) -- the discipline -- obsolete? And can it make software engineers -- the professionals -- redundant? This paper argues that the rise of capable AI coding agents makes SE and software engineers essential, not obsolete: the missing foundation without which AI-assisted development produces misleadingly plausible, unverifiable, and ultimately untrustworthy software. Three structural properties of large language models (probabilistic generation, agnosticism, and semantic statelessness) create a structural vacuum that no amount of training can eliminate. Filling it requires four knowledge levers: methodological knowledge, domain knowledge, design choices, and process choices. All four must be reified as persistent artifacts, and each requires the software engineer as methodologist, mediator, and custodian.

</details>


### [250] [TestGRAD: Evolving Test Suites via Failure Pattern Momentum for SWE-Agent Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242)
*Pengfei He,Jiayuan Zhou,Shaowei Wang,Ruiqi Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TestGRAD框架，将集成补丁的测试选择形式化为"测试空间优化"，通过差分损失、全CRUD梯度与失败模式动量实现自动测试优化，在SWE-bench Verified上以4智能体集成达到84.2% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-agent集成通过组合不同智能体的候选补丁来提升问题修复率，核心难题是基于测试的选择。现有测试生成方法作为优化器存在局限：缺乏面向集成选择的显式损失、更新方向不完整（主要只新增或删除测试）、且为一次性生成，无法从反复失败中获取反馈。

Method: 受带动量的梯度下降启发，提出TestGRAD框架，包含三个核心概念：(1) 差分损失——以执行定义的目标，使测试能依据行为区分候选补丁；(2) 全CRUD梯度——将更新方向从单纯增删测试扩展为读取现有测试基础设施、创建新测试、更新过时断言、仅删除废弃测试；(3) 失败模式动量——从记忆中挖掘频繁失败序列，避免重复无效方向并压缩失败历史上下文。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，TestGRAD使用4智能体集成取得84.2% Pass@1，较最强基线（80.6%）绝对提升3.6个百分点，同时将失败历史上下文压缩超过100倍。

Conclusion: 将测试选择表述为测试空间优化并引入差分损失、全CRUD梯度与失败模式动量，可有效提升集成补丁选择性能，并在保证效果的同时大幅压缩上下文开销。

Abstract: SWE-agent ensembles improve issue resolution by combining candidate patches from different agents with complementary strengths. The central problem is therefore test-based selection: generate tests, execute candidate patches, and identify the best patch. We formulate this process as test-space optimization: evolving an executable repository test suite until it distinguishes competing patches. Existing test-generation methods are limited optimizers. They usually lack an explicit loss for ensemble selection, optimize through incomplete directions that mostly create new tests or delete old ones, and perform one-off generation without feedback from repeated failures. Inspired by gradient descent with momentum, we introduce TestGRAD, a framework for automatic test optimization. TestGRAD centers on three concepts. Differential loss gives the optimizer an explicit execution-defined target: useful tests should separate candidate patches by behavior. Full CRUD gradients expand the update direction from merely creating or deleting tests to reading existing test infrastructure, creating new tests, updating stale assertions, and deleting only obsolete tests. Failure Pattern Momentum mines frequent failure sequences from memory, allowing the optimizer to avoid repeated non-discriminative directions while compressing the failure-history context. On SWE-bench Verified, TestGRAD achieves 84.2% Pass@1 with a 4-agent ensemble, outperforming the strongest baseline (80.6%) by an absolute improvement of 3.6 percentage points, while compressing failure-history context by over $100\times$.

</details>


### [251] [QuSema: Detecting Silent Bugs in Quantum Libraries via Quantum-knowledge-enhanced Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10258)
*Yujin Song,Kaining Zhang,Qixin Zhang,Shuai Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuSema，一种用于发现量子库中“静默缺陷”的自主测试智能体，利用量子语义与文档构建源码级语义预言机，通过智能体循环生成可执行测试，在 Qiskit 和 PennyLane 上发现了 40 个新缺陷（含 30 个静默缺陷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子库已成为量子算法开发的关键基础设施，但其正确性难以测试。现有测试技术主要依赖基于失败、基于比较的预言机，只有当执行失败、违反运行时检查或与另一实现不一致时才能暴露缺陷。当缺乏合适的执行型预言机时，这些方法适用性受限，导致部分静默缺陷未被发现；而这些被遗漏的缺陷会产生错误结果，传播到实验结论、仿真研究和算法设计中。

Method: QuSema 将量子语义与文档中的约束作为源码级语义预言机，用于评估实现逻辑是否会在有效输入下产生无效输出。它通过智能体循环反复检查库 API 文档与源代码，推理量子操作的预期行为，识别潜在的语义偏差，并通过库 API 生成可执行测试进行验证。在量子领域推理的引导下，QuSema 将高层行为不匹配转化为具体的、用户可触发的缺陷报告，从而发现非崩溃类缺陷。作者针对 Qiskit 和 PennyLane 实现了 QuSema。

Result: 在 20 个历史静默缺陷的基准上，QuSema 的平均缺陷重定位数量高于 Claude Code 和 Codex，其中 DeepSeek 配置的成本低于 Claude Code。QuSema 还发现了 40 个此前未知、经开发者确认的缺陷，其中包含 30 个静默缺陷。

Conclusion: QuSema 通过语义预言机与智能体循环相结合，有效弥补了传统预言机在缺乏执行型判据时难以检测静默缺陷的不足，在量子库测试中展现出优于现有智能体工具的效果，并具备发现真实新缺陷的实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum libraries are now critical infrastructure for quantum algorithm development, yet their correctness remains difficult to test. Existing testing techniques mainly rely on failure-based or comparison-based oracles, exposing bugs only when executions fail, violate runtime checks, or disagree with another implementation. Their applicability is limited when suitable execution-based oracles are unavailable, leaving some silent bugs undetected. Such missed bugs can produce incorrect results that propagate into experimental conclusions, simulation studies, and algorithmic designs. Here we present QuSema, an autonomous testing agent for finding silent bugs in quantum libraries. QuSema uses constraints from quantum semantics and documentation as a source-level semantic oracle to assess whether implementation logic can produce invalid outputs from valid inputs. It operates through an agentic loop that repeatedly inspects library API documentation and source code, reasons about the intended behavior of quantum operations, identifies potential semantic deviations, and validates them by generating executable tests through library APIs. Guided by quantum-domain reasoning, QuSema turns high-level behavioral mismatches into concrete, user-triggerable bug reports, enabling it to uncover non-crash defects. We implement QuSema for Qiskit and PennyLane. On a benchmark of 20 historical silent bugs, QuSema achieves higher mean bug relocation counts than Claude Code and Codex, with the DeepSeek configuration costing less than Claude Code. QuSema also discovers 40 previously unknown bugs confirmed by the developers, including 30 silent bugs.

</details>


### [252] [Using Small Language Models to Reverse-Engineer Machine Learning Pipelines Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10261)
*Nicolas Lacroix,Frederic Precioso,Mireille Blay-Fornarino,Sebastien Mosser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估小型语言模型（SLMs）能否利用其代码理解与分类能力，从源代码中提取ML流水线阶段，以克服现有方法依赖人工标注或分类器不支持领域多样性的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在定义ML流水线阶段分类法后，从源代码中提取这些阶段对理解ML实践至关重要。但由于ML（算法、数据集）持续演化带来的多样性，该任务具有挑战性。现有方法要么依赖不可扩展的人工标注，要么使用无法妥善支持领域多样性的分类器，因此需要更可靠的解决方案。

Method: 基于两项代表当前局限的参考工作进行验证性研究。先用Cochran's Q检验比较多个SLM，再通过两次McNemar检验将表现最佳的模型与参考研究对比。另用Cochran's Q检验考察分类法定义的差异如何影响SLM表现。最后用拟合优度检验比较SLM分类与先前研究得到的ML实践洞见。

Result: 第一，分类法的措辞显著影响分类表现。第二，表现最佳的SLM取得了不错的结果，但并未超越其他分类器。第三，在探索数据科学家的实践时，三种分类方法导致的洞见显著不同，且效应量各异。

Conclusion: 现有分类方法的局限性会偏倚我们对ML实践的理解。当前SLM无需事先微调即可取得有前景的结果，但仍存在常见局限，加之推理成本高，使其在大规模研究中的适用性受到挑战。

Abstract: Context: Once defined a taxonomy of stages structuring Machine Learning (ML) pipelines (e.g. Data Preprocessing, Modeling...), extracting these stages from source code is key for better understanding ML practices. However, the diversity caused by the constant evolution of ML (e.g., algorithms, datasets) makes this task challenging. Existing approaches either rely on non-scalable manual labeling or on classifiers that do not properly support domain's diversity. These limitations call for more reliable solutions.
  Objective: We evaluate whether Small Language Models (SLMs) can leverage their code understanding and classification abilities to address these limitations, and enhance our understanding of practices in ML.
  Method: We conduct a confirmatory study based on two relevant reference works representing current limitations in the state-of-the-art. We first compare several SLMs using Cochran's Q test, then evaluate the best-performing model against reference studies via two McNemar's tests. An additional Cochran's Q test examines how taxonomy definition variations affect the SLM performance. Finally, goodness-of-fit tests compare ML practice insights from SLM classification with those from prior studies.
  Results: First, we found that the taxonomy wording significantly impacts classification performance. Second, the best performing SLM yielded good results, yet, without outperforming other classifiers. Third, the three classification methods led to significantly different insights, with varying effect sizes, when exploring practices of data scientists.
  Conclusions: Limitations of existing classification methods bias our understanding of ML practices. While current SLMs show promising results without prior fine-tuning, they still exhibit common limitations, in addition to inference high costs challenging their applicability in large-scale studies.

</details>


### [253] [When Sub-Agents Work in Parallel: The Promises and Pitfalls of Dynamic Concurrency in Long-Horizon Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10263)
*Han Li,HanHaoNing Li,Ziqian Jiang,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究前沿编码智能体（Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code）的"动态并发"执行策略，通过对354个任务、2124次执行进行启用/禁用并发的受控对比，分析其端到端效果、调度行为、13种并发特有失败模式和28种可观察模式，以及该策略在何种条件下具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从有界软件工程任务迈向长时程开发，动态并发成为扩展复杂开发任务的有前景手段。然而现有研究多聚焦于短任务中的智能体失败或预定义多智能体工作流中的协作，对于前沿智能体在不同任务复杂度下的动态并发行为缺乏认识。长时程开发使编排成为任务完成的核心，因此需要针对动态并发执行策略开展系统研究。

Method: 将动态并发视为一种执行策略，对匹配的Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code执行进行启用与禁用的受控对比。覆盖354个任务、2124次执行，横跨多种任务复杂度与执行时程，评估端到端效果与调度行为，并对匹配轨迹进行分析。

Result: 刻画了13种并发特有的失败模式、28种可观察模式，并识别出动态并发策略产生优势的条件。短期任务上模型能力主导结果，而长时程开发中编排成为任务完成的关键。

Conclusion: 动态并发是一种有望扩展长时程复杂开发任务的执行策略，但其效果取决于任务复杂度与执行时程；研究揭示了该策略的失败模式、可观察规律及优势条件，为前沿编码智能体的动态并发设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: As coding agents advance from bounded software engineering tasks toward long horizon development, dynamic concurrency offers a promising way to scale complex development tasks. Under this policy, agents decide during execution whether and how to spawn concurrent sub-agents. Model capability largely determines outcomes on shorter tasks, whereas long horizon development makes orchestration central to task completion. Existing work, focused on coding agent failures on shorter tasks or collaboration in predefined multiagent workflows, offers little insight into dynamic concurrency in frontier agents across task complexity. We study dynamic concurrency as an execution policy through controlled comparisons of matched Codex, Claude Code, and Kimi Code executions with the policy enabled or disabled. Across 354 tasks and 2,124 executions spanning a range of task complexities and execution horizons, we evaluate its end to end effects and scheduling behavior, and analyze matched trajectories to characterize 13 concurrency specific failure modes, 28 observable patterns, and the conditions under which it provides an advantage.

</details>


### [254] [TaoD2C-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Industrial UI Code Generation Beyond Visual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10374)
*Chengwei Shi,Yunnong Chen,Tingting Zhou,Qiang Lu,Shiyu Yue,Xinyuan Hu,Jianfang Ru,Liuqing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TaoD2C-Bench，一个面向工业级设计到代码（D2C）任务的基准，用于评估多模态大模型在约束感知跨模态推理与 UI 代码生成方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的关键挑战在于从视觉识别走向约束感知的跨模态推理，即结合视觉线索与其他模态信息，在特定领域规则下理解元素关系。工业 D2C 场景（将 UI 设计转为代码）典型体现这一挑战：模型需连接设计图与杂乱图层元数据，推断组件与布局实现需求，并在目标库约束下用代码实现。然而这些能力在真实工业环境中尚未被充分评估。

Method: 构建 TaoD2C 数据集，包含来自 17 个商业平台的 2,861 个生产设计，以及 97,652 条专家标注，覆盖 Component、Group、Alignment、Position 四类，并区分必需约束与允许的实现选择。基于此定义三项任务：端到端 UI 代码生成、需求推断、需求实现。

Result: 对 8 个多模态大模型的评估显示，在生成满足实现需求的 UI 代码方面存在显著差距，且在需求推断与需求实现上呈现不同性能画像；同时发现模型的视觉重建能力并不必然意味着其具备生成满足需求的代码的能力。

Conclusion: TaoD2C-Bench 揭示了工业 UI 代码生成中约束感知推理的不足，并公开 TaoD2C 以支持相关研究。

Abstract: A key challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is moving beyond visual recognition to constraint-aware cross-modal reasoning. This involves combining visual cues with information from other modalities to understand elements' relationships under domain-specific rules. This challenge is acutely evident in industrial design-to-code (D2C), which converts user interface (UI) designs into code and requires MLLMs to connect design images with disorganized layer metadata, infer component and layout implementation requirements, and realize them in code under target-library constraints. However, these capabilities remain insufficiently evaluated in realistic industrial settings. To fill this gap, we present TaoD2C-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs' ability to generate UI code that satisfies implementation requirements in industrial applications. The TaoD2C dataset consists of 2,861 production designs from 17 commercial platforms with 97,652 expert annotations across four categories: Component, Group, Alignment, and Position. These annotations distinguish required constraints from permitted implementation choices. TaoD2C-Bench defines three tasks: end-to-end UI code generation, requirement inference, and requirement realization. Evaluating eight MLLMs reveals substantial gaps in generating UI code that satisfies implementation requirements, alongside distinct performance profiles in inference and realization. We further show that MLLMs' visual reconstruction ability does not necessarily imply an ability to generate code that meets these requirements. We release TaoD2C to support research on industrial UI code generation.

</details>
